#### Updated existing requirements

- TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013 (TR:1639) "UserPromptSubmit hook opens exactly one turn per prompt". Changes: the store text's dedupe ("a repeated identical prompt reuses the already-open turn", and inline AC1 "the second delivery of the same prompt returns turn-already-open and does not call beginTurn"; implemented today as the prompt-text window at `plugins/core/lib-ps/plugin-hook.ps1:796-835` at `a8d3934b`) is removed, and inline AC2 (a differing prompt opens a new turn) stays, restated by FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC004; each message event opens its turn through the server ingress mint with a nonce derived from that event (D1), so every delivery of one event gets one turn and identical prompts sent separately are separate turns, and a new prompt opens a new turn without canceling the open one. New ACs: TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC001 a retried or replayed mint for one delivery opens one turn [A3]; -AC002 two separate deliveries of identical text open two turns [A3]; -AC003 a new prompt opens a new turn and leaves the prior turn in_progress [A4]; -AC004 system events open no turn [A3]. Mapping: FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 and FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 rows above (T7).
- TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-013 (TEST:773). Changes: its statement ("the UserPromptSubmit hook reuses the open turn on a duplicate prompt (returns turn-already-open, does not call beginTurn)") becomes "the prompt hook opens one turn per message event through the ingress mint: a retry or failsafe replay of one event reuses its turn, and two events with identical text open two turns"; its host test `HookTurnDedupe.Tests.ps1:76` changes with it (`#### Test churn`). Adds: TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC001 on hosts whose nonce is a stable per-message id (codex, grok, grok-bot), two parallel hook processes given one recorded event payload open one turn, and two events with identical text open two; on copilot a second new invocation with the same recorded payload mints nothing and fails the host session closed (`CopilotEqualFieldsSecondInvocationFailsClosedNoSecondMint`), while a stored mint retry or failsafe drain reuses its nonce; each host's case runs under the P0.2 gating rule, otherwise that host's `<Host>P02NotPassedTurnLoggingUnsupportedZeroMintsZeroWrites` case [A3]; -AC002 no-cancel case [A4].
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001 (FR:2070). Changes (architecture 4.4, OpenTurnFromPromptHook hook statuses, where the degraded mint replaces the degraded beginTurn of this statement, ac1, and ac3): the statement's "A degraded beginTurn keeps queryText, queryTitle, ..." becomes "A degraded mint (an outage or a retryable answer, classified by `Resolve-MintOutcome`) keeps the prompt's fields in its write-ahead mint record and opens the local turn `pending-<nonce>`"; delete "Supersede persists canceled."; AC001 ("A degraded begin retains every cached turn field ...") becomes "A degraded mint retains every prompt field in its write-ahead mint record and the provisional turn `pending-<nonce>`, and never replaces a turn with a reduced object" [A3]; AC002 reports `turn-opened` only after the mint is confirmed and otherwise `turn-opened-degraded` naming the `pending-<nonce>` id and the failsafe path (what-if X-2), and adds the non-opening hook outcomes `system-event-skipped`, `turn-open-contended`, and `turn-open-failed` [A3]; AC003's cached query text and cached title are the bound turn's `turns/<requestId>.yaml` fields (for a turn still pending, its write-ahead mint record), never `current-turn.yaml` [A4]; AC005 drops its supersession clause; AC004 ("A missing-turn appendDialog on an already degraded local turn performs at most one bounded recovery submit and then retains a session_dialog failsafe ...") is replaced by "A dialog bound to a `pending-<nonce>` id is captured behind its mint record and never sent; a dialog answered 404 is `request_unknown`, final; no plugin write creates a turn except the mint" (architecture 3.1, "Degraded missing-turn dialog"; 3.4; 4.4 PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt) [A5]. New ACs: FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001-AC006 opening a turn never persists canceled for another turn [A4]; -AC007 hook reports system-event-skipped/turn-open-contended without minting [A3].
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 (FR:2081). Changes: the statement's "canceled and cancelled supersede first persists may omit either field and receive exact None" becomes "client-supplied canceled and cancelled first persists may omit either field and receive exact None" (the server branch stays, `IsSupersededHookPersist`, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/SessionLogService.cs:1467-1471` at `a8d3934b`; no hook produces such a persist any more), and its "Otherwise beginTurn, update, append, and complete use explicit values" names the mint in place of beginTurn (hooks mint with the `None` sentinels and a later update sets the real values, architecture 3.4); AC002 ("canceled or cancelled supersession may omit it") and AC005 ("canceled/cancelled supersession") take the same "client-supplied canceled or cancelled first persist" wording (recon C15) [A4]; the statuses an agent surface may write (the legacy `cancelled` accepted, `closed` refused) are FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007's, cited here with no AC added (coordinator ownership ruling, 2026-10-13); AC004 "request drift" rejection permits explicit targeting of a registered open turn. New AC: FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002-AC006 explicit requestId edits to a registered open turn are not drift [A4].
- TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 (TR:2695). Changes: drop "supersede None" from the statement and AC003; the statement's "Same-request beginTurn is omit-unbound only when durable" becomes "A same-nonce mint returns the stored turn unchanged (`merged`, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC002)"; AC002 identity immutability applies per turn, and "Assert-ReplCurrentTurnFresh must not rewrite a cached session id" holds with the rotation rebind removed (architecture 3.1, "Compaction and resume"). New AC: TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002-AC004 per-turn identity binding across multiple open turns [A4].
- TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 (TEST:1142). Changes: AC001's supersession case becomes the no-cancel case (owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003); AC002's exit list adds `skipped_terminal` (A5), and its "Stop ordering" becomes no-auto-close (A7) (owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005, TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006 contributing; AC ownership above, closing recon traceability defect T3). New ACs: TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002-AC005 no-cancel [A4]; -AC006 skipped_terminal exit [A5]; -AC007 stop gate blocks and never closes [A7].
- FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 (FR:2360, `ac-1`). Changes: "Superseded hook turns persist canceled with planFile and todoId None sentinels" becomes "Client-supplied canceled turns persist with None sentinels" (server path kept; no hook producer); the accepted spellings (`canceled` and the legacy `cancelled`) and the `closed` refusal are FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007's, cited with no AC added. New AC: `ac-2` hooks never produce canceled turns, except the package-failure close of a request's own turn in `Pilot` or `Stateless` mode (FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009, `IsolationHooksNeverPersistCanceledExceptOwnTurnPackageFailure`) [A4].
- TR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 (TR:3261, `ac-1`). Changes: "Plugin superseded persist stamps planFile and todoId None and uses UpsertTurn" becomes "Client-supplied canceled persists stamp None". No new AC.
- TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 (TEST:1355) and TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-006 (TEST:1375). Changes: wording "superseded hook persist" becomes "client-supplied canceled persist"; server tests unchanged [A4 wording only]; TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 names FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 `ac-1` only as a contribution of its unchanged server cases, because TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 owns that changed AC (AC ownership, round-4 finding R4-F12).
- TR-MCP-REPL-019 (TR:2427) "Supersede preserves a server-refined turn title". Changes: status `deferred` with the note "Superseded by TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 AC-005 (PLAN-SESSIONTURN-SESSIONREVIEW-001)", because `superseded` is not a writable status: the REPL validator accepts only `pending`, `in_progress`, `completed`, and `deferred` for `updateTr` (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplYamlMessageValidator.cs:639` at `a8d3934b`; recon traceability defect T5); the supersede path is deleted (TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003). Its intent (a refined title is never clobbered) moves to new AC TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003-AC005 per-turn edits never overwrite another turn's title [A4].
- TR-MCP-REPL-015 (TR:2399). Changes: remove "supersede" from the incidental re-submit list; add the decision-list verbs and recordReview to it. New ACs: TR-MCP-REPL-015-AC001 title omitted on incidental re-submit of decision-list verbs [A6]; -AC002 recordReview never sends a title [S8].
- FR-MCP-REPL-010 (FR:1951). Changes: inline AC (1) "updates current-turn.yaml queryTitle and persists the new title to the server for the active turn" becomes "updates the `turns/<requestId>.yaml` title of, and persists the new title to the server for, the turn resolved by the explicit requestId override or the invocation's host-context binding (D2)"; inline AC (2) "workflow.sessionlog.setSessionTitle writes session-state.yaml title and persists the new session title to the server" is replaced by -AC003 (architecture 4.2, SetSessionTitleCommand: the verb targets the host session's own `mcpSessionId`); inline AC (3) drops "supersede". New structured ACs: FR-MCP-REPL-010-AC001 setTurnTitle targets an explicit requestId [A4]; -AC002 re-submits never revert titles [A4]; -AC003 setSessionTitle persists the title to the invoking host session's own recorded `mcpSessionId` and changes no other host session's server session or local state (what-if W-20) [A4].
- TEST-MCP-REPL-030 (TEST:1046) and TEST-MCP-REPL-040 (TEST:1066). Changes: remove the supersede cases; add the per-turn targeted title case; TEST-MCP-REPL-040's cache-only `failTurn` cases follow TR-MCP-REPL-020-AC001. New ACs: TEST-MCP-REPL-030-AC001, TEST-MCP-REPL-040-AC001 [A4].
- TR-MCP-PLUGIN-012 (TR:1632) "Session rotation re-binds current-turn cache". Changes: retired: status `deferred` with the note "Retired by PLAN-SESSIONTURN-SESSIONREVIEW-001: a recorded MCP session is never rotated, so there is nothing to rebind" (architecture 3.1, "Compaction and resume", and 4.4, StartHostSession, which replace this record's rebind); its AC1 ("rewrites current-turn.yaml sessionId to B", already contradicted by develop, which fills only an empty turn session id, `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1:2000-2004` at `a8d3934b`, and by FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 ac4) goes with the rebind at `repl-invoke.ps1:1954-1965`; its AC2 (the failsafe persist path is unaffected) is carried by FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC004. No new AC: its intent, an open turn is never hard-rejected after a SessionStart, is FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC001's compaction and resume clause [A4].
- TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-012 (TEST:771). Changes: status `deferred` with the note "Retired with TR-MCP-PLUGIN-012; replaced by `PostCompactSameHostSessionKeyKeepsMcpSessionIdOpenTurnClosesInOriginalSession` (TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003)"; its host test is removed in A9 with the retired rebind (`#### Test churn`). No ACs.
- FR-MCP-XAGENT-001 (FR:2572) "CompleteTurn refuses cross-sourceType current-turn rebind". Changes: the statement's subject, the workspace `current-turn.yaml`, becomes the verb's bound turn, because each host session reads only its own `turn-state.yaml`, so no shared workspace file carries another agent's session (architecture 3.4; FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC001); "Same-agent session rotation still rebinds" is deleted (architecture 3.1, "Compaction and resume", replaces it); AC1 "cross-sourceType mismatch refused or restored to originating cache" becomes "an explicit requestId not known in the verb's own host session's MCP session, or an original pair from another conversation, is refused with `request_unknown`, so no verb writes to another agent's or another host session's turn" [A4]; AC2 "same-agent rotation can complete without 500" becomes "after a SessionStart, PostCompact run, or resume of the same host session key, an open turn completes in its original MCP session without 500" [A4]; AC3 "CompleteTurn does not hijack a concurrent other requestId" becomes "completeTurn mutates only the turn process BindVerbToTurn resolves (explicit requestId, else the invocation's binding; `request_ambiguous` while two or more delivered requests are open, D9)" [A4].
- TR-MCP-XAGENT-001 (TR:3769). Changes: "Assert-ReplCurrentTurnFresh compares sessionId sourceType prefix. Different prefix refuses CompleteTurn persist. Same prefix still rebinds." becomes "`Assert-ReplCurrentTurnFresh` keeps only its marker-drift check (architecture 4.1); a verb's agent and MCP session scope is enforced by `Resolve-ReplTargetTurn` within its own host session (TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 AC-002), and nothing rebinds"; "persist identity keeps planFile/todoId" stays. No ACs today and none added (description only).
- TEST-MCP-XAGENT-001 (TEST:1503). Changes: the statement becomes "Pester proves a verb never writes to another agent's or another host session's turn, and a same-key SessionStart or compaction keeps the recorded MCP session, so completeTurn succeeds without Submit 500"; its Pester cases are re-fixtured (`#### Test churn`). No ACs.
- FR-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001 (FR:2339) "Plugin root session, cache, console, and persist identity". The store holds the statement as its one AC, so the statement and FR-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001-AC001 change together: "Root UserPromptSubmit stays on the root session while background agents run" adds "a background agent is recognized only by the host's subagent markers, never by prompt text, so a user prompt carrying a hostile-review brief opens its own turn" (architecture 5.4, which replaces the text-detected background-brief reuse); "Plugin cache replacement does not break in-flight hooks or reports named version-drift" adds "and an arrival bridge whose running version differs from the active cache is restarted from the active cache with `appliedThroughOffset` kept" (architecture 3.1 and 4.4, RunArrivalBridge); "beginTurn Submit timeout is degraded or queued with failsafe retained" becomes "a mint outage or retryable answer reports `turn-opened-degraded` with its failsafe mint record retained" (architecture 4.4, OpenTurnFromPromptHook); "completeTurn survives sessionId rebind without leftover failsafe" becomes "completeTurn after a SessionStart, PostCompact run, or resume of the same host session key closes the turn in its original MCP session without leftover failsafe" (architecture 3.1, "Compaction and resume"); "New PowerShell consoles inherit workspace identity from hook payload" stays [A3 +A4]. This record therefore leaves the catalog's "Related, unchanged" list ("strengthened, no edit").
- TR-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001 (TR:3234). Changes: "cache-scope keeps root session-state for UserPromptSubmit" becomes "each host session keeps its own `sessions/<hostSessionKey>/turn-state.yaml`, and `session-state.yaml` keeps develop's trust and header fields and is never read for targeting"; "ReplacePluginCache retains or rebinds" stays; "beginTurn degraded path and completeTurn persist identity are implemented in repl-invoke.ps1" becomes "the degraded mint (`Resolve-MintOutcome`) and the bound turn's persist identity are implemented in `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1`". No ACs (description only).
- TEST-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001 (TEST:1323), TEST-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-004 (TEST:1335), and TEST-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-005 (TEST:1339). Changes: -001's "background openSession does not rebind root, ..., beginTurn timeout is degraded queued, and completeTurn after sessionId rebind clears failsafe" becomes "another host session's openSession never retargets this host session's MCP session, ..., a mint timeout reports `turn-opened-degraded` with its failsafe mint record kept, completeTurn after a same-key SessionStart or compaction closes in the original session and clears its failsafe, and a prompt carrying a hostile-review brief opens its own turn and cancels nothing"; -004's "beginTurn persist timeout after failsafe returns degraded/queued and retains failsafe" becomes "a mint timeout returns `turn-opened-degraded` and retains the failsafe mint record"; -005's "completeTurn persist identity prefers current-turn sessionId after sessionId rebind" becomes "completeTurn persists to the bound turn's recorded MCP session". No ACs; their Pester cases are in `#### Test churn`.
- TR-MCP-REPL-014 (TR:2392) "Plugin setTurnTitle and setSessionTitle handlers". Changes: "a handler that updates current-turn.yaml queryTitle (Set-ReplTurnCacheField)" becomes "a handler bound by process BindVerbToTurn that updates the bound turn's `turns/<requestId>.yaml` title"; "a handler that read-modify-writes session-state.yaml title" becomes "a handler that retitles the invoking host session's own recorded `mcpSessionId` (SetSessionTitleCommand) and writes no `session-state.yaml`"; "fail closed (non-zero result) when no active session/turn cache is present" becomes "fail closed with the binding refusal (`request_unknown`, `request_ambiguous`, `host_session_unknown`, `host_session_ambiguous`, or, for setTurnTitle on a locally terminal turn, `turn_immutable`)" (architecture 4.1 and 4.2, which replace its `current-turn.yaml` title targeting). New AC: TR-MCP-REPL-014-AC001 setTurnTitle and setSessionTitle target the bound turn and the host session's own MCP session, never read or write `current-turn.yaml` or `session-state.yaml`, and fail closed with the binding refusal codes [A4].
- TEST-MCP-REPL-029 (TEST:1044). Changes: the statement follows TR-MCP-REPL-014 (the bound turn file and the turn title route; the host session's own MCP session and the session title route; the binding refusals), and its Pester case is re-fixtured (`#### Test churn`). New AC: TEST-MCP-REPL-029-AC001 both verbs are proven against two host sessions and two open turns [A4].
- TR-MCP-REPL-020 (TR:2434) "failTurn resolves the active turn from the plugin cache". Changes: title "failTurn resolves its turn through the invocation binding"; "failTurn SHALL resolve the active session and turn the same way the rest of the plugin does, falling back to the cache when the in-process state is empty" becomes "failTurn resolves its target through process BindVerbToTurn like every verb (explicit requestId, else the invocation's binding to the host session's write target) and closes it as failed even when the in-process REPL state is empty; it never falls back to `current-turn.yaml`, and with no write target it is refused with `request_unknown` naming the host session key" (architecture 4.1, which replaces its cache fallback). New AC: TR-MCP-REPL-020-AC001 that rule [A4].

New AC: FR-MCP-171-AC001 a degraded persist keeps the bound turn in progress in its turn file and failsafe record and never reads or writes `current-turn.yaml` [A4]. The retryable answer set this record names (architecture 4.4, PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt, adds 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and a busy store's 503 `persistence_error`) is the persistence domain's change.

#### Test churn

Counts are at `a8d3934b` (counted with `git show a8d3934b:<path>` in PowerShell); host-repo lines are at each repo's checked-out head (`mcpserver-claude-code-plugin@472340b`). Every item is a step of the gate of the change that causes it, never an exclusion (architecture 8.4). Re-seeding is owned here; an outcome change another domain causes in the same files (drain rules, receipts, Stop gate, completion gate) is listed in that domain's `#### Test churn`.

- Core Pester re-seeding (G4, PluginCorePowerShell): 120 `current-turn.yaml` references in 7 files of `plugins/core/test-fixtures/pester` (`PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1` 48, `SessionLogP2Contracts.Tests.ps1` 48, `SessionLogLifecycle.Tests.ps1` 9, `SessionLogLifecycleMetadata.Tests.ps1` 6, `TriagePluginIdentity.Tests.ps1` 6, `SessionLogReviewRegression.Tests.ps1` 2, `SessionLogAuditReconcile.Tests.ps1` 1) and the `session-state.yaml` targeting seeds beside them (its trust and header fields stay, architecture 3.1, "Host session start state") are re-seeded through `sessions/<hostSessionKey>/turn-state.yaml` and `turns/<requestId>.yaml` with a host session key in the invocation context; each case keeps its asserted outcome except those below. Owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 (`BindingCurrentTurnYamlNeverUsedForTargeting`). [A4]
- No-turn refusals: `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1:313,948` keep exit 1, with the refusal now `request_unknown` (no write target) or `host_session_unknown` (no key). Owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 (`BindingNoWriteTargetRequestUnknownNamesHostSessionKey`). [A4]

### Domain: Turn persistence, decision lists, and closing

Bounded contexts: SessionTurnLogging (the server turn writes, their per-turn outcomes, turn-level isolation, and transactions), PluginCorePowerShell (receipts, the failsafe drain, the Stop gate, decision lists, model evidence, and the clear-session coordinator), and HostPluginAdoption (the clear-session skill text and its Pester case in the nine plugin repos, S9). Use cases: UC-2 and UC-3 (architecture 7.3 and 7.4). This section derives from the architecture document: section 3.1 ("Failsafe drain", "Deadlock and busy outcomes", "Retryable answers", "Turn-level isolation", "Retry bound", "Mint isolation", "Turn statuses on agent surfaces", the `closed` status decision, and the clear-session closing rule), section 3.4 (Phase A wire contracts and the Stop gate), sections 3.5.2, 3.5.3, and 3.5.6 (SerializableWrite, IdempotentCreate, DbErrorClassifier, ReadableKeyAllocator, ClassifiedErrorException and ErrorCodes, TurnStatus, TurnLocator, TurnWritePipeline, Test-ModelEvidence, Get-CloseRemedy, Update-TurnState), section 4.2 (its storage rules and the Submit, Upsert, Patch, Replace, Complete, and Fail units), section 4.3 (policy TerminalTurnOutcomeGuard), section 4.4 (processes PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt, DrainFailsafe, and EvaluateStopGate), section 5.4 (process ClearSession), and section 8.4 (test churn). It adds no design decision; a reading marked "(inference)" is listed for the coordinator in `review-arch-r5/edit4-pd-persist.md`.

#### Functional requirements

FR-SUPPORT-016 Turn replace and section API (created in P0. The behavior is cited in source and tests under the legacy id `FR-SUPPORT-010G`, which the requirements grammar rejects (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/RequirementsWorkflow.cs:32-34,400-406`: the suffix must be three digits), so P0 creates it as FR-SUPPORT-016 with TR-SUPPORT-CORE-016 and TEST-SUPPORT-024 (all three verified free in the live store), and S4, the first slice that edits these files, renames every legacy citation; the legacy id is cited 36 times in 9 files per `rg -n -F FR-SUPPORT-010G src tests`: `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/SessionLogService.cs` (5, including `TurnSection`/`ParseSection` at 1287-1317), `ISessionLogService.cs` (6), `src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Controllers/SessionLogController.cs` (7), `src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/McpStdio/FwhMcpTools.SessionLog.cs` (6), `src/QBrainAi.Client/SessionLogClient.cs` (7), `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/SessionLogModels.cs` (1), `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.cs` (2), and the integration tests `SessionLogControllerTests.cs` (1) and `SessionLogReplaceDeleteControllerTests.cs` (1); also cited by the retired `CODEX-HANDOFF.md:206`, readable with `git show f56dcf70:CODEX-HANDOFF.md`). Statement: the session-log API SHALL support replacing a turn (PUT turn), replacing or resetting one section of a turn (PUT/DELETE section), and deleting one item of a collection section (DELETE item), for the sections actions, tags, context, dialog, commits, designDecisions, requirementsDiscovered, filesModified, blockers, and (after Phase B) agentReview and hostileReview, which the section routes and the MCP section tools hand to the review units RecordSessionTurnReviewCommand and ResetSessionTurnReviewCommand (architecture 4.2, its closing paragraph, and 5.2). Same family and AC style as FR-SUPPORT-010E/010F and FR-SUPPORT-014/015 (`ac-N`). The cited test names are the analyzer-branch spellings (`chore/netanalyzers-all`, CA1707); at `a8d3934b` they carry underscores (`tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.cs:26-274`).
- `ac-1` Replacing a turn clears omitted scalars and collections and preserves the workspace stamp on children. [existing behavior; verified in P0 by `SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.ReplaceTurnAsyncClearsOmittedScalarsAndCollections`, `ReplaceTurnAsyncPreservesWorkspaceStampOnChildren` (`SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.cs:26,61`); both replace a turn with `completed` (`:36`, `:71`), so from the CompletionGate enforcement change set (S10) on they stay green only through the `WithCompletionEvidence(turn, requestClass)` fixture helper, which adds a class and terminal reviews and weakens no assertion (architecture 8.4); they are not green unchanged at every later gate]
- `ac-2` Replacing a section changes only that section; an empty payload clears it; clearing a section removes all its items; additive upsert never removes items. [existing; `ReplaceTurnSectionAsyncReplacesOnlyNamedSection`, `ReplaceTurnSectionAsyncEmptyPayloadClearsSection`, `ClearTurnSectionAsyncRemovesAllItemsInSection`, `UpsertTurnAsyncStaysAdditiveDoesNotRemoveExistingItems`; these seed `in_progress` turns (`SeedFullSession`, `SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.cs:303-335`), so the completion gate never reaches them]
- `ac-3` Item delete removes exactly the addressed item (string sections by value, commits by SHA, actions by order) and returns false for an unknown key. [existing; `DeleteTurnItemAsyncStringSectionRemovesByValue`, `DeleteTurnItemAsyncCommitRemovesBySha`, `DeleteTurnItemAsyncActionRemovesByOrder`, `DeleteTurnItemAsyncUnknownKeyReturnsFalse`]
- `ac-4` Replacing an unknown section throws `ArgumentException`. [existing; `ReplaceTurnSectionAsyncUnknownSectionThrows`, which asserts exactly this; the alias set in `ParseSection` (`SessionLogService.cs:1300-1317` at `a8d3934b`) is existing behavior but no test asserts it today, so it is not claimed as an AC]
- `ac-5` Sections `agentReview` and `hostileReview` (accepted case-insensitively as `agentreview`, `agent-review`, `agent_review`, `hostilereview`, `hostile-review`, `hostile_review`): replace sets the record on any turn status, terminal included (latest wins); clear resets it to the `ReviewDefaults` record for its kind and the turn's current class on an `in_progress`, `canceled`, `cancelled`, or `closed` turn, and on a `completed` or `failed` turn is refused with 400 `review_section_reset_on_terminal_turn` and changes nothing (FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-001-AC004); item delete is refused with 400; the MCP tools `sessionlog_replace_section`, `sessionlog_clear_section`, and `sessionlog_delete_item` apply the same rules to a review section or alias (architecture 3.4, "New surfaces", and 5.2, ResetSessionTurnReviewCommand). [S4 service, S5 REST/MCP/client]

FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 Truthful persist outcomes.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC001 Session submit returns a per-turn outcome, exactly one of `created`, `merged`, or `skipped_terminal`, with `storedStatus` and `reason` on REST, MCP `sessionlog_submit`, and the typed client; there is no `skipped_duplicate` outcome on any surface (operator, 2026-10-09: "Remove"); a resubmit identical to the stored turn reports `merged` with reason `no_change`; duplicate entries for one requestId in one submission are applied last-wins (today's behavior) and report one outcome for that requestId. The submit keeps status 201, and MCP `sessionlog_submit` returns `{ success, id, turns }` (architecture 4.2, SubmitSessionLogCommand). The outcome is decided by policy TerminalTurnOutcomeGuard (seam `ITerminalTurnOutcomeGuard`, architecture 4.3), which only the submit applies. The single-turn routes (turn upsert, PATCH, PUT, complete, fail) keep today's request and response bodies, report no per-turn outcome, and apply no terminal-turn outcome guard, because they are the explicit route for deliberate status corrections (`SessionLogService.cs:1610` at `a8d3934b`; `SingleTurnRoutesTerminalTurnStatusCorrectionTodayBodiesNoOutcomeGuard`, which extends the service-level `SessionLogSupersessionTests.UpsertTurnAsyncExplicitStatusCanCorrectTerminalTurn`, `SessionLogSupersessionTests.cs:125`, to every single-turn route). [A1]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC002 The plugin maps `skipped_terminal` to `persisted:false`, code `skipped_terminal`, non-zero exit, and a cleared failsafe record. A retryable answer (a transport outage, 503 `backend_unavailable`, a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`, or 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, the answer a deadlock or busy store returns once SerializableWrite's four attempts are spent) keeps the write-ahead failsafe record and reports `persisted:false`, code `queued`, exit 0, and the request stays open and ledgered until the drain confirms the write; a final answer on a live verb clears the record and reports the server code with exit 1, except a `rejected` receipt (an outcome whose identity or readback is not confirmed), which keeps it; the failsafe drain stops at the first retryable answer without counting an attempt and keeps that record and every later one (FR-MCP-REPL-011). A review is the exception: it keeps its no-failsafe rule and reports the retryable code with exit 1 (FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002, architecture 5.2). (Architecture 3.1, "Deadlock and busy outcomes" and "Retryable answers", and 4.4, PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt and SendBoundWrite; what-if W-2 and X-10, reconciled with the architecture: a retryable 409 is handled as an outage, never as a final refusal with exit 1 and no failsafe record.) [A5]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC003 Omitted-status enrichment and review-section writes on terminal turns report `merged` or success (review-section writes from S4, `SessionLogTurnReviewSectionTests.SectionPutTerminalTurnReportsSuccess`). [A1 +S4]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC004 A plugin verb targeting a locally terminal turn is refused with `turn_immutable` without a server call, except `recordReview` and requestClass-only updates, which are accepted on it, including an unqualified one on a closed write target with no delivered request open (architecture 4.4, BindVerbToTurn, "Closed write target"; what-if X-7 settled there; the exception from S8 and S9, when those verbs ship: `TerminalTurnRecordReviewAndClassOnlyAcceptedOtherVerbsTurnImmutable`). [A5 +S8]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC005 A result from a server without per-turn outcomes is reported `outcomeVerified:false`; its failsafe record is cleared, because the server stored the write, but it is never a confirmed close, so `openDelivered` and the stop ledger keep the request (architecture 4.4, MapSubmitOutcome). [A5]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC006 Writes to different turns of one session never answer a conflict: a single-turn write (turn upsert, PATCH, PUT, complete, fail) and the mint read their session row only to locate the parent and write only the turn they write and its children (architecture 3.5.2, isolation scope), so two concurrent writes to different turns of one session both commit within the storage budget with no retryable answer, on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server; a multi-turn submission keeps its session-level Serializable scope; the session summary refresh after a single-turn write is its own short write after the turn commits, and its failure never fails the turn write; a write whose transient conflict (deadlock, serialization failure, or busy store) outlasts SerializableWrite's four attempts, run within the unit's storage budget, answers 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and saves nothing (operator, 2026-10-12, verbatim answer "Turn-level (Recommended)"; architecture 3.1 "Turn-level isolation", "Mint isolation", "Retry bound", and "Deadlock and busy outcomes", and 4.2, storage rules; recon `risk.md` R-R3). [A1]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007 Agent surfaces (REST, MCP, the typed client, the REPL, the plugin, and QBAgent's internal `mcp_session_turn_update` and `mcp_session_turn_complete` tools) write only the turn statuses `in_progress`, `completed`, `failed`, `canceled`, and the legacy `cancelled`; a submit, turn upsert, PATCH, or PUT naming `closed` or any other status is refused with 400 `validation_error`, `details.field` `status`, before any write, while server-internal writers and existing rows keep `closed` (operator, 2026-10-12, verbatim answer "Refuse from agent surfaces (Recommended)"; architecture 3.1, "Turn statuses on agent surfaces", 3.5.3, `TurnStatus`, and 4.2: the refusal lives in the handlers, and in `UpsertTurnAsync` for QBAgent's origin only). This AC is the one owner of the `closed` refusal; FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002, FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002, and FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 cite it and add no AC of their own (coordinator ruling, 2026-10-13, `review-arch-r5/MERGE-BRIEF.md`; SessionTurnLogging owns `TurnStatus`, architecture 8.3). [A1]

FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 The stop gate enforces and never closes turns.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC001 Blocks with `turn_in_progress` when a delivered request in the stop ledger (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003-AC004) is still in progress; a withdrawn request still in progress blocks under AC009, and a minted request still `queued` only warns. A request in state `queued` (minted, not yet delivered) never produces `turn_in_progress`; the gate warns, naming its requestId and enqueue time. A Stop in one host session never blocks or warns on another host session's turns (`StopGateOtherHostSessionNeverBlocksOrWarns`), and a Stop payload without the host row's session key is refused and writes nothing (architecture 4.4, EvaluateStopGate, and 3.1: "A write is never attributed by guesswork"; `StopGatePayloadWithoutHostSessionKeyRefusedWritesNothing`). A request enters the stop ledger when it is delivered, so a request minted before a passing Stop and delivered after it is evaluated at the next Stop (`StopGateEnqueueR2PassForR1DequeueR2NextStopBlocksUntilClosed`, including a restart between the two Stops). Unsupported-session failsafe: in a host session with `unsupportedSince` (whatever its reason, host binding table unsupported-session rule), every session-log write the model makes for a turn of that host session still in progress when `unsupportedSince` was recorded (a delivered open request, or a `withdrawn` queue entry still `in_progress`, addressed by its original pair; a minted request still `queued` is not covered and stays a warning) (evidence actions, design decisions, `requestClass`, and the close call; a review is never captured, keeping the no-failsafe rule of TR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-005 AC-002 and S9, and is refused with `turn_logging_unsupported`) is captured as a plugin failsafe record that carries the turn's C4 binding and root-id envelope, is marked `unsupported_session`, and is never sent to the server during that host session; every mint and every write for any other turn stays refused with `turn_logging_unsupported` and captures nothing (`UnsupportedSessionMintOrWriteForOtherTurnRefusedNothingCaptured`). The Stop gate evaluates such a turn with its captured records counted: it blocks until the model's close is captured, with a remedy that names only the missing model evidence, the missing `requestClass` (from S8/S9, when the plugin's requestClass verb ships; a class write is captured like any other write), and the close call (all accepted, AC002; the plugin pre-check lets that close through, FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC002), because only the completion gate's terminal reviews cannot be written in that session; it then only warns with the cause and the retained failsafe records. The completion gate is judged at the drain: the server's gate judges the drained close, and a gate rejection keeps the turn in progress and listed with its original pair at that SessionStart, where its reviews are recorded through the C5 pair (FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002-AC002) before it is closed (`UnsupportedSessionReviewRefusedNotCaptured`, `UnsupportedSessionCodeTurnDrainedCloseRejectedByGateStaysListedReviewViaPairThenCloses`). The first SessionStart for the agent after that host session has ended, or a SessionStart that clears its cause under its rule, drains the captured records in original order through the trusted recovery flow with the turn's original (MCP `sessionId`, `requestId`) pair (C5); a post-compact or other SessionStart of the still-live host session drains none of them and counts no attempt (FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002; `DrainUnsupportedSessionRecordOfLiveHostSessionNotDrainedNoAttemptCounted`), and a record whose turn is no longer in progress on the server moves to the failsafe quarantine with its `.reason.txt` and is never forced onto that turn (architecture 4.4, DrainFailsafe and ReplayBoundRecord; what-if X-4 settled there; `DrainUnsupportedSessionRecordTurnNoLongerInProgressQuarantinedNeverForced`). Each reason is covered from an earlier open turn (`StopGateUnknownBridgeRecordEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedThenWarnsOnly`, `StopGateTruncatedTranscriptEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedThenWarnsOnly`, `StopGateSyntheticUnsupportedSinceAnyReasonEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedThenWarnsOnly` in A7; the real duplicate-hook transition `DuplicateHookAfterDeliveryUnsupportedSinceOpenTurnCloseCaptured` for copilot in A9 and `ClineDuplicateHookAfterDeliveryUnsupportedSinceOpenTurnCloseCaptured` for classic cline in A8). A withdrawn-before-delivery turn is covered too: its decision, its `requestClass` (from S8/S9), and `failTurn` by original pair are captured, the next Stop warns instead of blocking, and the drain closes it (after S10 the server gate accepts that fail, which needs only the class) (`StopGateWithdrawnBeforeDeliveryThenUnsupportedCloseCapturedByPairNextStopWarnsDrainCloses`). A Stop in another live host session still blocks on that session's own turns (`StopGateEqualFieldFailClosedEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedToFailsafeThenWarnsOnlyDrainsWithOriginalPair`, `StopGateBridgeAmbiguityEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedToFailsafeThenWarnsOnlyDrainsWithOriginalPair`). Outage close: a model close, a withdrawn request's `failTurn` (AC009), or, from Phase C, the plugin's package-failure `canceled` close (process RunStatelessInvocation; it never needs model evidence) held in the ordinary plugin failsafe by a retryable answer (a transport outage, a retryable 503, or 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, architecture 3.1, "Retryable answers") turns that turn's block into the existing degraded persistence warning (AC005, naming the package failure for the `canceled` close); its ledger entry stays until the server confirms the close (`StopGateOutageCloseQueuedWarnsDegradedLedgerKeptUntilConfirmed`; `StatelessPackageFailureCancelCloseQueuedByOutageStopWarnsDegradedDrainConfirmsAndClears` in AgentContextPluginCore, what-if WC-M1). [A7 +A8 +A9 +S8 +S10 +AgentContextPluginCore]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002 The plugin close verbs refuse to close a turn that has no model-authored action, design decision, or commit (`Test-ModelEvidence`, architecture 3.5.6, which counts the turn's records captured in the failsafe and never a hook invocation; the evidence predicate TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC003 keeps), with code `turn_missing_model_evidence`; the turn stays in progress, so the stop gate blocks it as `turn_in_progress` with the accumulated remedy of the wire contract (every unmet prerequisite, then the valid close call, computed by `Get-CloseRemedy`, architecture 3.5.6). A turn closed without model evidence by another surface accepts no plugin edit, so the gate reports it as a warning naming it and never blocks on it. Every stop-gate block names a remedy the plugin accepts on the blocked turn's current status (`StopGateEveryBlockRemedyAcceptedOnBlockedTurnStatus`, `CloseVerbNoEvidenceRefusedTurnStaysInProgress`, `StopGateClosedWithoutEvidenceElsewhereWarnsOnly`, `CloseVerbCommitOnlyEvidenceAccepted`). [A7 +A8]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003 Blocks with `session_turn_completion_gate` a turn whose most recent close the server refused with that code (recorded locally from the server response; such a turn is still in progress), naming the server's missing items first in its remedy (`StopGateServerGateRefusalBlocksWithServerMissingItems`), except for an already open turn of a host session with `unsupportedSince`, whose completion gate is judged at the drain (AC001); that exception is evaluated before a remembered refusal can block (`StopGateGateRefusedThenUnsupportedSinceCloseCapturedWarnsNotBlocks`). There is no marker condition: the server gate (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002) is the only completion authority. Its remedy is the accumulated per-turn remedy of the wire contract, so it also names any missing model evidence. [S8 +S9 +S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC004 Never calls `completeTurn` or `failTurn`. [A7 +A8]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC005 Warns without blocking for model-canceled turns, retained failsafe records, and degraded persistence. The warning list is exactly the architecture's (4.4, EvaluateStopGate, Warnings). Per turn: model-canceled turns (a plugin `canceled` close names its package failure), a turn closed without model evidence by another surface, degraded persistence (a model close, a withdrawn request's `failTurn`, or the plugin's package-failure `canceled` close held in the failsafe by a retryable answer, AC001), a captured unsupported-session close (naming the `unsupportedSince` reason and the captured records), and queued undelivered requests (naming the requestId and enqueue time). Per host session: each `unsupportedSince` reason (`queue_removal_ambiguous`, `indistinguishable_arrival`, `duplicate_prompt_hook`, `arrival_bridge_format_unrecognized`, or any other) naming the open turns of its fail-closed host session and their captured `unsupported_session` failsafe records; retained failsafe records, which are a mint refused for a final reason, queued or quarantined, and an `open-failed` queue entry, each naming the prompt, and every quarantined record of an open turn with its reason (what-if W-8; FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008 and -AC010); `duplicate_delivery_suspected`; `nonce_source_missing` and `turn-open-contended`, each naming the prompt (what-if W-5); `arrival_bridge_format_unrecognized`; an earlier `stop_gate_overridden_by_host`; a live bridge host session whose arrival bridge is not running; and a turn in progress in the legacy `current-turn.yaml` at upgrade (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC004, `StopGateLegacyCurrentTurnInProgressWarned`). Open turns of ended host sessions are not a Stop warning, because a Stop never blocks or warns on another host session's turns (AC001); the next SessionStart lists them with their pairs (AC006; what-if X-9). A legacy failsafe record that names no request is quarantined by the drain and belongs to no open turn, so it is no Stop warning; plugin Status counts it with the quarantine counts (architecture 3.1, "Quarantined legacy records"; FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC004; `plugins/core/lib-ps/mcp-status.ps1:85-88` at `a8d3934b`). [A7 +AgentContextPluginCore]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC006 Passes and clears the ledger only when every check passes. Every Stop invocation re-evaluates the ledger, including when the host reports that an earlier Stop already blocked (Claude Code `stop_hook_active`, Grok `stopHookActive`): while any turn remains open it blocks again with the same findings. If the host ends the session anyway (its own continuation limit), the gate records `stop_gate_overridden_by_host` with the open turn ids in the ledger, and the next SessionStart or prompt hook shows it as a warning until the model closes those turns. A ledger entry whose closing write the server has not confirmed terminal stays in the ledger across passing Stops until the server reports it terminal. Turns still in progress when their host session ends (any reason, including `/clear` and a host override) stay in progress and are listed at the next SessionStart for that agent in the workspace with their original (MCP `sessionId`, `requestId`) pairs; closing one through the trusted recovery flow with that pair succeeds, and a `requestId` alone is refused (C5). [A7 +A8]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC007 The existing failed-build and stale-session blocks are retained (architecture 3.4, Stop gate, and 4.4, EvaluateStopGate). The failed-build block reads `lastBuildStatus` from this host session's `turns/<requestId>.yaml` files, where code-verify writes it in place of `current-turn.yaml` (architecture 4.1; `plugins/core/lib-ps/plugin-hook.ps1:1034,1262` at `a8d3934b`; `StopGateFailedBuildBlockReadsLastBuildStatusFromTurnFile`), and the stale-session block evaluates this host session's open turns from the per-host-session store (`StopGateStaleSessionBlockEvaluatedFromPerHostSessionStore`). [A7]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC008 Warns (never merges, never blocks) with `duplicate_delivery_suspected` when one host session minted two requestIds within 2 seconds for prompts whose SHA-256 of the normalized text is equal, naming both requestIds so the operator can check hook registration; the warning is diagnostic only (it can fire only where a host's event identity is weaker than expected) and is not evidence of D1 compliance. [A7]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC009 Blocks with `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` while a `withdrawn` request is still `in_progress`, naming it, its withdrawal record (`popAll` or `remove` reason), and the action: close it with `workflow.sessionlog.failTurn` (`requestId` override, reason "withdrawn before delivery"); hooks still never close it. The instruction text includes a designDecision and, from S8/S9 (when the plugin's requestClass and recordReview verbs ship), the `requestClass` the close needs, so following it exactly makes the next Stop pass (`StopGateWithdrawnFailedPerInstructionNextStopPasses`). When that `failTurn` is held in the plugin failsafe by a retryable answer, the gate warns with the degraded persistence warning instead of blocking until the drain confirms the close (architecture 4.4, EvaluateOpenTurnAtStop; what-if W-12; `StopGateWithdrawnFailHeldByOutageWarnsDegradedNotBlocked`). [A7]

FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006 Decision lists through workflow verbs.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC001 `updateTurn`, `appendActions`, `completeTurn`, `failTurn` accept `designDecisions`, `requirementsDiscovered`, `blockers` and persist them. [A6]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC002 Those lists are part of the dedupe fingerprint, so a change to only them is persisted. [A6]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC003 Model-evidence counters count only model-invoked verbs, never hook-invoked ones (TypeScript hosts: `TsHostHookAuthoredWritesNotEvidence`). [A6 +A8]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC004 One decision call (`appendDecisions`, or a decision passed to `updateTurn`/`completeTurn`/`failTurn`) writes the workspace triple (C7): a `designDecisions` entry, a dialog item with category `decision`, and a `design_decision` action; replaying the same call adds nothing. Two separate invocations with the same decision text add two triples, while a replay of one invocation (a failsafe drain of its record, or a resend of the same write) adds nothing (what-if W-23; architecture 3.1, "Decision replay": the replay is per invocation, because each invocation captures its own binding, C4; `AppendDecisionsSameTextTwoInvocationsTwoTriplesReplayAddsNothing`). [A6]

#### Technical requirements

- TR-SUPPORT-CORE-016 Turn replace and section service (created in P0, same style as TR-SUPPORT-010E/010F). `ac-1` `ReplaceTurnAsync`, `ReplaceTurnSectionAsync`, `ClearTurnSectionAsync`, `DeleteTurnItemAsync` in `SessionLogService` with `TurnSection`/`ParseSection` (`SessionLogService.cs:1287-1317`) are the single implementation behind the REST, MCP, and client surfaces; the turn PUT reaches `ReplaceTurnAsync` through the delegating handler of ReplaceSessionTurnCommand (architecture 4.2), which keeps today's behavior [existing; P0]; `ac-2` `TurnSection` gains `AgentReview` and `HostileReview` with the FR-SUPPORT-016 `ac-5` aliases; for those sections the section PUT (REST, and MCP `sessionlog_replace_section`) dispatches RecordSessionTurnReviewCommand, which delegates its save to `ReplaceTurnSectionAsync`, and the section DELETE (REST, and MCP `sessionlog_clear_section`) dispatches ResetSessionTurnReviewCommand, which delegates to `ClearTurnSectionAsync`, where a completed or failed turn is refused with `review_section_reset_on_terminal_turn` and no mutation and any other turn gets the `ReviewDefaults` record; every non-review section keeps today's direct call (architecture 5.2) [S4]; `ac-3` item delete on a review section (REST, and MCP `sessionlog_delete_item`) is refused by the existing delete-item handler with 400 [S4 service, S5 surfaces].
- TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 Server per-turn submit outcomes, ingress mint, and turn write isolation (architecture 4.1 to 4.3; each command record and its handler are two files under `src/QBrainAi.Services/SessionLog/Commands/`, namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.SessionLog.Commands`). AC-001 SubmitSessionLogCommand delegates to `ISessionLogService.SubmitWithOutcomesAsync`, which upserts the session row and runs TurnWritePipeline in mode `Submit` for each turn inside one session-level SerializableWrite; `SubmitAsync` delegates to it; every unit of this TR also answers WorkspaceResolutionBehavior's 400 `validation_error` with `details.field` `workspacePath` after its dispatch (architecture 3.5.1 and 4.2; tested once by that behavior's cases in SharedComponents), which the plugin reports as a final code; AC-002 the seam `ITerminalTurnOutcomeGuard` (namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.SessionLog`, policy TerminalTurnOutcomeGuard, architecture 4.3) replaces the inline guard at `SessionLogService.cs:1606-1618` (at `a8d3934b`) without changing its rule, and only mode `Submit` applies it; AC-003 `SessionLogSubmitResult.turns` on REST (status 201), MCP `sessionlog_submit` (`{ success, id, turns }`), the client, and the REPL strategy; AC-004 the decorators `SessionLogSanitizingService`, `FederatedSessionLogService`, and `TransactionGatedSessionLogService` forward `SubmitWithOutcomesAsync` and `MintTurnAsync`; AC-005 a new mint operation (MintSessionTurnCommand; its DTOs `SessionLogMintTurnRequest` and `SessionLogMintTurnResult` in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/SessionLog/`, namespace `QBrainAi.Client.Models.SessionLog`), because the existing begin route requires `{requestId}` in its path (`SessionLogController.cs:388-396`) and the client interpolates it (`SessionLogClient.cs:174-191`): REST `POST /qbrainai/sessionlog/{agent}/{sessionId}/mint` (the controller's second route attribute also serves it under `/mcpserver/sessionlog`, `SessionLogController.cs:15-16`) with body `SessionLogMintTurnRequest { ingressNonce (required, `^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,64}$`), queryTitle, queryText, model, planFile (required; path or `None`), todoId (required; TODO id or `None`) }` validated like the begin body (`docs/context/session-log-schema.md:16,64-66`); responses 201 `{ requestId, outcome: "created" }`, 200 `{ requestId, outcome: "merged", storedStatus }` when the nonce already exists in that session, 400 `ingress_nonce_invalid`, 400 `validation_error` for an invalid session id (TurnLocator `InvalidIdentifier`) or the existing planFile/todoId validation errors, 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` after SerializableWrite's four attempts (nothing saved; the plugin keeps its failsafe mint record as for an outage), 404 with detail code `session_not_open` when the session is not open (only the trusted mint adapter interprets an authenticated, bound mint-endpoint 404 without a detail code as a missing route, as on a server older than A1, per FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008 and the scoped C9 exception); no MCP tool (the mint is not a model-visible tool; every `[McpServerTool]` in `FwhMcpTools.SessionLog.cs` is model-visible); client `MintTurnAsync(agent, sessionId, ingressNonce, queryTitle, queryText, model, planFile, todoId)`; REPL `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn`, which the plugin wrappers refuse with `mint_requires_host_ingress` unless the call comes from a hook or adapter entry point (`MCP_PLUGIN_HOOK_INVOCATION=1`; a guard against accidental model use, not a security boundary) and which no model-facing skill or tool description mentions; the begin route and `BeginTurnAsync` keep today's behavior; AC-006 `SessionLogTurnEntity.IngressNonce` (string 64, nullable) with a unique filtered index on (WorkspaceId, SessionLogId, IngressNonce) where not null and a second unique filtered index on (WorkspaceId, RequestId) where IngressNonce is not null (minted ids are workspace-unique across sessions; legacy rows are untouched), added by migration `<utc>_AddSessionLogTurnIngressNonce` for SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server (StorageSchema); the mint is IdempotentCreate keyed by (session, ingressNonce) with policy `ReturnStored` inside SerializableWrite, and a single-turn write under turn-level isolation (architecture 3.1, "Mint isolation", and 3.5.2): a mint whose nonce exists returns the stored turn's requestId as `merged` whatever the payload; a violation of the nonce index ends the attempt, and SerializableWrite's rerun returns the winner as `merged`; the insert takes its requestId from ReadableKeyAllocator strategy `RandomHex` (InsertMintedTurnWithRetry, architecture 4.2; the `req-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>-<8 hex>` format is tested once, as `ReadableKeyAllocatorTests.ReadableKeyAllocatorRandomHexRequestIdFormatCollisionNextCandidate` of TR-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001, SharedComponents), and a violation of the requestId index is a declared key collision retried with the next candidate up to 6 attempts in all, then 500 `mint_collision_exhausted` (C9 ACKed by Codex at 2026-10-09T23:22:30Z); a transient conflict keeps SerializableWrite's default bound (four attempts), then 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and nothing saved; any other database error fails the mint; AC-007 every turn write runs TurnWritePipeline inside SerializableWrite (architecture 3.5.2, 3.5.3, and 4.2, storage rules), using the components as TR-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 AC-001 (`DbErrorClassifier`) and AC-002 (`SerializableWrite`, with its bound, its in-memory single run, and its caller-owned transaction refusal) define them, which this AC does not restate; it states their use by the turn writes: a single-turn write (upsert, patch, replace, complete, fail) and the mint read their session row only to locate the parent and write only their turn with its children (turn-level isolation), a submission keeps its session-level scope, and the session summary refresh after a single-turn write is its own short write after the turn commits, whose failure never fails the turn write; the turn writes call `DbErrorClassifier` in place of develop's message-text predicates (`SessionLogService.cs:234,357,764-797,812-817` at `a8d3934b`); their four attempts run within the unit's storage budget, then 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and nothing saved; and develop's own turn-write cases for the caller-owned transaction refusal (`SessionLogService.cs:119-128`) and the single in-memory run (`SessionLogService.cs:155`) keep their outcome (section "Test churn"). [A1]
- TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 Plugin outcome mapping (process PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt and its nested activities SendBoundWrite and MapSubmitOutcome, architecture 4.4). AC-001 `Get-ReplTurnWriteOutcome`; AC-002 receipt `code`/`storedStatus` (codes `persisted`, `unchanged`, `queued`, `lost`, `rejected`, `skipped_terminal`, `turn_missing_model_evidence`, or the server code with its `retryable` flag; `reason`; `outcomeVerified`); AC-003 exit 1 and failsafe cleared on skip; AC-004 local `turn_immutable` refusal; AC-005 the write-ahead failsafe record is keyed by the bound requestId; a retryable answer, read from the REPL envelope's `code` and `retryable` (the one code table `ErrorCodes`, C9; the REPL classifier carries the server's `ClassifiedError` through at every status, so it keeps `retryable` for `conflict`, `ReplClassifierConflictRetryableTrueKept` in SharedComponents), keeps the record with receipt `queued`, exit 0; a final answer on a live verb, the REPL client mutation policy's `transaction_degraded` and `mutation_not_transactional` included (C9), clears the record and reports its code (every exit-1 receipt except `rejected` and `lost`); a repeated unchanged write with the fingerprint of the turn's last confirmed write is not sent (`unchanged`, exit 0); a submit answer is confirmed only with a matching identity and an exact readback, otherwise `rejected` with the record kept. [A5]
- TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006 Stop-gate functions. AC-001 `Get-PluginStopGateTurns` (IO) and `Get-PluginStopGateFindings` (pure); AC-002 block/warn/pass output contract and exact block text; AC-003 auto-close code removed; AC-004 `duplicate_delivery_suspected` computed from the queue entries' `mintedAtUtc` and `promptSha256`; AC-005 `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` computed from `turn-state.yaml` `withdrawn` entries whose turn is still `in_progress`; AC-006 in a host session with `unsupportedSince`, whatever its reason, the findings count a turn's captured `unsupported_session` failsafe records, so a captured close turns its block into a warning naming the cause and the retained records; outside `unsupportedSince`, a model close, a withdrawn request's `failTurn`, or (from Phase C) the plugin's package-failure `canceled` close held in the ordinary failsafe by a retryable answer turns that turn's block into the degraded persistence warning, and its ledger entry stays until the server confirms the close (`StopGateOutageCloseQueuedWarnsDegradedLedgerKeptUntilConfirmed`); AC-007 every block remedy comes from `Get-CloseRemedy` and every evidence check from `Test-ModelEvidence` (architecture 3.5.6, in `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1`), the pass clears confirmed-closed ledger entries and a host-forced stop records `stop_gate_overridden_by_host`, each through `Update-TurnState` (one atomic replace under `turn-open.lock` that re-reads the current state), and the failed-build block reads `lastBuildStatus` from this host session's `turns/<requestId>.yaml` files. [A7]
- TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007 Builder lists and evidence counters. AC-001 `New-McpPluginTurnUpsertRequest -DesignDecisions -RequirementsDiscovered -Blockers`; AC-002 fingerprint keys; AC-003 `modelActions`/`modelDecisions` counters, and `Test-ModelEvidence` (architecture 3.5.6) counts model-authored actions, design decisions, and commits, including the turn's records captured in the failsafe and never a hook invocation, and is required before a `completed` or `failed` close and never for a `canceled` close; AC-004 `Invoke-WorkflowAppendDecisions` writes the C7 triple with a per-decision idempotency key scoped to the invocation, so a replay of that invocation adds nothing and a separate invocation adds a new triple (architecture 3.1, "Decision replay"). [A6]

#### Testing requirements

Inside a TEST row, an AC of a TEST record is written `<TESTID> AC###` with a space (for example `TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 AC006`), because the strict traceability run reads every `TEST-` token of `Testing-Requirements.md` as a TEST id (`build/TraceabilityValidator.cs:15-16,41-48,147` at `a8d3934b`) and a hyphenated `-AC###` suffix would be read as a record id missing from the matrix; the ownership and mapping lists, which P0 does not store as TEST text, keep the `<TESTID>-AC###` form. Every new case below is PascalCase (CA1707); an existing C# case is cited by its analyzer-branch name, and a Pester or host-repo case keeps its spelling.

- TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 Server submit outcomes, ingress mint, and turn write isolation. Tests: `SessionLogSubmitOutcomeTests`, `TerminalTurnOutcomeGuardContractTests` (the seam `ITerminalTurnOutcomeGuard`), `TurnWritePipelineTests` and `TurnStatusTests` (the section 3.5.3 components' one test class each, with their three-provider cases in G3), `SessionLogIngressMintTests`, `AddSessionLogTurnIngressNonceMigrationTests` (per provider), controller/MCP/client/REPL/decorator forwarding tests (with `SessionLogSanitizingServiceTests.MutationMethodsDelegateWithoutSanitizingInputs` extended to `SubmitWithOutcomesAsync` and `MintTurnAsync`), and the existing service tests named below. Validates FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC001..AC003 (server side), FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC001, -AC003, -AC006, -AC007, TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 AC-001..AC-007. Also validates updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC008, server side (case `SessionLogSubmitOutcomeTests.InProgressOntoCanceledTurnReportsSkippedTerminalActionsRemainZero`). Also contributes the server mint collisions, terminal merge, and mint isolation to FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC001 and -AC002 (owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001; cases `MinterCollisionReturnsDistinctId`, `MintCollisionConcurrentCrossSessionDistinctIds`, `MintSameNonceTerminalTurnMergedNotReopened`, and the mint-isolation cases `MintConcurrentWithOtherTurnWriteSameSessionBothCommitNoConflictAnswer` and `MintTransientConflictFourAttempts409ConflictRetryableNothingSaved`, architecture 3.1 "Mint isolation") and validates the review-section writes on terminal turns of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC003 (S4, case `SessionLogTurnReviewSectionTests.SectionPutTerminalTurnReportsSuccess`). Also validates the single-turn routes of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC001 (case `SingleTurnRoutesTerminalTurnStatusCorrectionTodayBodiesNoOutcomeGuard`). Also validates turn-level isolation, transactions, and the retry bound of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC006 and TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 AC-007 (cases `TurnWriteConcurrentDifferentTurnsSameSessionBothCommitNoConflictAnswer`, dense-session fixture on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server at SessionTurnLogging's gate, architecture 8.4; `TurnWriteSummaryRefreshFailureNeverFailsTurnWrite`; `TurnWriteUnitsMapSerializableWriteExhaustionTo409ConflictRetryable`; the rewritten `SessionLogDenseGraphTests.UpsertTurnAsyncSqlDeadlock1205DoesNotReportMissingSession` and `SessionLogSupersessionTests.SubmitAsyncConcurrentCompletionPreservesCompletedResponse`; and, kept green as the caller-owned transaction refusal and the single EF in-memory run, `SessionLogSupersessionTests.SubmitAsyncCallerTransactionRejectsBeforeMutation`, `SubmitAsyncAmbientTransactionRejectsBeforeMutation`, `SubmitAsyncPendingTrackedEditRejectsWithoutDiscardingEdit`, `SessionLogControllerReceiptTests.SubmitAsyncOuterRollbackNeverReturnsDurableReceipt`, and the in-memory suites of section "Test churn"). Also validates the agent-surface statuses of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007 (cases `SubmitClosedStatusRefused400FieldStatusNothingSaved`, `SingleTurnRoutesClosedOrUnknownStatusRefused400FieldStatus`, `SubmitAndSingleTurnRoutesLegacyCancelledAccepted`, `QBAgentTurnUpdateToolClosedStatusRefused400`, and, kept green because their `closed` rows reach the service with no handler, `SessionLogSupersessionTests.SubmitAsyncStaleCancellationPreservesTerminalTurn` and `SubmitAsyncStaleActiveSnapshotPreservesTerminalTurn`). Also validates updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC007 (case `DenseSessionSubmitWithinBudgetOrOne409ConflictRetryableNoPartialRows`, three providers), updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005-AC005 and TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005-AC004 (cases `UpsertInsertWhenAbsentRaceMergedIntoWinnerNoDuplicateChildren` and the re-fixtured `SessionLogServiceTests.UpsertTurnAsyncUniqueSessionLogTurnsRaceRetriesOnceAndUpdates`), updated TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004 AC001 and AC006 (the same race cases and the rewritten 1205 case), and updated TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-005 AC001 (the rewritten 1205 and concurrent-completion cases and, kept green, `McpErrorClassifierTests.ClassifySqliteBusyIsRetryablePersistenceError`).
- TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 Truthful plugin receipts and the failsafe drain. Tests: `SessionTurnIntegrity.Tests.ps1` Describes `Outcomes` and `Drain`; the rewritten drain cases of `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1`, `SessionLogLifecycle.Tests.ps1`, and `SessionLogQuarantineRepair.Tests.ps1` (section "Test churn"); `PluginSessionLogIntegration` case `AppendActionsToCanceledTurnReportsSkippedTerminalServerHasZeroActions`. Validates FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC002, -AC004, -AC005, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008 (drain through the persist path), TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 AC-001..AC-005. Also validates the retryable and final answers of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC002 and TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 AC-005 (cases `OutcomesRetryableAnswerQueuedFailsafeKeptExitZeroTurnStaysLedgered`, for a transport outage, 503 `backend_unavailable`, a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`, and 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`; `OutcomesFinalServerCodeLiveVerbClearsRecordExitOne`, which rewrites the `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1:3386` case; `DrainRetryableAnswerStopsWalkNoAttemptLaterRecordsKept`). Also validates updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC008, plugin side (case `OutcomesSkippedTerminalPersistedFalseExitNonZero`). Also validates updated TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC005 (case `OutcomesPerTurnMappingFromServerTurns`), TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 AC006 (case `OutcomesSkippedTerminalPersistedFalseExitNonZero`), and changed TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 AC002 (case `OutcomesSkippedTerminalPersistedFalseExitNonZero`; TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006 contributes `StopGateNeverCompletesOrFailsTurn`; ownership stated in the arrival domain). #### Updated existing requirements

Store facts: the updated records below carry no AC ids in the store (recon `req-ab.md` T4); an `AC###` named here is the ordinal of the record's existing AC list, and a record that has no ACs today (the TRs of the drain, TR-MCP-FAILSAFE-001, TR-MCP-PERSIST-003, TEST-MCP-REPL-037, TEST-MCP-FAILSAFE-001, TEST-MCP-195, TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-005, FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001, TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001) gets structured ACs in P0. Each changed or new AC below is owned by the TEST that section "AC ownership" or the bracket of its mapping row names, and that TEST's row in section "Testing requirements" names its RED case; an AC whose text is kept needs no new receipt.

- TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011 (TR:1625) "Stop gate no-ops phantom turns and counts commits". Changes: AC1 (system-event or zero-work in_progress turns are no-op'd) is replaced, because system events never open turns (TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013) and the stop gate now blocks on any real in_progress request turn and never calls completeTurn; the gate's "audit is incomplete" block becomes the close verbs' model-evidence refusal (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002); AC2 (commits count) kept. New ACs: TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC001 blocks on in_progress request turns [A7]; -AC002 never calls completeTurn/failTurn and the auto-close at `plugin-hook.ps1:1017` (at `a8d3934b`) is removed [A7]; -AC003 commits count toward model evidence (former AC2) [A7]. Mapping: FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005's row gains this TR.
- TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-011 (TEST:769). The "no-ops a task-notification phantom turn" case becomes "the prompt hook never opens it" (A3) and new cases "blocks a real in_progress turn" and "never completeTurn" (A7). New ACs: TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC001 [A3], owned by TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 (case `SystemEventsHookReportsSkippedNoMint`), and -AC002 [A7], owned by TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006. Mapping: FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005's row gains this TEST.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003 (FR:2092) "Three session-log persist outcomes". Changes: title becomes "Session-log persist outcomes"; AC001 enumerates four outcomes (primary persisted, degraded queued, failed/lost, and per-turn skipped_terminal); AC002 adds per-turn `turns[]` outcome fields; AC003 "honored only when it matches the active turn" becomes "honored when it identifies the current or a registered open turn"; statement "appendActions refuses a request-id mismatch" becomes "refuses an unknown requestId"; AC006 ("Write-ahead recovery is retained before backend submission and deleted only after durable confirmation") keeps its text, and its outcome now also holds for 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` (architecture 3.1, "Deadlock and busy outcomes", which names this ac6; its cases are FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC002's); AC007's "or returns one classified retryable outcome inside that budget" names that outcome: 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` once SerializableWrite's four attempts are spent inside the submit's storage budget, with no partial or duplicate child row (architecture 3.5.2) [A1]. New ACs: FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC008 skipped_terminal is reported as not persisted with non-zero exit [A1 server, A5 plugin]; -AC009 explicit requestId targets an open turn [A4].
- TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003 (TR:2706) "Wrapper three-outcome exit contract". Changes: title "Wrapper outcome exit contract"; AC001/AC002 add skipped_terminal and forbid persisted:true for a skipped turn; "appendActions honors requestId" becomes explicit target resolution; AC004 (a transient SQL Server deadlock or timeout bounded inside the storage budget, never primary success) keeps its text and is met by SerializableWrite's bound. New AC: TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC005 per-turn outcome mapping from server `turns[]` [A5].
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 (FR:2118) "Stop hook stale pin and session-log uniqueness". Changes: statement "runs failed-build and incomplete-audit gates first" becomes "runs the failed-build and stale-session blocks first; incomplete audit is the close verbs' model-evidence refusal (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002)", "the local request id is not the active turn" becomes "every open request turn", and "one bounded uniqueness retry" becomes "the insert-when-absent race merged into the winning row"; AC001 drops "auto-close" and "incomplete-audit" [A7]; AC002 "reconciled only by authoritative Completed proof" becomes "reconciled only by authoritative terminal proof from any surface for the exact workspace, agent, session, and request" (architecture 4.4, EvaluateStopGate: "this replaces the 'authoritative Completed proof' of FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 ac2"; what-if X-13; recon `req-ab.md` C13) [A7]; AC003's "completed or missing local files" are this host session's `turns/<requestId>.yaml` files and `turn-state.yaml` [A7]; AC005 "One bounded retry applies only to the known unique same-session/request race" becomes "The insert-when-absent race of a single-turn write is merged into the winning row by IdempotentCreate with policy `MergeInto` within SerializableWrite's bound (four attempts, then 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, nothing saved); the winner is reread and merged without duplicate children or loss of unrelated state, while unrelated failures and cancellation propagate" (architecture 4.2: "it replaces the one bounded retry of FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 ac5 and TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 ac4") [A1]. New ACs: FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005-AC006 the stop gate never closes a turn [A7]; -AC007 it blocks while a delivered request (the stop ledger, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003-AC004) is in_progress, blocks a withdrawn request still in_progress with `turn_withdrawn_unclosed`, and only warns for a minted request still `queued` although its server turn is in_progress (D5) [A7].
- TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 (TR:2730) "Stop-hook stale pin and upsert uniqueness". Changes: `Close-PluginTurnIfNeeded` blocks instead of closing; AC001's "exact-turn proof ... after build and audit gates" applies to every open turn, after the failed-build and stale-session blocks [A7]; AC002's "shared cache-state lock" is `turn-open.lock` through `Update-TurnState` (architecture 3.5.6): the gate loads state, computes findings with no IO, and clears only confirmed-closed ledger entries in one atomic replace that re-reads the current state [A7]; AC004 "Upsert retries once only for the known same-session/request unique race" becomes "the insert-when-absent race of every single-turn write (upsert, PATCH, PUT, complete, fail) is IdempotentCreate with policy `MergeInto`: a lost insert ends the attempt, SerializableWrite reruns the unit, and the lookup merges into the winning row without duplicate child collections; `DbErrorClassifier` replaces the message-text race predicate (`SessionLogService.cs:764-797,812-817` at `a8d3934b`)" [A1]. New AC: TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005-AC005 findings function is pure and IO is separated [A7].
- TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004 (TEST:1155). Changes: AC001's "the bounded same-turn unique race retry" becomes "the insert-when-absent race merged into the winning row (IdempotentCreate `MergeInto`)"; AC006's "one normalized retryable outcome" for the SQL Server row becomes "409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` after SerializableWrite's four attempts for error 1205, and 503 `backend_unavailable` with `retryable: true` for a command-budget expiry, which is never a transient conflict (`DbErrorClassifier`, architecture 3.5.2)" [A1].
