- FR-MCP-REPL-011 (FR:1956) "Plugin failsafe queue drains instead of accumulating" (named inline by architecture 3.1, "Failsafe drain", and 4.4, DrainFailsafe). Changes: the statement's "automatically on the first proven-reachable call and on demand through an explicit drain verb" becomes "automatically at each per-host-session trigger (SessionStart, and the next prompt hook or verb whose host session's failsafe holds drainable records) and on demand through the explicit drain verb, which follows the same rules", and "a record the backend rejects does not block the newer records behind it" becomes "a record the backend refuses with a final answer counts one drain attempt and holds back only the later records of its own turn"; AC001's "both automatic proven-reachable replay and explicit drain" becomes "every drain trigger" (the first-success trigger `Invoke-ReplFailsafeDrainOnFirstSuccess`, `repl-invoke.ps1:1188-1238` at `a8d3934b`, wired in `Invoke-ReplRaw` at `:652-655`, is removed; architecture: "this replaces the first-success trigger of FR-MCP-REPL-011 ac1") [A5]; AC002 "deleted only after the backend confirms durable persistence" becomes "deleted only after a confirmed answer for its exact workspace, agent, session, and request: a `persisted` receipt (with `outcomeVerified: false` included), `unchanged`, `skipped_terminal`, or a mint that returns a turn" [A5]; AC003 becomes "A final answer increments the record's `drainAttempts` and the walk continues, skipping only the later records bound to the same turn while that turn's mint or write is unresolved; a malformed record, a record at the attempt budget of 5 (0 again after a repair), a legacy record that names no request, a write bound to a pending id whose mint is quarantined, and an `unsupported_session` record whose turn is no longer in progress move to the quarantine subdirectory with a `.reason.txt`, are never deleted, and never replay from there" [A5]; AC005's second sentence becomes "A timeout abort leaves `drainAttempts` unchanged; no REPL call starts a nested drain, and there is no completion latch" [A5]. New ACs: FR-MCP-REPL-011-AC006 a retryable answer (a transport outage, 503 `backend_unavailable`, a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`, or 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`) stops the walk without counting an attempt and keeps that record and every later one [A5]; -AC007 a record with a captured binding replays through it (the idempotent session open for its envelope `mcpSessionId` first, then the mint or the write under the journaled alias, the bound id, or the original pair), a legacy record whose parameters name its session and request replays its method and parameters as today, and an `unsupported_session` record of a host session still live with `unsupportedSince` is not drained and counts no attempt [A5].
- TR-MCP-REPL-016 (TR:2406) "Failsafe queue drains oldest-first after a proven-reachable backend call" (named inline by the architecture with FR-MCP-REPL-011). Changes: title "Failsafe queue drains oldest-first at each per-host-session trigger"; the clause "The drain MUST be wired to the first successful Invoke-ReplRaw in the process (Invoke-ReplFailsafeDrainOnFirstSuccess, latched to run at most once and disabled by MCP_FAILSAFE_DRAIN_DISABLED=1)" is removed; "the drain increments that record's drainAttempts counter and continues the walk" gains "skipping only the later records bound to the same turn"; "A transport-level failure ... MUST abort the pass without consuming any attempt budget" covers every retryable answer; the in-flight skip (a record written by the submit in flight in the same process is not replayed by that process's drain, `repl-invoke.ps1:876-928,1094` at `a8d3934b`; architecture 3.1, "Drain in flight", and 4.4, DrainFailsafe) and the explicit `workflow.failsafe.drain` verb are kept. New ACs: TR-MCP-REPL-016-AC001 develop's `Invoke-ReplFailsafeDrain` (`repl-invoke.ps1:1027-1186`) is process DrainFailsafe, run oldest first by SessionStart, the prompt hook, a verb with drainable records, and the explicit verb, and never by `Invoke-ReplRaw`; -AC002 a final answer increments `drainAttempts`, keeps `lastDrainError`, and skips the later records of the same turn; -AC003 a retryable answer stops the walk with no attempt counted; -AC004 a bound record replays through `Invoke-SessionOpen`, then `Resolve-MintOutcome` as a stored retry for a mint or SendBoundWrite for a write (architecture 4.4, ReplayBoundRecord) [A5].
- TR-MCP-REPL-017 (TR:2413) "Failsafe quarantine and truthful pending queue depth in plugin status". Changes: the quarantine reasons gain three, each with its `.reason.txt`: a legacy record without a captured binding that names no request, a write bound to a pending id whose mint is quarantined, and an `unsupported_session` record whose turn is no longer in progress on the server; the queue location, status counts, and `workflow.failsafe.status` are kept. New ACs: TR-MCP-REPL-017-AC001 the three added reasons; -AC002 nothing replays from the quarantine subdirectory (only the counts read it, `mcp-status.ps1:85-88` and `repl-invoke.ps1:2864-2872` at `a8d3934b`) [A5].
- FR-MCP-FAILSAFE-001 (FR:1285) "Failsafe drain treats 503 backend_unavailable as backend-down and can retry" (named inline by the architecture). Changes: the statement's retryable set gains a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` and 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, and "After storage answers, a later drain in the same process can replay" becomes "the next drain trigger of that host session replays it"; AC002 "Test-ReplFailsafeBackendUnreachable treats HTTP 503 and code backend_unavailable as unreachable" becomes "the drain treats every retryable answer of `ErrorCodes` (C9) as an outage" [A5]; AC003 "Drain does not latch failed=1 as permanently completed in-process" becomes "No drain trigger latches completion; the first-success latch is removed" [A5].
- TR-MCP-FAILSAFE-001 (TR:966) "Failsafe drain abort markers include backend_unavailable and HTTP 503". Changes: the `Invoke-ReplFailsafeDrainOnFirstSuccess` clause is removed with the function. New ACs: TR-MCP-FAILSAFE-001-AC001 the drain's retryable classification reads the REPL envelope's `code` and `retryable` flag, so 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` abort like 503 `backend_unavailable`, with no `drainAttempts` increment and no quarantine; -AC002 no drain runs inside a REPL call and no completion latch exists [A5].
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004 (FR:2105) "Failsafe replay, child deadline, and contention classification" (named inline by the architecture). Changes: the statement's "Automatic and explicit drain do not latch completion on contention" becomes "No drain trigger latches completion; contention (409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` inside SerializableWrite, 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` outside it) stays retryable"; the repair and replay of quarantine files 3358, d5f9, and 9ad7 even when `drainAttempts` is exhausted is kept (the drain repairs each record before the budget check, `Repair-ReplFailsafeQueuedRecord`, `repl-invoke.ps1:997-1025,1115,1133-1135` at `a8d3934b`); AC004's "both drain entry points retain retryable work without consuming attempts or latching completion" becomes "every drain trigger (SessionStart, the hook or verb trigger, and the explicit verb) retains retryable work without consuming attempts and later replays it exactly once; there is no completion latch" [A5].
- TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004 (TR:2718) "Quarantine repair, import recovery, and contention drain". Changes: AC002 "Both drain entry points use one contention classifier and identical attempt, latch, ordering, and delete-after-durable-success rules" becomes "Every drain trigger runs process DrainFailsafe with one retryable classification (the `ErrorCodes` retryable flag) and identical attempt, ordering, same-turn skip, quarantine, and delete-after-confirmed-answer rules, with no latch" [A5].
- FR-MCP-171 (FR:1199) "Retryable session-log persist degrade-queue" and TR-MCP-PERSIST-002 (TR:1553) "PersistTurn maps 503 to timeout degrade-queue": the arrival domain's entries carry their `current-turn.yaml` change (FR-MCP-171-AC001, TR-MCP-PERSIST-002-AC002) and leave the retryable answer set to this domain (`20-turn-arrival.md`, FR-MCP-171 entry). Changes: FR-MCP-171's "returns timeout, HTTP 503, or backend_unavailable" and TR-MCP-PERSIST-002's "treat HTTP 503 and payload code backend_unavailable the same as timeout" extend to the retryable set of architecture 4.4, PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt: a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` and 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`. New ACs: FR-MCP-171-AC002 every retryable answer produces the same degrade-queue contract (`persisted: false`, degraded, `queued`, failsafe retained, exit 0) [A5]; TR-MCP-PERSIST-002-AC003 `Invoke-ReplPersistTurn` reads the REPL envelope's `code` and `retryable` flag (`ErrorCodes`, C9) to choose the degrade-queue path [A5].
- FR-MCP-172 (FR:1209) "Failsafe drain does not poison successful plugin calls" and TR-MCP-PERSIST-003 (TR:1564) "Failsafe drain inspects errors without Write-Error Stop skip". Both state the first-success drain inside a successful plugin call, which the architecture removes and now names with them (4.4, DrainFailsafe, Trigger: "this replaces the first-success trigger ... and the same statement in FR-MCP-172, TR-MCP-PERSIST-003, TR-MCP-FAILSAFE-001, TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004, TEST-MCP-FAILSAFE-001, TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005, and TEST-MCP-195"; 3.1, "Drain in flight"). Changes: FR-MCP-172's statement "SHALL NOT latch drain completed, and a later drain after storage answers SHALL replay" becomes "starts no drain, and a later drain trigger replays the queued record"; AC001 and AC002 hold, because no REPL call starts a drain; AC003 "ReplFailsafeDrainCompleted stays false on abort" becomes "there is no drain completion latch"; AC004 "Next drain in-process replays after a stubbed success" becomes "the next drain trigger of the host session replays after a stubbed success" [A5]. TR-MCP-PERSIST-003's latch clause is removed. New ACs: TR-MCP-PERSIST-003-AC001 no REPL call starts a drain, so a successful getFr, getTr, or queryHistory never waits on one; -AC002 the drain inspects `Invoke-ReplRaw` failures without the Write-Error Stop skip (kept) [A5].
- TEST-MCP-REPL-037 (TEST:1060), TEST-MCP-FAILSAFE-001 (TEST:623), TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 (TEST:1164), and TEST-MCP-195 (TEST:542). Changes: TEST-MCP-REPL-037 (named inline by the architecture) asserts that no `Invoke-ReplRaw` call starts a drain and that a verb whose host session's failsafe holds drainable records drains before its own send, instead of the once-per-process first-success latch; TEST-MCP-FAILSAFE-001 adds 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` to its no-attempt aborts and replays through the next trigger instead of a second first-success call; TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005 AC001 "automatic nested drain defers while a REPL call is active, and timeout leaves attempt and latch state unchanged" becomes "no REPL call starts a nested drain, and a timeout leaves `drainAttempts` unchanged" (its AC003 holds); TEST-MCP-195's items (3) and (4) lose the latch and the getFr-time drain. New ACs: TEST-MCP-REPL-037-AC001, TEST-MCP-FAILSAFE-001-AC001, TEST-MCP-195-AC003 and -AC004 (structured from its items (3) and (4)) [A5].
- FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (FR:1268) "Shared clear-session reset skill for all agent plugins". The store holds no AC for it (`acceptanceCriteria: []`), so the earlier "inline AC1" named a clause that exists only in TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (recon `req-ab.md`). Changes: the statement's step "(1) end the current MCP session" becomes "(1) finalize each open request turn of the host session by its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair per the clear-session closing rule, then end the current MCP session", and "All QBrain.AI agent plugins" is the nine plugins (grok-bot added, D8). New ACs: FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001 before it ends the MCP session, clear-session finalizes each open request turn of the host session (a delivered request still open, a withdrawn entry still in progress, and a minted request still `queued`) by its original pair: failed when withdrawn or still queued (reason "withdrawn before delivery") or marked blocked (its first blocker as the reason), completed when it meets the completion policy and has model evidence, otherwise left in progress and reported with its exact missing items; a turn the server already reports terminal is reported closed elsewhere with its `storedStatus` and nothing is sent; no review is ever fabricated (architecture 3.1, clear-session closing rule, and 5.4, ClearSession) [S8 +S9 +S10]; -AC002 every one of the nine plugins provides the same clear-session skill [S9].
- TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (TR:661) "clear-session skill content contract". Changes: inline AC1 "end the session by finalizing the open turn via workflow.sessionlog.completeTurn" becomes "end the session by finalizing each open request turn per TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001 (completeTurn, failTurn, or left in progress)" [S8 coordinator +S9 skill text], and "byte-identical in all 8 agent plugin repositories" becomes all nine [S9]. Phasing: S8 ships the PowerShell clear-session coordinator runtime in the synced plugin core with its PowerShell RED cases and edits no skill text; because `SyncAgentPlugins` does not copy this skill, S9, once the TypeScript hosts reach the S8 coordinator through their adapters, edits the clear-session skill text in all nine plugin repos at once (the six PowerShell hosts and cline, cline-v2, opencode). Its evidence is application evidence, so it runs in application suites, never in Build.Tests, which is infrastructure and evidences only TR-MCP-NUKE TRs (architecture 3.1, "Test placement"): each repo's `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1` case `ClearSessionSkillListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (extending today's clear-session case, `mcpserver-claude-code-plugin/tests/Skills.Tests.ps1:29-33` at the repo's current head) runs in G6 through `./build.ps1 TestAgentPlugins`, and the PluginIntegration case `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionAndGateSteps` reads `skills/clear-session/SKILL.md` from the nine repos of the scenario catalog under the sibling plugin parent (`PluginSessionLogCatalog.SiblingPluginParent`, `tests/QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests/PluginSessionLogCatalog.cs:36-57` at `a8d3934b`, which resolves `/home/sharpninja/github` from a worktree) and runs in G3 through `./build.ps1 PluginSessionLogIntegration`; it fails, never skips, when fewer than nine repos are found. This replaces the earlier Build.Tests hosting of `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionSteps` and `ClearSessionSkillListsGateSteps`, its direct test-runner step outside Nuke, and its `MCP_AGENT_PLUGIN_PARENT` hand-off to `ResolveAgentPluginParentDirectory`. New ACs: TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001 clear-session selects with the shared completion policy before any close, so it holds before S10, for each open request turn addressed by its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair: a delivered request still open, a `withdrawn` entry still in progress, and a minted request still `queued` (architecture 5.4, ClearSession; what-if Q-9). A turn the server already reports terminal from any surface is reported closed elsewhere with its `storedStatus`, and nothing is sent. Otherwise, in this precedence: a withdrawn turn, or a minted request still `queued`, is failed (reason "withdrawn before delivery"); else a turn marked blocked is failed with its blocker reason (blocked takes precedence over a ready completion); else a turn whose completion gate and model-evidence check (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002) are met is completed; else it stays in progress, reported by requestId with its exact missing items; `failed` requires `requestClass` only, and no review is ever fabricated. "Marked blocked" means the turn's `blockers` section (the existing decision-list field, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC001) holds at least one nonblank model-authored entry, read by the coordinator for that turn by its original pair; the first entry is the fail reason (operator option A, 2026-10-10). Before failing a withdrawn or blocked turn, the coordinator checks its close prerequisites in the Stop gate remedy order (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC009): model evidence (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002) and a `requestClass` that is neither missing nor `Unclassified`. A turn that lacks one is not sent to `failTurn`: the coordinator reports it by requestId with its `queryText`, the reason it will be failed with, and each missing prerequisite; within the same clear-session run, before its session-end step, the model writes them by the original pair (a designDecision, then the class, Code, Docs, or Chore, judged from that `queryText`), and the coordinator runs again, reads them back by that pair, and fails the turn with its reason, so neither the plugin's evidence check nor the server gate refuses a clear-session fail. In a host session with `unsupportedSince`, `Get-CloseRemedy` counts the turn's captured `unsupported_session` records as written, as the Stop gate does, and a captured close is reported as captured for the next SessionStart drain (what-if W-16). Each close goes through processes BindVerbToTurn and PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt by the original pair, and its receipt is reported (nested activity ReportCloseReceipt): `persisted` as closed, `skipped_terminal` as closed elsewhere with its `storedStatus`, `outcomeVerified: false` as unverified and not counted closed (the coordinator is rerun), a retryable answer (a transport outage, 503 `backend_unavailable`, a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`, or 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`) as queued in the failsafe and drained later, a captured close as captured, and a refusal or another server code as left in progress with that code (and, for `session_turn_completion_gate`, the server's missing items) [S8 +S9 +S10]; TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC002 the clear-session skill text is byte-identical in all nine plugin repos, PowerShell only, and lists the selection and gate steps, evidenced by TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 through `ClearSessionSkillListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (G6) and `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (G3) [S9; S11 re-runs it].
- TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (TEST:612). Changes: the statement, which names the claude-code repo's `Skills.Tests.ps1` five-step case, becomes "Validates FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC002 and TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC002 with each of the nine plugin repos' `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1` case `ClearSessionSkillListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (G6, `./build.ps1 TestAgentPlugins`) and the PluginIntegration case `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (G3, `./build.ps1 PluginSessionLogIntegration`)", and the record carries the three ACs of the new-TEST rule [S9; S11 re-runs it]. It leaves the catalog's "Related, unchanged" list.

- `SessionLogLifecycle.Tests.ps1:314,347` and `SessionLogQuarantineRepair.Tests.ps1:20,52` keep their outcome (the drain still repairs and replays the exhausted numeric-keyed records; architecture 4.4, DrainFailsafe, "Repair, as today"); `SessionLogLifecycle.Tests.ps1:386` and `SessionLogQuarantineRepair.Tests.ps1:90` keep a locked answer retryable with no attempt, run their second pass through the explicit drain verb instead of `Invoke-ReplFailsafeDrainOnFirstSuccess` (`:421`; `:123`), drop their `ReplFailsafeDrainCompleted` latch assertions (`:397,413`; `:101,117`), and take as their locked answer the REPL envelope a busy store returns (503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`; a reading the coordinator accepted on 2026-10-13).

- Receipt enumerations `SessionLogP2Contracts.Tests.ps1:385` and `SessionLogReviewRegression.Tests.ps1:127,188` keep their rows (develop's dispositions `primary`, `queued`, `lost`, `unchanged`, `rejected` already emit the codes `persisted`, `queued`, `lost`, `unchanged`, `rejected`, `repl-invoke.ps1:1368-1400`) and gain rows for `skipped_terminal`, `turn_missing_model_evidence`, and the retryable and final server codes.
- Stop gate (owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006): the auto-close case `SessionLogP2Contracts.Tests.ps1:1398` asserts that the gate never closes; the stale-session cases `SessionLogLifecycleMetadata.Tests.ps1:207,242` move to the per-host-session store; in the claude-code plugin repo at its current head `472340b`, `tests/StopGateHardening.Tests.ps1:42` (a completed turn whose only evidence is commits is not blocked) keeps its outcome re-seeded through the per-turn store (TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC003), and `:65` (task-notification phantom turn) becomes "the prompt hook never opens it" (TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC001, owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001) with the gate never calling `completeTurn`. The failed-build case `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1:2512` (TEST-MCP-BUGTRIAGE-068) is rewritten in the arrival domain's churn; its block is FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC007's.

Plugin repos, HostPluginAdoption (G6, `./build.ps1 TestAgentPlugins`):
- `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1:29-33` in the six PowerShell-host repos (at their current heads; it asserts `workflow.sessionlog.completeTurn` in the clear-session skill) becomes `ClearSessionSkillListsSelectionAndGateSteps`, and the three TypeScript hosts gain the case with their Pester suites. Owner: TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001.

### Domain: Request class and the completion gate

Bounded contexts (architecture 8.3): TurnReviews (policies RequestClassification, CompletionPolicy, and CompletionGate, the request-class normalizer, and the CompletionGate enforcement change set, which merges and deploys after the plugin sync and HostPluginAdoption, architecture 8.5), SessionTurnLogging (`TurnWritePipeline`, `TurnStatus`, and the write-origin scope in `SessionLogService.cs`), ReplAndAgents (the REPL and McpAgent advisory pre-checks, REPL `importRecovery`'s report of the turns the gate refuses, and the optional arguments of QBAgent's `mcp_session_turn_complete`), StorageSchema (the `RequestClass` column, its database default, and the S3 backfill), and PluginCorePowerShell (the FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 plugin verbs and `Get-CloseRemedy`). The SharedComponents components this domain uses (`SerializableWrite`, `ClassifiedErrorException`, and the one `ClassifiedErrorException` branch of `McpErrorClassifier`, architecture 3.5.2) are called through their Phase 2 seams and owned there. Use cases: UC-4 and UC-5 (architecture 7.5 and 7.6).

#### Functional requirements

FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001 Request classification.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC001 `requestClass` accepts `Code`, `Docs`, `Chore` case-insensitively and returns PascalCase. [S1]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC002 Other values, empty strings, and `Unclassified` from agent surfaces are rejected with 400 `request_class_invalid` (policy RequestClassification, architecture 5.3), and nothing of that write is saved. [S1 +S5]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC003 An omitted value keeps the stored value on additive writes; PUT replace resets it. [S4]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC004 The migration backfills every existing row with `Unclassified`, soft-deleted rows included, so a soft-deleted row reads `Unclassified` after it is revived (`BackfillSoftDeletedRowsUnclassifiedAndUnstartedAfterRevive`). The column's database default is `Unclassified`, so a turn the prior binary inserts during a binary-only rollback reads `Unclassified` after the roll-forward, which runs no second backfill (`RollbackPriorBinaryInsertsTurnRollForwardReadsDefaults`, architecture 5.1 and 8.5); a turn the new binary inserts keeps a null class until it is classified. [S3]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC005 Turns can be created and updated without a class. [S4]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC006 The field is readable and writable on REST, MCP, client, REPL, and McpAgent, and writable through QBAgent's `mcp_session_turn_complete` (TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 AC-004). Every read surface returns it through `TurnReadProjection` (architecture 3.5.3). MCP `sessionlog_complete_turn` and `sessionlog_fail_turn` copy `requestClass` from `turnJson` in addition to the fields `FinalizeLifecycleTurnToolAsync` copies today, and copy no review (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/McpStdio/FwhMcpTools.SessionLog.cs:328-369` at `a8d3934b`; architecture 4.5, CompleteSessionTurnCommand sequence). [S5 +S6 +S7]

FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002 Completion gate.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC001 `completed` requires `requestClass`, `agentReview` in Pass/Fail/Other, and for Code/Docs `hostileReview` in Pass/Fail/Other; for Chore `hostileReview` is not checked. For the gate `Unclassified` counts as missing, and a missing class is not `Chore`, so a `completed` write with no class also lists `hostileReview` and following the missing items literally passes whichever class the model then sets (policy CompletionPolicy, architecture 5.3; `CompletionPolicyMissingClassRequiresHostileReviewAsNonChore`). The gate evaluates the turn as it will be stored after the same write's review records (policy ReviewValidation), class change, and Chore flip (policy RequestClassification), in that order inside `TurnWritePipeline` (architecture 3.5.3), so one write carrying `requestClass`, terminal reviews, and `completed` succeeds. [S1 policy, S10 enforcement]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC002 `failed` requires `requestClass` only. [S1, S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC003 The gate applies on every surface: submit, upsert, PATCH, PUT, complete, fail, MCP tools, REPL, McpAgent, and QBAgent's internal session-turn tools (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC004); the plugin, REPL-workflow, and McpAgent verbs close through submit (SubmitSessionLogCommand, architecture 4.2). [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC004 It fires only on a write that moves the turn into `completed` or `failed`, or on a PUT whose replacement carries `completed` or `failed` even when the stored status already equals it (a whole replacement is not enrichment, architecture 4.2). A write that only enriches an already terminal turn is not re-gated, including a requestClass-only change that leaves a terminal turn failing the completion policy (for example `Chore` to `Code` with hostile `Unnecessary`), which is accepted and changes no review (`ClassOnlyChangeOnCompletedTurnLeavesPolicyUnmetAcceptedNotRegated`; architecture 3.1, round-5 resolutions). A transition into `canceled` or the legacy `cancelled` is not a completion and is not gated, a deliberate difference from the QBAgent evidence gate (`TurnStatus.IsTerminal`, develop's `IsTerminalTurnStatus`, counts both), so that client-supplied canceled persists (FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001) and the plugin's own `canceled` close of a failed Phase C invocation (architecture 6.6) keep working; it never satisfies the gate, and the Stop gate warns a canceled turn as model-canceled. A status outside the agent write set (`closed` included) is refused with 400 `validation_error`, `details.field` `status`, before the gate, by the agent status rule that FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007 owns (turn-persistence domain; `TurnStatus`, architecture 3.5.3), so `closed` never bypasses the gate (UC-5 alternate flow E5). [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC005 A rejection is atomic and leaves the data context reusable: the gate check and the save run as one `SerializableWrite` delegate, which rolls the rejected write back, so nothing of it is saved (architecture 3.4, Write isolation, and 3.5.2). A submission in which any turn fails the gate is rejected as a whole, after every turn is evaluated, with 400 `session_turn_completion_gate` naming each failing requestId with its missing items, and no turn of that submission is persisted. A concurrent review reset, class change (a Chore flip included), review write, or close of the same turn commits wholly before or after the gated write, never between its gate check and its save (`TurnWriteForcedInterleavingReviewResetVsCompleteNeverClosedWithoutReview`, `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingClassChangeVsCompleteGateSeesCommittedClass`, each on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server at the gate of the CompletionGate enforcement change set, architecture 8.4). When `SerializableWrite`'s default bound (four attempts) is exhausted, the gated write answers 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and saves nothing (`GatedCloseWriteBoundExhausted409ConflictRetryableNothingSaved`, the unit mapping the component's exhaustion exit through its Phase 2 seam; the component's own case belongs to SharedComponents). [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC006 The error is 400 `validation_error` with `retryable: false`, its message starting with the token `session_turn_completion_gate` and listing the missing items in fixed order (requestClass, agentReview, hostileReview). The handler fails with `ClassifiedErrorException` (architecture 3.5.2); REST returns the `ClassifiedError` problem+json body and MCP the classified error object through the one `ClassifiedErrorException` branch of `McpErrorClassifier`, with no gate-specific branch (`CompletionGateRefusalBodyTokenAndMissingItemsInOrderRestAndMcp`). [S1 message, S5 surface mapping, S10]

FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003 Exempt write origins.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC001 Import (`SessionLogIngestor`, `TranscriptSessionLogPersister`), ServerInternal (`AuditedAgentCliClient`, `WorkspacePolicyService`, `QuadBrainOpenAiChatService`), and Replication (federation apply) origins bypass the gate (policy CompletionGate, architecture 5.3). [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC002 ServerInternal writers stamp `Chore`; Import and Replication stamp `Unclassified` when no class arrives; no review is ever fabricated. Import and Replication store a supplied review record exactly as received (status and stamped thresholds, not re-derived) after the same per-status and score validation as Agent writes (policy ReviewValidation, architecture 5.3); an invalid record is rejected with its `review_*` code. [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC003 No REST, MCP, REPL, client, or plugin code can select a non-Agent origin (architecture test). [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC004 QBAgent's internal session-turn tools are gated Agent-origin writers: `mcp_session_turn_update` and `mcp_session_turn_complete` call `ISessionLogService.UpsertTurnAsync` directly (`QuadBrainInternalToolExecutor.cs:299-328` at `a8d3934b`), which applies the agent status rule (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007, whose owning TEST holds the QBAgent `closed` case `QBAgentTurnUpdateToolClosedStatusRefused400`) and the gate to them. A `mcp_session_turn_complete` call without a class or terminal reviews fails with the gate's refusal and stores nothing, and one that carries its optional `requestClass`, `agentReview`, and `hostileReview` arguments (TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 AC-004) completes (`QbAgentTurnCompleteToolWithoutClassOrReviewsRefusedWithThemCompletes`; architecture 3.1, "Completion gate writers"). [S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC005 REPL `importRecovery` (`SessionLogWorkflow.ImportRecoveryAsync`, `src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/SessionLogWorkflow.cs:326-365` at `a8d3934b`) is gated: it submits the merged session through SubmitSessionLogCommand (`:361`), so a gate refusal saves nothing of that submission; its import result reports each recovered turn the gate refuses with the gate's missing items, and it never writes a fabricated review (`ImportRecoveryGateRefusedTurnsReportedWithMissingItemsNothingSavedNoReviewFabricated`). Its status normalization stays as today (`:507-509,725-750`: `completed-local` and `partial` become `completed`, `interrupted` becomes `failed`), so the one normalized value outside the agent write set, `superseded` (`:738-741`), reaches the server and is refused with 400 `validation_error`, `details.field` `status` (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004-AC007), which the REPL surfaces unchanged, because the REPL classifier carries a server `ClassifiedError` through at every status (architecture 3.5.2, SharedComponents' case `ReplClassifierServerClassifiedErrorKeepsCodeRetryableDetailsEveryStatus`) (`ImportRecoverySupersededKeptByNormalizationServerRefusalSurfaced`; architecture 3.1, "Completion gate writers", and 5.3). [S10]

FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 Plugin class and gate surfaces.
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC001 Plugin verbs accept `requestClass`. [S8]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC002 The local pre-check is advisory: before the close is sent it computes the missing completion items with the exact gate message (the golden-contract rules) and reports them as a warning; it never refuses a close. A class or review recorded by another process, host session, or surface before the completion is honored and is not reported missing. The server gate (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002, S10) is the only completion authority, so nothing refuses a close that a pre-S10 or rolled-back server would accept, and no activation signal is needed: the gate adds no marker field, and nothing of FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 changes the marker or its signature (the contract digest and signature values change only through FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-008-AC005's nine `agent_plugins` contracts, with the signed field list of TR-MCP-SEC-005 unchanged, in the plugin-hosts domain). When the server refuses a close with 400 `session_turn_completion_gate`, the surface reports the server's message and missing items, the turn stays in progress, and AC003 applies (`PrecheckAdvisoryWarnsMissingItemsNeverRefuses`). Unsupported session (operator option 1, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC001): for a turn of that host session still in progress when `unsupportedSince` was recorded (a delivered open request, or a `withdrawn` queue entry still `in_progress`, addressed by its original pair; a minted request still `queued` is not covered and stays a warning), the close goes to the `unsupported_session` failsafe with no server write per FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC001 (the advisory warning still prints), and the server gate judges it at the drain after its host session ends or its cause is cleared (architecture 4.5, close sequence); the model-evidence check (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002) still applies. This is plugin-core state only; the McpAgent pre-check of TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 has no host session (`PrecheckUnsupportedSessionCodeTurnMissingReviewsCloseCapturedNoServerWrite`). [S8 +S10]
- FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC003 A close that carries actions, decisions, requirements, or blockers is sent as two writes: the carried evidence first, as an additive write that changes no status (so the completion gate never applies to it, FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC004) and is verified by readback against the sanitized read projection (GetSessionLogQuery through `TurnReadProjection`, architecture 3.1 and 5.2), then the status transition. If the evidence write is refused with a final code (a server refusal, or `transaction_degraded` or `mutation_not_transactional` from the REPL's client mutation policy), the transition is not sent, the turn stays in progress, and the receipt carries that code with `persisted: false`, exit 1 (`CloseWithEvidenceEvidenceWriteRefusedFinalCodeTransitionNotSentTurnStaysOpen` against a server double in S8, named in the owner TEST-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-005). If it is answered with a retryable answer (a transport outage, 503 `backend_unavailable`, a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`, or 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`), the failsafe keeps two records in order, the evidence write and then the close, the receipt is `queued` with exit 0, and the Stop ledger keeps the request until the drain confirms the close (architecture 4.5, close sequence). The failsafe keeps each part until that part is verified on the server, so a crash between them replays only what is missing and replay is idempotent. A server gate rejection of the transition therefore loses no evidence: it clears only the transition part, leaves the turn in progress, and reports the refusal with the server's missing items (`CloseWithEvidenceEvidenceWriteVerifiedBeforeTransitionGateRefusalLosesNothing` against a server double in S8; `RealGateRejectedCloseEvidenceSurvivesRestartReadBackEveryField` against the real gate in S10). This includes a drained `unsupported_session` close: its `session_turn_completion_gate` rejection clears that failsafe record and keeps the turn in progress and listed with its original pair. The close's carried writes follow this rule until its reviews are recorded through the C5 pair and a later close is accepted (`UnsupportedSessionCodeTurnDrainedCloseRejectedByGateStaysListedReviewViaPairThenCloses`, run against the real gate in S10). [S8 +S10]

#### Technical requirements

File layout for every TR below: each new type is one file (MA0048), and its namespace is its project's RootNamespace followed by its folder (architecture 3.5 and 3.1, "Analyzers first, adopt rules"); a seam that the server, the REPL, and McpAgent all use lives in `QBrainAi.Client` (architecture 3.5).

- TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001 requestClass column, normalizer, backfill. AC-001 `RequestClass` string(16), indexed, nullable (null until the turn is classified), with the database default `Unclassified` that the binary-only rollback relies on (architecture 5.1 and 8.5; StorageSchema's registered model change and migrations); AC-002 `IRequestClassNormalizer`, the S1 seam that policy RequestClassification calls (implementation owned by TurnReviews, architecture 8.3); AC-003 backfill SQL per provider over every existing row, soft-deleted rows included (architecture 5.1). [S1 +S3 +S4]
- TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002 Completion policy and gate. AC-001 policy CompletionPolicy, the S1 completion-policy seam (`CompletionPolicyContractTests`) in `QBrainAi.Client` with one C# implementation used by the server, the REPL, and McpAgent, and its language-boundary copy `Get-CloseRemedy` in the PowerShell core, both run against the golden contract `docs/context/session-turn-review-contract.json` (architecture 5.3); AC-002 policy CompletionGate (architecture 5.3), applied by `TurnWritePipeline` to the turn as the write will store it, after policies ReviewValidation and RequestClassification and before the QBAgent terminal compliance check `ValidateTerminalTurnCompliance` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/SessionLogService.cs:1329` at `a8d3934b`), which runs only in the single-turn modes and only once the gate is met; in `Submit` mode the pipeline returns each turn's missing items and SubmitSessionLogCommand rejects once after every turn is evaluated (architecture 3.5.3 and 4.2); AC-003 the refusal is `ClassifiedErrorException` (`QBrainAi.Client.Errors`) built from the S1 gate message builder (400, `validation_error`, `retryable: false`, the token and the missing items in order), mapped by the one `ClassifiedErrorException` branch of `McpErrorClassifier` with no gate-specific branch (architecture 3.5.2); AC-004 the gate check and its save are one `SerializableWrite` delegate, which rolls a rejected write back, so nothing of it is saved and the data context stays reusable (architecture 5.3, policy CompletionGate). [S1 +S10]
- TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003 Write origins. AC-001 `WriteOrigin` and `IWriteOriginAccessor` (AsyncLocal scope), the write-origin scope SessionTurnLogging adds to `SessionLogService.cs` (architecture 8.3); AC-002 exempt writers wrap their calls: ServerInternal `AuditedAgentCliClient`, `WorkspacePolicyService`, and `QuadBrainOpenAiChatService`, Replication the federation apply, Import `SessionLogIngestor` and `TranscriptSessionLogPersister` (architecture 5.3); every other writer, QBAgent's internal session-turn tools and REPL `importRecovery` included, runs with the default origin Agent and is gated; AC-003 architecture test with exact allow-list. [S10]
- TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 McpAgent and QBAgent class and review fields, and the McpAgent pre-check. AC-001 request types carry class and reviews, `SessionLogTurnCompleteRequest` included (`src/QBrainAi.McpAgent/SessionLog/SessionLogTurnCompleteRequest.cs:7` at `a8d3934b`, the payload QBAgent's `mcp_session_turn_complete` publishes, `src/QBrainAi.McpAgent/Hosting/McpHostedAgentToolAdapter.cs:67-69`); AC-002 `RecordTurnReviewAsync`; AC-003 policy pre-check (policy CompletionPolicy, process AdvisoryCompletionPrecheck, architecture 5.4), advisory like the plugin pre-check: it reports the missing items with the exact gate message as a warning and never refuses a close; a server gate refusal is surfaced with the server's message and missing items (`McpAgentPrecheckAdvisoryWarnsMissingItemsNeverRefuses`); AC-004 QBAgent's `mcp_session_turn_complete`, executed by `QuadBrainInternalToolExecutor.SessionTurnCompleteAsync` (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Services/QuadBrainInternalToolExecutor.cs:314-328` at `a8d3934b`; declared at `McpHostedAgentToolAdapter.cs:68` and `src/QBrainAi.McpAgent/QBAgentDefinition.cs:15`), accepts the optional `requestClass`, `agentReview`, and `hostileReview` arguments of CompleteSessionTurnCommand and passes them to the turn write unchanged as the Phase B fields of `UnifiedRequestEntryDto`, which it already deserializes its arguments as (`:323`; inference that the added fields carry them, as architecture 5.3 states), and it never fabricates a review (`QbAgentTurnCompleteToolForwardsClassAndReviewsArgumentsUnchanged`; architecture 3.1, "Completion gate writers", which places this tool under this TR). [S7]

#### Testing requirements

- TEST-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001 Request class normalization, storage, and surfaces. Tests: `RequestClassNormalizerContractTests` (S1), `AddSessionLogTurnReviewsAndRequestClassMigrationTests` backfill cases (S3), `SessionLogRequestClassServiceTests` (S4), `SessionLogRequestClassSurfaceTests` (REST, MCP, and client read and write `requestClass`, S5), `ReplRequestClassReadWriteOnTurnVerbs` (S6). Validates FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001-AC001..AC006 (McpAgent and QBAgent-tool surfaces of AC006 via TEST-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004), TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001 AC-001..AC-003. AC004 by the S3 cases `BackfillSoftDeletedRowsUnclassifiedAndUnstartedAfterRevive` and `RollbackPriorBinaryInsertsTurnRollForwardReadsDefaults`, each on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server; AC006's MCP lifecycle-tool copy by the S5 case `McpCompleteAndFailToolsCopyRequestClassFromTurnJsonNoReview`.
- TEST-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002 Completion policy and gate. Tests: `CompletionPolicyContractTests` (S1), `CompletionGateContractTests`, `CompletionGateServiceTests` surface table (S10), the gate refusal's surface mapping through the one `ClassifiedErrorException` branch of `McpErrorClassifier` (S5), the forced-interleaving cases on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server at the gate of the CompletionGate enforcement change set (S10), and the eight develop tests the gate reverses, rewritten to the gated outcome (S10, `#### Test churn`). Validates FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC001..AC006, TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002 AC-001..AC-004 (AC001 also by case `CompletionPolicyMissingClassRequiresHostileReviewAsNonChore`; AC004 by `ClassOnlyChangeOnCompletedTurnLeavesPolicyUnmetAcceptedNotRegated` and `ReplaceCompletedTurnOmittingReviewsRefusedCompletionGateReviewsKept`; AC005 by `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingReviewResetVsCompleteNeverClosedWithoutReview`, `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingClassChangeVsCompleteGateSeesCommittedClass`, and `GatedCloseWriteBoundExhausted409ConflictRetryableNothingSaved`; AC006 by `CompletionGateRefusalBodyTokenAndMissingItemsInOrderRestAndMcp`). Also validates updated FR-SUPPORT-013 ac-5 (cases `CompletionGateServiceTests.StandardAgentCompleteWithoutClassRejected`, the rewritten `SessionLogServiceTests.UpsertTurnAsyncStandardAgentClosingTurnWithoutEvidenceSucceeds`, and the rewritten integration `SessionLogControllerTests.WhenStandardAgentClosingTurnWithoutComplianceItemsThenSucceeds`) and FR-SUPPORT-014 ac-4 (cases `CompletionGateServiceTests.LifecycleCompleteAndFailEnforceGate` and the rewritten integration `SessionLogControllerTests.CompleteTurnWithEvidenceFinalizesTurn` and `SessionLogControllerTests.FailTurnSetsFailedStatusWithNote`). Also validates the submit path of FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002-AC003 and -AC005, the route the plugin, REPL-workflow, and McpAgent verbs close through (cases `CompletionGateServiceTests.SubmitTransitionWithoutClassOrReviewsRejectedAtomicallyWholeSubmission`, `CompletionGateServiceTests.SubmitTwoTurnsFailGateErrorNamesEachRequestIdNothingPersisted`). Also validates the unsupported-session drain against the real gate (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC001, FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC003; cases `UnsupportedSessionCodeTurnDrainedCloseRejectedByGateStaysListedReviewViaPairThenCloses`). Also validates the accumulated Stop remedy of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003 (case `StopGateCodeTurnMissingEvidenceAndReviewsAccumulatedRemedyNextStopPasses`, against a server double in S8 and the real gate in S10). Also validates updated TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-003-AC001 (cases `CompletionGateServiceTests.EvidenceGateQbAgentOnlyStandardAgentNoEvidenceItemsCompletesWithClassAndReviews`, the rewritten `SessionLogServiceTests.UpsertTurnAsyncAcidAgentClosingTurnWithoutEvidenceThrowsArgumentException`, and the rewritten integration `SessionLogControllerTests.WhenAcidAgentClosingTurnWithoutComplianceItemsThenReturns400`), TEST-MCP-SESSIONLOG-003 AC001 (case: the rewritten `SessionLogServiceTests.UpsertTurnAsyncCompletedEmptyTurnAcceptedForClaudeCodeRejectedForQBAgent`, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/SessionLogServiceTests.cs:538` at `a8d3934b`, the test that record already cites, so the former planned duplicate `ClaudeCodeClassAndReviewsNoEvidenceAcceptedNoClassRejected` is dropped), and TEST-SUPPORT-014 `ac-5` (cases `CompletionGateServiceTests.LifecycleCompleteNoClassReturns400` and the rewritten integration `SessionLogControllerTests.CompleteTurnStandardAgentWithoutEvidenceSucceeds`). Also validates the real-gate behavior of FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC002, -AC003 and FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003 on every host at S10 (cases `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocks<Host>` for ClaudeCode, Cowork, Codex, Copilot, Grok, GrokBot, Cline, ClineV2, OpenCode (the TypeScript hosts through their adapters and stop-equivalents); `RealGateReplSurfacesServerRefusal`, which relies on the REPL classifier carrying the server's `ClassifiedError` through at every status, architecture 3.5.2, SharedComponents' case `ReplClassifierServerClassifiedErrorKeepsCodeRetryableDetailsEveryStatus`; `RealGateMcpAgentSurfacesServerRefusal`), each only when its P0.2 row passed (otherwise its `<Host>P02NotPassedTurnLoggingUnsupportedZeroMintsZeroWrites` case). Also validates that a gate-rejected close loses no evidence across a restart (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC003; cases `RealGateRejectedCloseEvidenceSurvivesRestartReadBackEveryField`).
- TEST-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003 Write origins. Tests: `WriteOriginTests`, `WriteOriginExemptionTests`, `WriteOriginArchitectureTests`, `QuadBrainSessionTurnToolGateTests` (QBAgent's internal session-turn tools on a real `SessionLogService`), REPL `SessionLogWorkflowImportRecoveryGateTests`. Validates FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003-AC001..AC005 (AC004 by case `QbAgentTurnCompleteToolWithoutClassOrReviewsRefusedWithThemCompletes`; AC005 by cases `ImportRecoveryGateRefusedTurnsReportedWithMissingItemsNothingSavedNoReviewFabricated` and `ImportRecoverySupersededKeptByNormalizationServerRefusalSurfaced`), TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-003 AC-001..AC-003. The ServerInternal scope of AC-002 is also held by the develop test `WorkspacePolicyServiceTests.ApplyAsyncValidDirectiveUpdatesWorkspaceAndLogsPolicyChange` (`tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/WorkspacePolicyServiceTests.cs:110` at `a8d3934b`, renamed on the analyzer branch), which runs the real service and keeps its outcome only under that scope.
