### Domain: Responses, grading, trust, and response lifecycle

Bounded contexts (architecture 8.3): ResponsesAndTrust (the response, grade, trust, lifecycle, staleness, and HV request units; `ResponseStatusTransition`, `IResponseStalenessRule` with its implementation, `ResponseIdList`, the grader, and the receipt verifier), StorageSchema (the response, grade, and HV request entities), ReplAndAgents (the REPL lossless normalizer for the grade scores, architecture 3.5.4), and the Phase 2 shared files (the DTOs, `AgentContextOptions` `Trust:*`, `ModelGrader:*`, `HvRequest:MaxTurns`, and the dispatch of EvaluateStalenessCommand from the requirement update and delete paths). It calls `BuiltPackageLookup` and `SourceResolver` (ContextPackages), `RelationshipWriter` (ContextGraph), and `ScoreVerdict` (TurnReviews) through their Phase 2 seams. Use cases: architecture 7.12 and the lifecycle and staleness steps of 7.14. Scope entries: AgentContextContracts, AgentContextStorage, AgentContextResponsesAndTrust, and AgentContextServerSurfaces for the REPL normalizer.

Operator decision "Outline now, catalog in P0" (catalog rules above): each FR below is an outline statement aligned with the architecture; P0 writes every AC of FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-003, -004, and -005 and of their TRs from architecture sections 6.2 to 6.7, starting from "Named cases for the P0 catalog" below.

#### Functional requirements

- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-003 Response submission: in `Pilot` and `Stateless` the model submits its response with the `agent_context_submit_response` tool before it closes the turn, and the plugin supplies the request's `packageId` (FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009). A submission answers a `Built` package (BuiltPackageLookup: an unknown package is 404 `not_found` `package`, an `Overflow` package 409 `agent_context_package_overflow`) and carries `agent` (equal to the package's), `model`, `effort`, `invocationMode` (`Stateless` only for a package built in `Stateless`), the content, at least one claim, and for every claim at least one evidence item of kind `File`, `Requirement`, `Receipt`, `Commit`, or `SessionLogTurn` that never names the request's own turn and, except `Commit`, names an existing source; each refusal is 400 `validation_error` naming the field. The server computes each evidence hash at submission through SourceResolver (`File` at its `commitSha`, by default the workspace HEAD commit; an unreadable commit is 409 `agent_context_commit_unavailable`) and keeps the cited hash beside it, so a wrong cited hash stays a deterministic FAIL; it redacts the content and hashes its stored normal form (`ContentSha256`); it stores the response `Submitted` under `rsp-<requestId>-<nn>` with its claims, its evidence, the package's root ids, and its protected `derived_from` and `supports` links, all in one transaction. A repeated idempotency key returns the stored response only for an identical whole submission (`SubmissionSha256`); any differing input is 409 `conflict` with `retryable: false`. Responses stay readable in every Mode and each read reports the effective status (FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-005). Units: SubmitAgentResponseCommand with the nested activity ReadSubmissionInputs, GetAgentResponseQuery, QueryAgentResponsesQuery (architecture 6.3, 6.4, 6.5).
- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-004 Grading, HV requests, and trust: every response is graded by deterministic evidence checks and by the configured independent model grader. The deterministic grade (re-run on request) records one finding per evidence item (PASS, FAIL, or UNKNOWN by kind; `Commit` by ancestry of the response's commit, UNKNOWN without a git checkout) and the global findings (every claim evidenced, no secret, the response fresh), with correctness PASS over PASS plus FAIL (0 when nothing could be evaluated) and completeness the share of claims whose every check passes. The model grade is recorded by the HV agent in a grade-only continuation of its own reviewer thread after the runner froze its review receipts, for the responses of an HV request that names a range of session-log turns (no grading worker or HV pipeline in this effort, operator 2026-10-11); it is accepted only from the configured grader (`ModelGrader:*`, default Codex `gpt-6-astra` `max`; otherwise 403 `agent_context_grader_not_allowed`), only with its receipt paths and reviewer ids (400 `agent_context_receipt_paths_required`), only for a gradable response (409 `agent_context_response_not_gradable` for `Rejected` or `Superseded`) that is an item (409 `agent_context_grade_not_in_hv_request`) of an open HV request (409 `agent_context_hv_request_closed`), and only after the eight checks of architecture 6.3 pass (`receipt_path`, `request_meta`, `verdict_block`, `verdict_binding`, `verdict_match`, `reviewer_turn`, `reviewer_independent`, `runtime_identity`; otherwise 422 `agent_context_receipt_unverified` naming the check); an equal resubmission returns the stored grade and a different one is 409 `conflict` with `retryable: false`. Each grade unit runs its read-only checks, then dispatches EvaluateStalenessCommand for its response, then saves in one SerializableWrite that re-checks the response's `StatusVersion` and dispatches the internal trust decision (DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand: no surface, not mode-gated, reads only the database). "Latest" means the greatest server-stamped `RecordedUtc`, then `GradeId`; a client `gradedUtc` is provenance only. A response is `Trusted` only when it is fresh, neither `Rejected`, `Stale`, nor `Superseded`, and the verdicts of its latest deterministic and latest model grade, re-derived from their stored scores and counts at `max(configured now, 0.98)`, are both `Pass` with zero FAIL and zero UNKNOWN findings; otherwise it is `Grading`, and a trust threshold below 0.98 fails startup. An HV request (`hvr-<sessionId>-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>`, idempotent by key) lists only the gradable responses of its range (400 `agent_context_hv_request_invalid` for an unknown session, a bad range, a range over `HvRequest:MaxTurns`, or none gradable), each `Pending` in at most one open request; an item closes when its response is rejected or superseded, the request completes when no item is `Pending`, and a rerun of an abandoned review grades the remaining items. The model grader and its option set are separate from the hostile-review worker of FR-MCP-HOSTILEREVIEW-003 (effort `xhigh`), and an HV request is not a request of the FR-MCP-HOSTILEREVIEW queue (architecture 6.1). Units: RunDeterministicGradeCommand, RecordModelGradeCommand with the nested activity ReadModelGradeInputs, DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand, CreateAgentContextHvRequestCommand, GetAgentContextHvRequestQuery, QueryAgentContextHvRequestsQuery (architecture 6.2, 6.3, 6.4, 6.5).
- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-005 Rejection, staleness, and supersession: rejected, stale, and superseded responses are preserved for audit and never retrieved as trusted context. Every status change follows the response lifecycle of architecture 6.3 through ResponseStatusTransition: a repeat of an applied transition returns 200 unchanged, a transition the lifecycle does not draw is 409 `agent_context_transition_invalid` (a reject of a `Superseded` response, a supersede of a `Rejected` original or of one superseded by another response), and in the same transaction a response leaving `Trusted` marks its GraphRAG projection row and a reject or supersede closes the response's `Pending` HV items. A supersede names a replacement for the same turn (the same `agent`, `sessionId`, and `requestId`, the whole turn key, so a replacement with the same `requestId` in another session or of another agent is refused) that is neither `Rejected` nor `Superseded` and closes no cycle (otherwise 409 `agent_context_supersede_invalid`), and it is the only writer of a `supersedes` link. A response is stale when a response it depends on is not effectively `Trusted` and fresh (a package item or a `depends_on`, `derived_from`, or `supports` target that names a response, evaluated in turn at any depth, each response once per evaluation and a cycle edge adding nothing, because a response's `ContentSha256` never changes when it is rejected, superseded, made stale, or demoted; architecture 3.1, Dependent responses), and when a dependency changed: the `depends_on`, `derived_from`, and `supports` targets of the response and of its package, its evidence (including evidence outside its package), and every cited requirement (stale when deleted or superseded), with files compared at the workspace HEAD commit (an uncommitted edit changes nothing, a file absent at HEAD has changed), and never the request's own turn or a Rule file outside version control; a requirement's hash covers the effective record's id, title, description, and acceptance-criterion texts in order, never its status, its criteria's satisfaction flags or evidence, or timestamps, so recording a criterion satisfied or an HV verdict stales nothing (architecture 3.1, Requirement hash). A unit whose commit moved a response out of `Trusted` or into `Rejected`, `Superseded`, or `Stale` dispatches EvaluateStalenessCommand after that commit for the responses that depend on it, only while Mode is not `Off`, logging a failed dispatch without failing the unit (architecture 3.1, Dependent responses). Only EvaluateStalenessCommand persists `Stale` (ids validated and selected by ResponseIdList, unknown ids returned in `notFound`); every read that returns or counts a response's status (the response reads, retrieval, the status query, and the pilot report) reports the effective status and writes nothing. After the commit of each requirement write that changes or deletes a requirement or its acceptance criteria, the requirement service dispatches EvaluateStalenessCommand for the responses that cite a changed requirement (and, after a change of the current requirement layer, with no `responseIds`), only while Mode is not `Off`, outside the requirement transaction, and a failed or exhausted dispatch is logged and never fails the requirement write (architecture 3.1, Requirement writes and staleness). A unit that marks a projection row (the grade units through the trust decision, Reject, Supersede, EvaluateStalenessCommand) dispatches the projection pass after its commit (FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007). Units: RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, EvaluateStalenessCommand with the nested activity EvaluateOneResponseStaleness, and the freshness rule of GetAgentResponseQuery and QueryAgentResponsesQuery (architecture 3.5.5, 6.3, 6.5).

#### Technical requirements

- TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-003 Response submission and response reads. Scope for P0's ACs: the record and handler files of SubmitAgentResponseCommand in `src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Responses/Commands/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.AgentContext.Responses.Commands`) and of GetAgentResponseQuery and QueryAgentResponsesQuery in `src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Responses/Queries/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.AgentContext.Responses.Queries`), one type per file; the entities `AgentResponseEntity` (`StatusVersion`, `SubmissionSha256`, `ContentSha256`, `CommitSha`), `AgentResponseClaimEntity`, and `AgentResponseEvidenceEntity` (`CitedSha256` and `Sha256`) in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Entities/AgentContext/`; the DTOs in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/AgentContext/`; keys `rsp-<requestId>-<nn>`; IdempotentCreate with policy `Conflict` over `SubmissionSha256`, looked up read-only before ReadSubmissionInputs; the database-only SerializableWrite inserting the response, its children, and its RelationshipWriter links (`CreatedBy` `SubmitAgentResponseCommand`), each attempt under `StorageCommandBudget.Default` (architecture 3.1, Storage budget and sizes); the error lists of the three units (architecture 6.4, 6.5). [AgentContextContracts +AgentContextStorage +AgentContextResponsesAndTrust]
- TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-004 Grades, HV requests, and the trust decision. Scope for P0's ACs: the record and handler files of RunDeterministicGradeCommand, RecordModelGradeCommand, DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand, and CreateAgentContextHvRequestCommand (`Responses/Commands/`) and of GetAgentContextHvRequestQuery and QueryAgentContextHvRequestsQuery (`Responses/Queries/`); the grader and the receipt verifier (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Responses/`; the eight checks of architecture 6.3, `runtime_identity` requiring at least one review-run `turn_context` and `verdict_match` the per-entry `verdict` and `overallVerdict` consistency); the entities `AgentResponseGradeEntity` (`RecordedUtc`, the filtered unique natural key (`WorkspaceId`, `ResponseId`, `HvRequestId`) for `Model`, `PayloadSha256`, the receipt paths and hashes, `ReviewerThreadId`), `AgentResponseGradeFindingEntity`, `AgentContextHvRequestEntity`, and `AgentContextHvRequestItemEntity`; keys `grd-<responseId>-<kind>-<nn>` and `hvr-<sessionId>-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>`; the options `Trust:Correctness`, `Trust:Completeness` (0.98 to 1, startup validation), `ModelGrader:AgentName`, `RequiredModel`, `RequiredEffort`, `ReceiptRoot`, and `HvRequest:MaxTurns`; ScoreVerdict's floor and count parameters (`max(configured, 0.98)`, zero FAIL and zero UNKNOWN; the component is TurnReviews', and its agent-context floor cases are this TR's, TR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-007 holding its Phase B cases) and ScoreLexemeJsonConverter at deserialization (architecture 3.5.4); the per-response `StatusVersion` lock in ascending `ResponseId` order; the read-only checks, the EvaluateStalenessCommand dispatch, and the SerializableWrite of each grade unit (architecture 3.1, "Database-only delegates"), each attempt under `StorageCommandBudget.Default`, whose expiry is the 503 `backend_unavailable` storage answer (architecture 3.1, Storage budget and sizes); the post-commit dispatch of the projection pass when the trust decision marked a projection row (its AC is FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007's); the REPL lossless normalizer for the `correctness` and `completeness` of `RecordModelGradeAsync` (ReplAndAgents). [AgentContextContracts +AgentContextStorage +AgentContextResponsesAndTrust +AgentContextServerSurfaces]
- TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-005 Response lifecycle and staleness. Scope for P0's ACs: the record and handler files of RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, and EvaluateStalenessCommand (`Responses/Commands/`); the ResponsesAndTrust components `ResponseStatusTransition` (lifecycle of architecture 6.3, timestamps, projection row mark, HV item closing), `IResponseStalenessRule` and its implementation (the Phase 2 seam ContextPackages and ContextGraph call read-only), and `ResponseIdList` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Responses/`), each with one test class; the post-commit EvaluateStalenessCommand dispatch of `RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService`, the database `IRequirementsDocumentService` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Requirements/IRequirementsDocumentService.cs:9` at `a8d3934b`): after each write that changes or deletes a requirement or its acceptance criteria (the single FR, TR, and TEST writes, `UpdateBatchAsync` at `RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService.cs:817`, and the recovery apply and import that write through it) with the `responseIds` of the responses citing a changed requirement (evidence `Ref`, or a `depends_on`, `derived_from`, or `supports` link to it), and after `SetWorkspaceCurrentRequirementLayerAsync` (`:343`) with none, outside the requirement transaction, only while Mode is not `Off`, a failed or exhausted dispatch logged without failing the write (architecture 3.1, Requirement writes and staleness; a Phase 2 shared file, architecture 8.3); the dependency rule of `IResponseStalenessRule` for a dependency that names a response (effectively `Trusted` and fresh, evaluated recursively with per-evaluation memoization, a cycle edge adding nothing) and the post-commit EvaluateStalenessCommand dispatch for the dependents of a response that left `Trusted` or became `Rejected`, `Superseded`, or `Stale` (architecture 3.1, Dependent responses; 3.5.5); the post-commit dispatch of the projection pass by Reject, Supersede, and EvaluateStalenessCommand when they marked a projection row (its AC is FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007's); each SerializableWrite attempt under `StorageCommandBudget.Default`; the error lists of the three units. [AgentContextResponsesAndTrust]

#### Named cases for the P0 catalog

Conventions as in the Context packages domain: "plan-named" marks a case this plan names for a branch or code the architecture lists without one; shared-component cases (the workspace 400, the SerializableWrite and ReadableKeyAllocator exhaustion 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, ReadableKeyAllocator's key cases) stay with the shared-platform domain, and `ReadableKeyAllocatorSuffixPast99WidensLatestGradeOrderKept` runs at ResponsesAndTrust's gate because it needs the grade types (architecture 3.1, Test placement); the cross-FR theories are named here for this domain's rows only. Cases that need another context's real component run at the gate of the context that merges later (architecture 8.4; rollout order ContextPackages, ResponsesAndTrust, ContextGraph).

#### AC ownership

- Every AC P0 writes for FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-00N or TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-00N (N = 3, 4, 5) is owned by TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-00N.
- The Submit row of `SubmitOrReplayOverflowPackage409PackageOverflow` is contributed to TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-001, which owns BuiltPackageLookup's overflow AC (Context packages domain).
- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-005's freshness AC ("every read that returns or counts a status reports the effective status and writes nothing") is owned by TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-005 with the four response-read cases; the other reads contribute their cases: `BuildFocusRefNamesRejectedResponseOmittedRejected` (TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-001), `RetrieveAfterEvidenceFileChangeResponseExcludedNothingWritten` (TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007), and `StatusAfterDependencyChangeCountsStaleNotTrustedWritesNothing` and `PilotReportAfterDependencyChangeCountsStaleNotTrustedWritesNothing` (TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009).
- The new staleness-dispatch AC of TR-MCP-REQRECOVERY-001, TR-MCP-REQ-002, TR-MCP-BATCH-109, and TR-MCP-REQ-005 is owned, on each record, by TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-005 (the four requirement-write cases above; mapping additions below).
- The projection-pass dispatch these units make after their commit is an AC of FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007 (owner TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007, case `ProjectionPassDispatchedAfterTrustCommitFailureLoggedUnitSucceeds`).

#### Updated existing requirements

The requirement writes of `RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService` gain one side effect, the post-commit staleness dispatch (architecture 3.1, Requirement writes and staleness: "P0 records the side effect on TR-MCP-REQRECOVERY-001 and on the TRs of these `IRequirementsDocumentService` writes"; 6.5, EvaluateStalenessCommand "Callers"). Each record below gets the same new AC, P0 assigning its id in the `<REQID>-AC###` form after the record's existing ACs in store order: "After the commit of a write that changes or deletes a requirement or its acceptance criteria, the requirement service dispatches EvaluateStalenessCommand with the `responseIds` of the responses that cite a changed requirement (and, after a change of the current requirement layer, with none), outside the requirement transaction and only while `Mcp:AgentContext:Mode` is not `Off`; a failed or exhausted dispatch is logged and never fails the requirement write." [AgentContextResponsesAndTrust]
- TR-MCP-REQRECOVERY-001 (TR:2561) "Transactional requirements recovery service and shared plugin contract": the recovery apply writes through the service (architecture 3.1), so its commit dispatches as above.
- TR-MCP-REQ-002 (TR:2456) "Requirements Document Management Service": the single FR, TR, and TEST create, update, and delete writes (`RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService.cs:586-749` at `a8d3934b`) and the current-layer change (`:343`); inference: the layer change has no TR of its own beyond the REQSCOPE placeholders (TR-MCP-REQSCOPE-001..004, placeholder statements), so it rides on this record.
- TR-MCP-BATCH-109 (TR:539) "Requirements batch endpoint and workflow support": `UpdateBatchAsync` (`RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService.cs:817`).
- TR-MCP-REQ-005 (TR:2477) "Wiki Import Selection and Authoritative Sync": the import that creates, updates, and deletes records through the service (architecture 3.1 names "the recovery apply and import"; the identification of this TR as that import is inference from its statement).

Related existing records, unchanged (architecture 6.1):
- FR-MCP-HOSTILEREVIEW-003 (FR:1381), AC 4 (FR:1389, effective model `gpt-6-astra` and effort `xhigh`): the hostile-review worker keeps that model and effort with agent `Astra`, all three enforced at startup (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Options/HostileReviewWorkerOptions.cs:13,16,19,77-82` at `a8d3934b`); `ModelGrader:*` is a separate option set, so neither constrains the other.
- The FR-MCP-HOSTILEREVIEW queue (FR-MCP-HOSTILEREVIEW-001 to -006; `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/HostileReviewService.cs:290` at `a8d3934b`, its artifact-link resolution): separate; an HV request of FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-004 is a turn range an external HV agent grades, and a hostile review result is never a model grade.
- FR-MCP-REQRECOVERY-001 (FR:2002), FR-MCP-040 (FR:325), FR-MCP-109 (FR:871), and FR-MCP-084 (FR:726): unchanged; each gains the mapping row above for its TR's new AC, and FR-MCP-REQRECOVERY-001's Serializable transaction precedent (`RequirementsRecoveryService.cs:80-126`) is cited by SerializableWrite.

#### Test churn

None changes outcome at `a8d3934b`:
- The hostile-review worker suite keeps its outcomes (`tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/HostileReviewG4OverlayTests.cs:159,166,461,470` assert effort `xhigh`), because `ModelGrader:*` is a separate option set.
- The requirement write, batch, import, layer, and recovery suites keep their outcomes: the dispatch runs after the requirement commit and outside its transaction, a failed dispatch never fails the write (architecture 3.1, Requirement writes and staleness), every existing suite runs with `Mcp:AgentContext:Mode` `Off` (architecture 8.4, "Agent context Mode in the gates"), so the new dispatch never fires there, and no test constructs `RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService` directly (git grep at `a8d3934b` finds no `new RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService(` under `tests`), so the dispatcher the Phase 2 change adds reaches them only through DI (inference).

### Domain: Relationships, retrieval, and GraphRAG projection

Bounded contexts (architecture 8.3): ContextGraph (`RelationshipWriter`, the relationship units, ProjectAgentResponsesCommand, and the GraphRAG path of RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery behind the retrieval seam), ContextPackages (RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery's record, handler, and relational path with `CandidateRanker`), StorageSchema (the relationship and projection entities), and the Phase 2 shared files. ContextGraph calls `SourceResolver` (ContextPackages), `IResponseStalenessRule` read-only and `ResponseIdList` (ResponsesAndTrust), and dispatches EvaluateStalenessCommand through `IDispatcher`. Use cases: architecture 7.13, 7.16, and the relationship steps of 7.14. Scope entries: AgentContextStorage, AgentContextRelationships, AgentContextGraphRag, and AgentContextPackages for the relational path.

Operator decision "Outline now, catalog in P0" (catalog rules above): each FR below is an outline statement aligned with the architecture; P0 writes every AC of FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-006 and -007 and of their TRs from architecture sections 6.2 to 6.7, starting from "Named cases for the P0 catalog" below.

#### Functional requirements

- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-006 Typed relationships: packages, responses, requirements, turns, TODOs, code files, receipts, profile files, and rule files (the `SourceKind` wire values `Package`, `Response`, `Requirement`, `SessionLogTurn`, `Todo`, `CodeFile`, `Receipt`, `Profile`, `Rule`) are linked with `implements`, `verifies`, `depends_on`, `supports`, `contradicts`, `supersedes`, `blocks`, `derived_from`, and `relevant_to`. Both refs resolve through SourceResolver, and the target's hash (`ToSha256`), read before the write, is kept on the link for staleness; a ref that names no existing source is 400 `validation_error` naming `fromRef` or `toRef`, an unknown kind or type or a self-link is 400 `agent_context_relationship_invalid`, and a client request for type `supersedes` is 400 `validation_error` with `details.field` `type`, because only SupersedeAgentResponseCommand writes the supersede chain. An identical link, created again or concurrently, returns the stored link with `created: false`; a deleted identical link is revived with its `RelationshipId` and `created: true`. Every link a command made (Build's `derived_from` and `relevant_to`, Submit's `derived_from` and `supports`, Supersede's `supersedes`) is protected (409 `agent_context_relationship_protected`); a client-made link of any type can be deleted (soft delete, 204). Links are queried by node, type, and direction (`out`, `in`, `both`) with bounded paging. The record states why the links are one store of their own, beside `GraphRelationshipEntity`, `TodoRequirementLinkEntity`, `RequirementTraceabilityLinkEntity`, `MemoryEdgeEntity` (TR-MCP-MEMORY-MODEL-002), and `HostileReviewArtifactLinkEntity`, and that an identical link returns `created: false` where a duplicate memory edge is 409 (architecture 6.3). Units: CreateAgentContextRelationshipCommand, DeleteAgentContextRelationshipCommand, QueryAgentContextRelationshipsQuery (architecture 6.5).
- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007 GraphRAG projection with deterministic fallback: trusted responses are projected into GraphRAG for semantic retrieval and removed when they stop being trusted. A projection pass (ProjectAgentResponsesCommand, gated like every agent-context command) runs on request and as the background pass: the unit whose transaction marked a projection row (the grade units through the trust decision, RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, EvaluateStalenessCommand) dispatches it with no `responseIds` after its commit, and a failed dispatch is logged and never fails that unit (architecture 3.1, Projection pass). The pass first persists each selected response's staleness (EvaluateStalenessCommand), then ingests a `Trusted` response into `GraphRagStorageScope.Workspace` only, when its projected hash differs (title the `responseId`, `sourceType` `agent-response`, `sourceKey` `agent-context/<responseId>`, so a hit maps back to its response), and deletes the document of a response that is no longer `Trusted`; `GraphRag:ProjectionEnabled` false skips ingest only, GraphRAG disabled skips ingest and keeps a removal `Pending` until GraphRAG is enabled, an ingest or delete error is `Failed` with `LastError` and retried on the next pass (a delete of a document already gone, which `DeleteDocumentAsync` answers with `Success: false`, is a completed removal), and each row is saved through SerializableWrite with the response status re-read after the GraphRAG call (projection row states, architecture 6.3); every pass ends by reconciling the workspace's `agent-response` documents with the projection rows, deleting each document no `Projected` row names that was ingested before the pass started and returning a `Projected` row whose document is gone to `Pending`, so racing ingests, a save that exhausted its retries after an ingest, and a failed delete leave no document past the next complete pass (architecture 3.1, Projection pass; 6.5, ReconcileProjectedDocuments). Retrieval (RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, readable in every Mode) takes the GraphRAG path only when `preferGraphRag` asks for it, `GraphRag:UseForRetrieval` allows it, and only `PriorResponse` is requested; that path keeps one workspace-scoped hit per response that is `Trusted` and fresh; any other case answers from the deterministic relational path with the stated `fallbackReason` (`graphrag_disabled`, `graphrag_not_initialized`, `graphrag_not_indexed`, `index_in_progress`, `retrieval_disabled`, `graphrag_error`, `graphrag_kinds_unsupported`), which ranks by the relationship neighborhood of the turn (`agent`, `sessionId`, `requestId`, never itself a candidate) and the focus refs the request names, or by the existing hybrid search (FR-MCP-004) when it names neither, a hit mapping by its document's source type to a `CodeFile` (`repo`) or `SessionLogTurn` (`session-log`) candidate and every other source type dropped (architecture 3.1, Hybrid-search hits as candidates); every `PriorResponse` candidate is checked for staleness read-only and nothing is written. Units: RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, ProjectAgentResponsesCommand with the nested activity ProjectOneResponse (architecture 6.3, 6.5).

### Domain: Agent-context surfaces, mode, and stateless invocation

Bounded contexts (architecture 8.3): the Phase 2 shared files (work package `DESIGN-MERGEDPLAN-001`: `AgentContextController`, the MCP tools in `FwhMcpTools.AgentContext.cs`, `AgentContextClient`, the REPL allow-list entry and YAML validators, `ClientMutationPolicy`, the surface error mapping, the DTO registrations in `McpClientJsonContext`, `AgentContextOptions`, and the golden contract file `docs/context/agent-context-contract.json`), SharedComponents (`ModeGateBehavior` and `IModeGatedRequest`, architecture 3.5.1), ContextPackages (GetAgentContextStatusQuery), ResponsesAndTrust (GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery), PluginCorePowerShell (the `New-McpAgentContext<Verb>Params` builders, the agent-context entries of `lib/tool-descriptors.json`, and process RunStatelessInvocation), HostPluginAdoption (each host's injection operation, adapter, and sentinel test), and Documentation (the feature-matrix rows and the agent-context docs). Use case: architecture 7.15. Scope entries: AgentContextContracts, AgentContextServerSurfaces, AgentContextPluginCore, AgentContextExit.

Operator decision "Outline now, catalog in P0" (catalog rules above): each FR below is an outline statement aligned with the architecture; P0 writes every AC of FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-008 and -009 and of their TRs from architecture sections 6.2 to 6.7, starting from "Named cases for the P0 catalog" below.

#### Functional requirements

- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-008 Surfaces: every externally callable agent-context unit (GetAgentContextStatusQuery, BuildAgentContextPackageCommand, GetAgentContextPackageQuery, QueryAgentContextPackagesQuery, ReplayAgentContextPackageQuery, SubmitAgentResponseCommand, GetAgentResponseQuery, QueryAgentResponsesQuery, RunDeterministicGradeCommand, RecordModelGradeCommand, RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, EvaluateStalenessCommand, CreateAgentContextRelationshipCommand, DeleteAgentContextRelationshipCommand, QueryAgentContextRelationshipsQuery, RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, ProjectAgentResponsesCommand, GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery, CreateAgentContextHvRequestCommand, GetAgentContextHvRequestQuery, QueryAgentContextHvRequestsQuery) is available, dispatching the same record through `IDispatcher`, through REST (`AgentContextController` under `/qbrainai/agent-context` and the alias `/mcpserver/agent-context`), the MCP tools `agent_context_*` (`workspacePath` first; the unit's result DTO as camelCase JSON, or `{ code, error, message, retryable, details }`), the typed client (`QBrainAiClient.AgentContext`), the REPL (`client.AgentContext.<Method>`, bound by parameter name through `GenericClientPassthrough`), and, in all nine plugins, the PowerShell builders `New-McpAgentContext<Verb>Params` and one `agent_context_*` tool descriptor per unit (the TypeScript hosts through their adapters). Every error is the unit's listed code in the `ClassifiedError` body (a workspace that does not resolve is 400 `validation_error` with `details.field` `workspacePath`, an unknown route id 404 `not_found` naming the resource, a paging value outside its bounds 400 naming the field). The REPL's `agentContext` client namespace is service-gated with its eleven query methods on the read list: a read is never refused, and a command is refused in the REPL with `transaction_degraded` while the turn transaction coordinator is degraded and allowed while required turn transactions are active (updates FR-MCP-120 and TR-MCP-TXN-001). The hosted agent's generic `mcp_client_invoke` reaches `client.AgentContext.*` under the same units, Mode gate, and REPL client policy as any agent, cannot record a model grade (only the configured grader can), and the ACID profile is unchanged (architecture 3.1, Hosted agents). The two internal units have no endpoint by design: DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand (the trust decision, run inside RunDeterministicGradeCommand and RecordModelGradeCommand) and process RunStatelessInvocation (the plugin's stateless invocation, run by the plugins' prompt hooks) (architecture 6.4, 6.5).
- FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009 Opt-in mode, stateless invocation, and pilot: `Mcp:AgentContext:Mode` is `Off` by default, and stateful behavior stays the default until the operator changes it from the pilot report. The server reads the Mode at each dispatch, so a reloaded configuration takes effect without a restart (architecture 3.1, Mode at runtime). In `Off` every gated agent-context command (every command except the internal trust decision carries the marker) answers 409 `agent_context_disabled` while the status query and every read-only query stay available for audit; `Pilot` builds, records, and grades packages without injecting them; `Stateless` gives the model only the rendered package, the current request, and the host's deterministic `REQUIRED MEMORIES` block, with the messages of earlier requests excluded and, on each later model call of the same request's tool loop, that request's own prompt, tool calls, and results kept (architecture 3.1, Tool loops in `Stateless`), on a host whose P0.2 row records an authoritative history-exclusion operation, and any other host runs `Stateless` as `Pilot` with its feature-matrix limitation; a host whose prompt hook cannot refuse a prompt, or runs the prompt anyway after a hook timeout, crash, or `plugin_core_unavailable`, runs `Pilot` and `Stateless` as `Off`. The plugin reads the Mode at host session start through GetAgentContextStatusQuery (at the first prompt on cline, cline-v2, and opencode), caches it for the host session, treats an unreadable or unknown Mode as `Off`, caches `Off` and continues statefully when a build or submission answers 409 `agent_context_disabled`, and applies a change away from `Off` at the next host session start. For a delivered request (on claude-code and claude-cowork after syncing the transcript to take the bridge-minted request id; an absorbed mid-turn message runs as `Pilot`) the plugin builds the package with an idempotency key derived from the whole turn key (`agent`, `sessionId`, `requestId`; architecture 6.6), so two legacy turns that share a request id build two packages, and keeps its `packageId` in the per-turn file; any build error or outage (the REPL's `transaction_degraded` and `mutation_not_transactional` included), a success whose package is not `Built`, or a pending mint fails the invocation (operator, 2026-10-11: "Fail the invocation"): the prompt is refused with the code and message (and the token counts of an overflow), no answer is produced, and the plugin closes the request's own turn `canceled` with `failureNote` `agent_context_invocation_failed <code>: <message>` (the only `canceled` a hook writes, updating FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 `ac-2`), held in the failsafe on an outage while the Stop gate warns; a final mint refusal, a hook that mints nothing, and a bridge request with no enqueue record leave no turn, so the prompt is refused with no close (for a final mint refusal the Stop gate warns, naming the prompt, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008). The model submits its response with `agent_context_submit_response` before it closes the turn, the plugin supplying the `packageId` (found again by its whole turn key when lost) and then requesting the deterministic grade; a missing submission is not a Stop-gate item. The pilot report compares responses by `invocationMode` with counts by effective status, means over model-graded responses, and the trust rate, and counts `Overflow` packages, packages without a response, and refused prompts. Units: GetAgentContextStatusQuery, GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery, process RunStatelessInvocation with the nested activities FailInvocation and AnswerAndSubmit (architecture 3.1, 6.2, 6.4, 6.5, 6.6).

#### Technical requirements

- TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-008 Agent-context surfaces. Scope for P0's ACs: `AgentContextController` (`[ApiController]`, both route prefixes, `WorkspaceAuthMiddleware`, with `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware` answering its own `{ error }` bodies before any controller, TR-MCP-MT-002); the MCP tools in `FwhMcpTools.AgentContext.cs`; `AgentContextClient : McpClientBase` exposed as `QBrainAiClient.AgentContext` with the parameter names the REPL binds (`packageId`, `responseId`, `relationshipId`, `hvRequestId`, `request`, `query`, scalars by name); the `AGENTCONTEXT` entry of the `GenericClientPassthrough` allow-list and the YAML validators with explicit `payload.params.<key>` paths; `KnownUnsafeClientMutationPolicy`: `agentContext` in `ServiceGatedClientNamespaces`, its eleven query methods in `ReadMethods`, and `Evaluate` without the `!ReadMethods.ContainsKey(clientName)` clause (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ClientMutationPolicy.cs:100-174,197-218` at `a8d3934b`); the surface error mapping of every route and tool from `ClassifiedErrorException` through `McpErrorClassifier` (Phase 2, one golden case per route, architecture 8.3); the DTOs in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/AgentContext/` with their `McpClientJsonContext` registrations (type, `List<>`, `IReadOnlyList<>`, each closed `PageResult<TItem>`); the builder contract and the tool descriptor contract of architecture 6.5, generated against `docs/context/agent-context-contract.json`, with the builder binding case in `tests/QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests` (PluginCorePowerShell), which starts `pwsh` for each builder and binds its output through the real `GenericClientPassthrough` binder (architecture 3.1, Builder binding case). [AgentContextContracts +AgentContextServerSurfaces +AgentContextPluginCore]
- TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009 Mode gate, status, pilot report, and stateless invocation. Scope for P0's ACs: `AgentContextOptions` in `src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/AgentContextOptions.cs` (namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.AgentContext`, `SectionName` `Mcp:AgentContext`, declared under `QBrainAi: AgentContext:` and projected by `CanonicalSectionProjectionProvider`, enum `AgentContextMode`, `Validate()` with `ValidateOnStart()` over every range of architecture 6.2; not a G5 check, architecture 8.4); `ModeGateBehavior` and `IModeGatedRequest` (two files in `src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/`, registered in both composition roots; every agent-context command implements the marker except DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand, checked by reflection; the Mode read through `IOptionsMonitor<AgentContextOptions>` at each dispatch, the pattern of `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/AgentPoolService.cs:36` at `a8d3934b`, architecture 3.1, Mode at runtime and Mode gate completeness); the record and handler files of GetAgentContextStatusQuery (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Packages/Queries/`; the Mode through the same `IOptionsMonitor`; effective counts with one SourceResolver read per distinct dependency and commit, within the SessionStart hook bound) and GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Responses/Queries/`; the refused prompts counted through SessionTurnLogging's Phase 2 read seam, architecture 3.1, Pilot report and session-log turns); process RunStatelessInvocation as `Invoke-PluginAgentContextInvocation` in `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1` (dot-sourced by `plugin-hook.ps1`, called in-process by ApplyArrivalBridgeRecord for an `absorbed_mid_turn` delivery), with the Mode cache `agentContextMode` in `turn-state.yaml` through `Update-TurnState`, the `packageId` in `turns/<requestId>.yaml`, FailInvocation through process PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt (no `Test-ModelEvidence` on a `canceled` close), and AnswerAndSubmit; each host's injection operation from its P0.2 row (opencode `experimental.chat.messages.transform`, cline-v2 `beforeModel`, or a fresh host invocation), removing earlier requests' messages only and keeping the current request's tool loop; the package bounded by `TokenBudget` and each host's prompt-context limit recorded in its P0.2 row (architecture 3.1, Storage budget and sizes); the feature-matrix rows for refusal capability and history exclusion. [AgentContextContracts +AgentContextServerSurfaces +AgentContextPackages for the status query's handler +AgentContextResponsesAndTrust for the pilot report's handler +AgentContextPluginCore +AgentContextExit]

#### Updated existing requirements

- FR-MCP-120 (FR:951) "QBrain.AI transaction gating", `ac-5`, its fifth AC in store order ("Generic REPL client passthrough blocks unsafe protected namespaces and unclassified client namespaces while required transaction gating is active ..., while explicitly service-gated namespaces remain routed to server-side gates"). Changes: the AC adds that the `agentContext` client namespace is service-gated with its eleven query methods as listed reads, and that a service-gated namespace stays routed while required turn transactions are active even when it also has listed reads (the `!ReadMethods.ContainsKey(clientName)` clause of `src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ClientMutationPolicy.cs:213` at `a8d3934b` is dropped; no develop namespace is in both lists, `:100-174`, so no existing decision changes) (architecture 3.1, "Transaction-gated REPL clients", and 6.5). [AgentContextServerSurfaces]
- TR-MCP-TXN-001 (TR:3316) "Turn Transaction Coordinator", no AC in the store. New AC (P0 assigns its id in the `<REQID>-AC###` form): "`KnownUnsafeClientMutationPolicy` classifies `agentContext` as a service-gated client namespace and its eleven query methods as reads: a listed read is allowed in every coordinator state, a command is refused with `transaction_degraded` while the coordinator is degraded and allowed while required turn transactions are active, and every other namespace keeps its develop decision." [AgentContextServerSurfaces]

Related existing records, unchanged (listed so reviewers can check):

- FR-MCP-MEMORY-007 (FR:1564): one updated entry, in the Plugin hosts domain (`24-plugin-hosts.md`, "Updated existing requirements"), which carries this domain's Stateless-input clause and which P0 writes as one update; no AC changes here. Its TypeScript-host injection cases (`ClineV2BeforeModelForwardedRequiredMemoriesAddedOnce`, `OpenCodeChatMessageOutputCarriesRequiredMemories`) are that domain's, and the Stateless cases (`StatelessHostSentinelPriorTurnAbsentFromModelRequest`, `StatelessToolLoopOwnToolCallsKeptPriorRequestsExcluded`) are this domain's FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009 TEST's.

- FR-MCP-136 (FR:1116) and FR-MCP-QBEXEC-002 (FR:1712): unchanged; the hosted-agent tool catalog is a fixed list (`src/QBrainAi.McpAgent/Hosting/McpHostedAgentToolAdapter.cs:47-232` at `a8d3934b`) to which this plan adds no `agent_context_*` tool, and the ACID profile exposes no generic passthrough (FR-MCP-136 AC 3). #### Test churn

None changes outcome at `a8d3934b`:
- `tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/ClientMutationPolicyTests.cs:173-253` (theory `EvaluateWithRequiredTransactionsAppliesKnownUnsafeMutationBlockList`, the name after the analyzer branch's CA1707 rename) keeps every row's outcome, because no namespace with listed reads is service-gated today (`ClientMutationPolicy.cs:100-174`); the `agentContext` rows are new cases of TEST-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-008, and the theory stays TEST-MCP-161's baseline-green evidence.
- `tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/GenericClientPassthroughValidClientNamesTests.cs:48,69` (`InvokeAsyncUnknownClientErrorTextStillListsResolvableClients`, `InvokeAsyncEveryAdvertisedClientNameResolvesThroughThePassthrough`, names after the analyzer branch's CA1707 rename) keep their outcomes and cover the new `AgentContext` name, because the advertised list and the resolver share one source.
- The prompt-hook and SessionStart Pester cases keep their outcomes: every existing fixture has no cached `agentContextMode`, which reads as `Off`, and a status read the fixture's REPL double cannot answer is `Off` (architecture 6.2, 6.6); git grep at `a8d3934b` finds no exact REPL call-list assertion in `plugins/core/test-fixtures` that the one status read at host session start would break (inference from the search, not a run).

### Domain: Shared platform: dispatch, workspace resolution, storage, and errors

Bounded contexts (architecture 8.3): SharedComponents (sections 3.5.1 and 3.5.2: the `QBrainAi.Cqrs` dispatch scope, WorkspaceResolutionBehavior with `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace`, the registration of the two CQRS behaviors, and the storage, error, redaction, and hashing components; scope entry SharedPlatform), StorageSchema (the explicit dump policy of each new `DbSet`, in the same change as the sets; scope entries S3 for the review reason set and AgentContextStorage for the agent-context sets), and the Phase 2 shared files of `DESIGN-MERGEDPLAN-001` that this domain's records name (the `agent_context` federation domain registration, section 6.1). `ModeGateBehavior` and `IModeGatedRequest` are delivered by SharedComponents, and their ACs are TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009's (domain Agent-context surfaces). Use cases: none (cross-cutting).

This domain adds no FR. It adds one TR with its mirroring TEST, TR-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 and TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001, because no TR of any domain covers the section 3.5.2 components DbErrorClassifier, SerializableWrite, IdempotentCreate with PayloadFingerprint, ReadableKeyAllocator, PagedQuery with PageBounds and PageResult, SecretRedactor, and ContentHasher as components; they are the single reusable location TR-MCP-DRY-001 requires for logic that more than one unit uses (architecture 3.5). Every other change of this domain updates a live record (below). The uses of these components by units are ACs of the domains that own the units (for example the turn-write forced interleavings at SessionTurnLogging's and TurnReviews' gates, and `BuildOnSqliteHookWriteDuringRetrievalNotBlocked` at ContextPackages', architecture 8.4). Component rule (architecture 3.5, 8.2, 8.4): each component has one implementation and one test class named for it, which runs in G2, with its three-provider cases (SQLite, PostgreSQL, SQL Server) in G3 through `./build.ps1 MigrationIntegrationTests --skip InstallTestDependencies`; a unit's tests use the component's Phase 2 seam and never test the component's branches again; SharedComponents' exit runs only the cases its own components complete, and a case that needs a later context runs at that context's gate. File layout (architecture 3.1, "Analyzer rules", and 3.5): one type per file (MA0048), each namespace the project's RootNamespace plus its folder (IDE0130), test methods without underscores (CA1707; cited develop tests carry the analyzer branch's spelling). P0.3 verifies that `TR-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001` and `TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001` are free (the 2026-10-12 store export holds no SHAREDPLATFORM id, and no TEST-MCP-CQRS, TEST-MCP-MT, or TEST-MCP-DB-003 record). A case marked "named here" is named by this plan because the architecture states the rule without a case.

#### Functional requirements

None. The shared-platform behavior is accountable through the updated FRs below (FR-MCP-MEMORY-001, FR-SUPPORT-012, FR-SUPPORT-010B, FR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001, FR-MCP-130, FR-MCP-103, FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003) and through the existing FRs the new and updated TRs map to (FR-MCP-029, FR-MCP-043, FR-MCP-044, FR-MCP-105).

#### Technical requirements

- TR-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 Shared storage, paging, redaction, and hashing components (architecture 3.5.2; owner SharedComponents; mapped to FR-MCP-105, the FR of the storage-integrity TRs TR-MCP-DB-001..006, because every unit writes and reads its stored records through these components). Files and namespaces: `DbErrorClassifier` in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Errors/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Errors`), `SerializableWrite` in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Transactions/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Transactions`), `IdempotentCreate` and `PayloadFingerprint` in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Idempotency/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Idempotency`), `ReadableKeyAllocator` in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Keys/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Keys`), `PagedQuery` and `PageBounds` in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Paging/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Paging`) with `PageResult<TItem>` in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Paging/` (`QBrainAi.Client.Paging`), `SecretRedactor` in `src/QBrainAi.Services/Redaction/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Redaction`), and `ContentHasher` in `src/QBrainAi.Services/Hashing/` (`QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Hashing`). AC-001 `DbErrorClassifier.IsUniqueViolation(exception, out indexName)` recognizes SQL Server 2601 and 2627, PostgreSQL 23505, and SQLite 2067 and 1555, and `IsTransientConflict(exception)` recognizes SQL Server 1205, PostgreSQL 40001 and 40P01, and SQLite 5 and 6, each by provider code; a timeout or transport failure is never a transient conflict (`HandoffDbExceptions.IsCommitAmbiguous`, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/HandoffDbExceptions.cs:49` at `a8d3934b`, keeps its meaning), and new code never calls develop's message-text predicates (`SessionLogService.cs:234,357,812-817`) (`DbErrorClassifierProviderCodesTimeoutNeverTransient`, named here, one theory row per provider code); AC-002 `SerializableWrite` runs a unit's checks and save as one delegate in one Serializable transaction on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server, so a concurrent write to the same entity commits wholly before or after it (`SerializableWriteForcedInterleavingTestEntityCommitsWhollyBeforeOrAfter`, on a test entity SharedComponents owns, three providers); a transient conflict or a unique violation on a key the delegate declares rolls the transaction back and reruns the whole delegate at most three times (four attempts) unless the caller states its own bound and exhaustion outcome, then the unit answers 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and saves nothing (`SerializableWriteRetriesExhausted409ConflictRetryableNothingSaved`, three providers); a refusal the delegate returns rolls back and is returned unchanged; a command dispatched inside the delegate joins the caller's transaction and retry and never opens a second one; per-response writes increment each named response's `StatusVersion` in ascending `ResponseId` order as the transaction's first statements; on the EF in-memory provider the delegate runs once with no transaction, as develop does (`SessionLogService.cs:155`), and a caller-owned transaction is refused as develop refuses it (`SessionLogService.cs:119-128`) (`SerializableWriteInMemoryRunsOnceCallerTransactionRefused`, named here; the turn-write units keep develop's own cases for both rules, TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 AC-007, domain Turn persistence); AC-003 `IdempotentCreate` runs inside SerializableWrite and looks its key up before any precondition that can change between attempts; `PayloadFingerprint` is SHA-256 over the canonical JSON of the client-supplied inputs only, never over server-observed sources; an equal fingerprint returns the stored record with `created: false`, and another follows the caller's stated policy, `Conflict` (409 `conflict` with `retryable: false`), `ReturnStored`, or `MergeInto`; a unique violation on the key index ends the attempt, and the rerun's lookup finds the winner (`IdempotentCreateMismatchPoliciesConcurrentDuplicateFindsWinner`, named here; precedent `TodoPayloadFingerprint`, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/TodoPayloadFingerprint.cs:10`, and TR-HANDOFF-TODO-001); its soft-delete revive is TR-MCP-DB-003-AC001; AC-004 `ReadableKeyAllocator` allocates `NextOrdinal` keys (`<prefix>-<parent>-<nn>`: the suffix has at least two digits, starts at 01, is one more than the highest stored suffix for that prefix in the workspace compared as a number, and widens past 99) and `RandomHex` keys (`req-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>-<8 hex>`, the Phase A minter); a key longer than 128 characters shortens its embedded parent id by the rule of architecture 6.3 while the full root ids stay in their own columns; every key is per workspace, so two workspaces holding the same parent id get the same key string with no collision; a collision on the key's unique index declares a retry to SerializableWrite with the next candidate, under SerializableWrite's default bound and its 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, except that the Phase A minter states its own bound for a collision of its random request id only (six attempts, then 500 `mint_collision_exhausted`) (`ReadableKeyAllocatorRandomHexRequestIdFormatCollisionNextCandidate`, named here; `ReadableKeyAllocatorMaxLengthRootIdsFitAndStayDistinct`, three providers; `ReadableKeyAllocatorShortRootIdsKeepFullReadableForm`; `ReadableKeyAllocatorSameParentIdTwoWorkspacesSameKeyNoCollisionNoExhaustion`; `ReadableKeyAllocatorSuffixPast99WidensLatestGradeOrderKept`, at ResponsesAndTrust's gate because it needs that context's types, architecture 3.1 "Test placement"); its soft-deleted suffix rule is TR-MCP-DB-003-AC002; AC-005 `PagedQuery` counts the matches and reads one page in the caller's stable order into `PageResult<TItem>` (`totalCount`, `limit`, `offset`, `items`); `PageBounds` `Clamp` keeps QuerySessionLogsQuery's behavior (limit default 100, clamped to 1..1000, a negative offset read as 0, `SessionLogService.cs:476-477`), and `Refuse` answers a limit outside 1..1000 (default 50) or a negative offset with 400 `validation_error` and `details.field` `limit` or `offset` (`PagedQueryClampAndRefuseBounds`, named here; the per-query theory `PagedQueriesLimitOrOffsetOutOfBounds400NamesField` belongs to the agent-context catalog); AC-006 `SecretRedactor.Redact(text)` and `ContainsSecret(text)` apply the one rule set of `SessionLogSanitizer` and `ApiKeyRedaction` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/SessionLogSanitizer.cs:15`, `src/QBrainAi.Common.AgentCli/ApiKeyRedaction.cs:9`), so the sanitizer's review paths, the turn read projection, and the agent-context secret filter and secret scan redact alike (`SecretRedactorEverySanitizerAndApiKeyRuleRedactedAndDetected`, named here; the sanitizer's use of it is TR-MCP-SESSIONLOGSAN-001's, domain Turn reviews); AC-007 `ContentHasher` returns the lower-case hex SHA-256 of the UTF-8 bytes of a text's stored normal form, the form `McpDbContext` saves through `LineSanitizer.Sanitize`, which replaces U+2014 and U+2013 with an ASCII hyphen (`McpDbContext.cs:1351-1360,1704-1718`, `src/QBrainAi.Common.AgentCli/LineSanitizer.cs:23-28`), so a value read back from the store hashes as it was recorded (`ContentHasherTextWithEmAndEnDashesStoredAndReadBackHashEqual`). [SharedPlatform +AgentContextResponsesAndTrust for `ReadableKeyAllocatorSuffixPast99WidensLatestGradeOrderKept`]

#### Testing requirements

- TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 Shared platform components, dispatch scope, workspace resolution, soft-delete revive, global memory, federation domain, dump policies, and brain-slot admission scope. Tests: `DbErrorClassifierTests`, `SerializableWriteTests`, `IdempotentCreateTests`, `ReadableKeyAllocatorTests`, `PagedQueryTests`, `SecretRedactorTests`, `ContentHasherTests`, `WorkspaceResolutionBehaviorTests`, and `DispatchScopeTests` (named here: the architecture names the dispatch-scope cases, not their class) (SharedPlatform), the baseline classes `WorkspaceResolutionMiddlewareTests`, `MemoryGlobalDefaultWorkspaceTests`, and `DispatcherTests` (SharedPlatform), `WikiDumpG7OverlayTests` (AgentContextStorage), `FederationStateAdapterRegistryTests` (AgentContextContracts), and ContextGraph's projection suite (AgentContextGraphRag). baseline the `QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests` suite `DispatcherTests`, unchanged), TR-MCP-CQRS-005-AC001 (case `PipelineBehaviorsBothCompositionRootsWorkspaceResolutionBeforeModeGate`, named here), TR-MCP-MT-001-AC001 (case `WorkspaceResolutionParallelDispatchesTwoWorkspacesNoCrossWorkspaceRead`), TR-MCP-MT-002-AC001 (case `WorkspaceResolutionUnregisteredPath400EverySurface`), TR-MCP-MT-002-AC002 (case `WorkspaceResolutionGlobalMemoryCreateNoWorkspaceCreated`), TR-MCP-MT-002-AC003 (baseline-green: `WorkspaceResolutionMiddlewareTests.XWorkspacePathHeaderInvalidPathReturns400` and `NoHeaderNoKeyPassesThroughWithoutResolution`, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Middleware/WorkspaceResolutionMiddlewareTests.cs:110,145` at `a8d3934b`, with `ExistingHandlersProductionPipelineBehaviorUnchanged`), TR-MCP-MT-003-AC001 (case `WorkspaceResolutionRecordPathFiltersDbContextCreatedBeforeBehavior`, architecture 3.1 "Workspace and the database context"), TR-MCP-DB-003-AC001 (case `IdempotentCreateSoftDeletedKeyRevivedCreatedTrue`), TR-MCP-DB-003-AC002 (case `ReadableKeyAllocatorSoftDeletedSuffixNeverReused`), FR-MCP-MEMORY-001-AC006 (case `WorkspaceResolutionGlobalMemoryCreateNoWorkspaceCreated`; baseline `MemoryGlobalDefaultWorkspaceTests.ControllerGlobalCreateEmptyWorkspaceReturnsCreated`), FR-MCP-103-AC006 (cases `FederationStateAdapterRegistryTests.AddFederationStateAdaptersCoversRequiredDomains`, rewritten, and `FederationAgentContextRoutesLocalOnlyNeverQueuedNeverApplied`, named here), FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003-AC005 (case `WikiDumpAgentContextTablesExplicitPoliciesProjectionsOmitLocal`, named here; baseline `WikiDumpG7OverlayTests.DumpPolicyRegistryCoversEveryCurrentDbSetExactlyOnce`), FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003-AC006 (case `WikiDumpReviewReasonsCopySanitizeHistory`, architecture 3.1 "Review reason dump policy"), and FR-MCP-130-AC003 (case `ProjectionTrustedResponseIngestedNoBrainSlotAdmissionCheck`, named here, at ContextGraph's gate; baseline the Curiosity cases of TEST-MCP-178 and TEST-MCP-179, unchanged).
