#### Updated existing requirements

- TR-MCP-CQRS-001 (TR:696) "Standalone CQRS Library". Changes: the statement adds that `IDispatcher` is registered scoped, as a facade that resolves the handler and every behavior from the DI scope it was itself resolved in and runs them through the singleton `Dispatcher` (which keeps its logger-provider and active-dispatch bookkeeping), in place of today's singleton registration that resolves transient handlers from the root provider (`src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:22-27`, `src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/Dispatcher.cs:17,93,96`); the scope is the caller's (the HTTP request scope on REST and MCP Streamable HTTP, the MCP server's per-call scope on stdio through `McpServerOptions.ScopeRequests`, default on, and a scope a hosted service or the stdio host's startup work creates, `McpStdioHost.cs:340`); Phase 2 keeps today's registration and SharedComponents changes it (architecture 8.2); the published `QBrainAI.Cqrs` takes a major version for it (architecture 8.1: a consumer that resolves `IDispatcher` from the root provider or injects it into a singleton fails under DI scope validation, inference), and no `src` singleton injects `IDispatcher` at `a8d3934b` (its five users are `MemoryController`, `ProductsController`, `UseCasesController`, `FwhMcpTools.Memory.cs`, and the scoped `TransactionGatedMemoryService`, `Program.cs:484`). The stored status text "complete - 37 unit tests passing" is not one of the four statuses the REPL validator writes (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplYamlMessageValidator.cs:639`), so the update sends status `pending`. New AC: TR-MCP-CQRS-001-AC001 a dispatch made inside a handler or inside a SerializableWrite delegate shares the caller's `McpDbContext` and transaction, two parallel requests for two workspaces share no `McpDbContext`, and the existing UseCases, Products, Memory, and GraphRAG suites pass unchanged through the production composition of both roots [SharedPlatform].
- TR-MCP-CQRS-005 (TR:728) "Pipeline Behaviors". Changes: the statement drops "Built-in behaviors: `LoggingBehavior`, `ValidationBehavior`", which no code implements (at `a8d3934b` the only `IPipelineBehavior` is the test's `PrefixBehavior`, `tests/QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests/DispatcherTests.cs:74`, beside the registration helper `src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:80`), and names the first production behaviors, WorkspaceResolutionBehavior (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Workspaces/`, `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Workspaces`) and ModeGateBehavior (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/`, `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.AgentContext`, whose gating ACs are TR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009's), with request validation in the handler (architecture 3.5.1, "Order"); the same status note as TR-MCP-CQRS-001 applies. New AC: TR-MCP-CQRS-005-AC001 both behaviors are registered with `AddCqrsBehavior` in both composition roots (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Program.cs:489-490`, `src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/McpStdio/McpStdioHost.cs:319-320`) and run, for every command and query, after `SendAsync` or `QueryAsync` and before the handler, WorkspaceResolutionBehavior first [SharedPlatform].
- TR-MCP-MT-001 (TR:1488) "WorkspaceContext Scoped Per-Request Service". Changes: "Populated by `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware`" becomes populated per dispatch scope: by the middleware on REST before any controller, and by WorkspaceResolutionBehavior from a record's non-empty `WorkspacePath` through `WorkspaceIdentity` (`src/QBrainAi.Storage/WorkspaceIdentity.cs:22`) on every surface (the MCP tool's `workspacePath`, the REST controller's resolved workspace, the REPL session workspace), while a record with an empty path keeps the workspace its transport resolved (architecture 3.5.1). New AC: TR-MCP-MT-001-AC001 two dispatches running in parallel for two workspaces never read across workspaces [SharedPlatform].
- TR-MCP-MT-002 (TR:1495) "WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware". Changes: the statement is corrected to the code (it resolves `/qbrainai/*`, the `/mcpserver/*` aliases, and `/mcp-transport`, `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware.cs:85-91`: the `X-Workspace-Path` header, an unregistered path answered 400 `{ error }`, `:97-112`; else an API-key reverse lookup for callers without a Bearer token, `:121-151`; else a workspace-independent route (fourteen prefixes, `:33-47`) or a global memory create passes with an empty context, `:158-166`, and every other route is answered 404 `{ error }`, `:168-177`; the store text's `Mcp:RepoRoot` and primary-workspace fallbacks are not in the code, inference from those lines) and adds the dispatch-level rule of WorkspaceResolutionBehavior and `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Workspaces/`, `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Workspaces`) (architecture 3.5.1, and section 3.1 "Workspace failure"). New ACs: TR-MCP-MT-002-AC001 a record whose non-empty `WorkspacePath` names no registered workspace, or a record with no workspace from itself or its transport that does not implement `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace`, is refused after the dispatch with 400 `validation_error` and `details.field` `workspacePath`, the status this TR gives an unregistered header, in the `ClassifiedError` body on REST, the MCP tool error, and the REPL envelope [SharedPlatform]; -AC002 a record that implements `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace` runs with no workspace, and the behavior stamps none [SharedPlatform]; -AC003 the middleware keeps its answers and `{ error }` bodies before any controller, including its API-key resolution and the fourteen workspace-independent prefixes, and the behavior never refuses a dispatch develop serves today, so on REST a unit-level workspace refusal is reachable only through a record path naming an unregistered workspace (baseline-green) [SharedPlatform].
- TR-MCP-MT-003 (TR:1502) "EF Core Global Query Filter for WorkspaceId". Changes: the statement is corrected to the code: each workspace-owned entity has a named `Workspace` query filter (40 filters, `McpDbContext.cs:1115-1175`), an empty workspace id returns none of its rows, and the tool, tag, bucket, and agent-definition filters also return global rows with an empty `WorkspaceId` (`:1125-1128`), where the store text says an empty id "disables filtering" on "all 14 entity types". New AC: TR-MCP-MT-003-AC001 every query a handler runs filters by the workspace its dispatch resolved, whether the transport or WorkspaceResolutionBehavior resolved it, including through the `McpDbContext` the scope created before the behavior ran: after resolving, the behavior calls `OverrideWorkspaceId` on the dispatch scope's `McpDbContext`, as the REST middleware does, because the context captures its workspace at construction and the handler, with its context, is resolved before the pipeline runs (architecture 3.5.1 and 3.1 "Workspace and the database context"; `McpDbContext.cs:25-28,47`, `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware.cs:255`, `src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/Dispatcher.cs:93-107`; develop precedent case `XWorkspacePathHeaderOverridesDbContextWorkspaceId`, `WorkspaceResolutionMiddlewareTests.cs:91`) [SharedPlatform].
- FR-MCP-MEMORY-001 (FR:1498) "Global and workspace memory storage". Changes: the second AC ("Global memories are returned for every workspace and are not stamped with a workspace owner") is unchanged; one AC is added. New AC: FR-MCP-MEMORY-001-AC006 a global memory create dispatched with no workspace through the production pipeline of both composition roots runs, because its command implements `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace`, and stores no workspace owner (the develop case `ControllerGlobalCreateEmptyWorkspaceReturnsCreated`, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Memory/MemoryGlobalDefaultWorkspaceTests.cs:100` at `a8d3934b`, stays green, and builds its own dispatcher, so it never runs the behavior, recon req-c.md C4) [SharedPlatform].
- TR-MCP-DB-003 (TR:791) "Soft deletes for persistent MCP data". Changes: the statement adds that a create on a key a soft-deleted row holds revives that row (its deletion columns cleared) and writes it anew, and that a readable-key suffix a soft-deleted row holds is never allocated again, because the unique indexes cover deleted rows (`McpDbContext.cs:391,404,1302-1336`); develop revives a tombstoned session the same way (`SessionLogService.cs:161-169`, `SessionLogResubmissionReviveTests`) (architecture 3.1, "Soft delete", and 3.5.2). New ACs: TR-MCP-DB-003-AC001 IdempotentCreate reads past the soft-delete filter, and a soft-deleted key is revived and written as a new record with `created: true` [SharedPlatform]; -AC002 ReadableKeyAllocator reads past the soft-delete filter, so a deleted record's suffix is never allocated again [SharedPlatform]. The agent-context relationship revive (`CreateRelationshipAfterDeleteRevivedSameRelationshipIdCreatedTrue`) is FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-006's.
- FR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001 (FR:2325) "Normalized error envelope for REST, MCP, REPL, and plugins". Changes: no text change; P0.4 records in the record's notes that its one AC stays open (`isSatisfied: false`, as on develop) for the `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware` `{ error }` bodies (`WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware.cs:106-111,171-177`) and every existing body a unit keeps (for example the dialog route's `{ error }` 400, the "Invalid turn body." problem, and the "Invalid session identifier." problem), while every REST error of a unit this plan adds or changes is the `ClassifiedError` body (architecture section 3, Errors). AC it updates: its one AC (notes only).
- TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001 (TR:3216) "Shared error classifier and envelope". Changes: "persistence_error, validation_error, not_found, and conflict are retryable false unless SQLITE_BUSY or deadlock" is replaced by the retryable rule of architecture C9 and 3.5.2 (the architecture names this record as replaced); the classifier keeps `details.inner` for EF and provider failures (`McpErrorClassifier.cs:120-140`). New ACs: TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001-AC001 every handler this plan adds fails with `Result<T>.Failure(new ClassifiedErrorException(code, status, message, details, retryable))` (`ClassifiedErrorException` in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Errors/`, `QBrainAi.Client.Errors`), and `McpErrorClassifier` (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Services/McpErrorClassifier.cs:14`) maps it in one branch, with no per-feature branch, to the `ClassifiedError` problem+json body `{ type, title, status, detail, code, message, retryable, details }` and the MCP tool error `{ code, error, message, retryable, details }` [SharedPlatform]; -AC002 `ErrorCodes` (`src/QBrainAi.Client/Errors/`) is the one table of codes with their HTTP status and retryable flag, read by the server, the REPL envelope, and the plugin's receipt mapping, and only these are retryable: 503 `backend_unavailable`, 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` for a busy store outside SerializableWrite (today's answer, `McpErrorClassifier.cs:129-139`), and 409 `conflict` when SerializableWrite or key allocation exhausted its attempts, which is also the answer when a deadlock or a busy store outlasts them; 409 `conflict` for an idempotency key reused with other inputs is final, and so is every other code, the REPL client-policy codes `transaction_degraded` and `mutation_not_transactional` (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ClientMutationPolicy.cs:203-218`) included [SharedPlatform]; -AC003 the REPL classifier carries a server `ClassifiedError` answer through at every status, its `code`, `message`, `retryable`, and `details` reaching the REPL envelope unchanged, where today only a 503 passes the server's code through and `conflict` is forced to `retryable: false` (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplMcpErrorClassifier.cs:60-95`) and any other answer becomes a classifier code or `method_invocation_error` (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplCommandDispatcher.cs:3346-3370`); an exception without a server body keeps today's classification, so a raw unique violation it classifies itself stays `conflict` with `retryable: false` (architecture 3.5.2) [SharedPlatform].
- TEST-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001 (TEST:1315). Changes: the statement keeps its validation, not-found, persistence-with-inner, and backend-unavailable cases and adds "Also validates TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001-AC001 (case `McpErrorClassifierClassifiedErrorExceptionOneBranchRestAndMcpBodies`), -AC002 (case `ErrorCodesRetryableOnlyOutageBusyStoreAndExhaustedConflict`; baseline `McpErrorClassifierTests.ClassifySqliteBusyIsRetryablePersistenceError`, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/McpErrorClassifierTests.cs:47` at `a8d3934b`, and the deadlock-outside-SerializableWrite classifier cases `ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests.FromExceptionGraphMaterializationDeadlockIsBackendUnavailable`, `tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests.cs:77`, and `StorageCommandBudgetTests.ClassifyGraphMaterializationDeadlockIsBackendUnavailable`, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/StorageCommandBudgetTests.cs:66-68`), -AC003 (cases `ReplClassifierConflictRetryableTrueKept` and `ReplClassifierServerClassifiedErrorKeepsCodeRetryableDetailsEveryStatus`; baseline `ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests.FromExceptionDbUpdateUniqueIsConflictWithInner`, `tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests.cs:24`), and FR-SUPPORT-012-AC003 and FR-SUPPORT-010B-AC001 (case `ClassifiedErrorDomainRefusalNamesJsonPathInDetailsFieldNoErrorsMember`; baseline `SessionLogControllerTests.WhenPostingMalformedWorkspaceFieldThenReturnsProblemDetailsWithoutDtoKey`, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests/Controllers/SessionLogControllerTests.cs:235`)", with test classes `McpErrorClassifierTests`, `ErrorCodesTests`, `ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests`, `StorageCommandBudgetTests`, and `SessionLogControllerTests`. New ACs: TEST-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001 AC001..AC003, the three texts of the catalog's three-AC rule [SharedPlatform].
- FR-SUPPORT-012 (FR:2631) "SessionLog ProblemDetails Errors". Changes: the statement's "Error responses cite the offending JSON path under errors" and the third AC are qualified for the domain refusals this plan adds (architecture 3.4, Errors, which names this AC and its twin FR-SUPPORT-010B as replaced for those refusals only). Changed AC: FR-SUPPORT-012-AC003 a body-binding failure cites the offending JSON path under `errors` (today's `ValidationProblemDetails`, `InvalidModelStateResponseFactory`, `src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Program.cs:743-770`), and a domain refusal this plan adds (the review and request-class codes and the completion gate) names its field by JSON path in `details.field` of its `ClassifiedError` body and carries no `errors` member; neither ever names an action parameter [SharedPlatform]. The existing validation refusals of the session-log routes keep today's bodies, so TR-SUPPORT-LOG-010 ("error keys ... identify the JSON root or offending domain field") holds unchanged.
- FR-SUPPORT-010B (FR:2598), the store twin of FR-SUPPORT-012 with no ACs. Changes: the same statement qualification. New AC: FR-SUPPORT-010B-AC001 with the text of FR-SUPPORT-012-AC003 [SharedPlatform].
- FR-MCP-130 (FR:1055) "Transaction-gated Curiosity external result admission". Changed AC: FR-MCP-130-AC003 "Only CuriosityEngine may request GraphRAG/context admission" becomes "Only CuriosityEngine may request GraphRAG/context admission of brain-slot output; projecting a trusted agent response into GraphRAG (ProjectAgentResponsesCommand, FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-007) is no brain-slot admission", because the FR governs QuadBrain brain-slot output and `src/QBrainAi.GraphRag` holds no Curiosity check (architecture 6.1, which names this AC as updated) [AgentContextGraphRag].
- FR-MCP-103 (FR:825) "Hub-and-Spoke Federation". Changes: the first AC holds; one AC is added for the new mutable domain (architecture 6.1). New AC: FR-MCP-103-AC006 `agent_context` joins `FederationStateAdapterRegistry.RequiredDomains` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/FederationStateAdapters.cs:128-142`) as a local-only domain through the existing `LocalOnlyFederationStateAdapter` registration (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/FederationAdapters/FederationStateAdapterServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:23-37`), `InferDomain` maps `/qbrainai/agent-context` to it (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/FederationProxyService.cs:440-469`), a LocalProxy never queues it during a hub outage (`CanQueueDomain`, `:332-338`) and never applies it from hub fanout (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/FederationOperationApplyService.cs:32-39`), and forwards its routes live while the hub is reachable (`FederationProxyService.ProxyAsync`, `:78`) [AgentContextContracts: a Phase 2 shared file that completes behavior, so its case is recorded red in Phase 2 before the registration and Phase 3 keeps it green, architecture 8.3 and 3.1 "Test placement"].
- FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003 (FR:2537) "Optional wiki export JSON dump". Changes: the fourth AC (the registry covers every live `DbSet`) holds; two ACs are added for the new sets. New ACs: FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003-AC005 each agent-context `DbSet` has an explicit policy in `WikiDumpTablePolicyRegistry.PolicyFor` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/WikiDumpTablePolicyRegistry.cs:19-43`): `COPY-SANITIZE+HISTORY+INERT`, the HostileReview tables' policy (`:35-36`), for every agent-context table except `AgentContextProjections`, which is `OMIT-LOCAL` (`:28`) because its rows name GraphRAG documents of the source workspace (inference from architecture 6.1: a copied row would point at a document the destination does not hold), registered in the same change as the sets (StorageSchema) [AgentContextStorage]; -AC006 the Phase B review reason set `SessionLogTurnReviewReasons` (`SessionLogTurnReviewReasonEntity`, architecture 5.1) takes `COPY-SANITIZE+HISTORY`, the policy of the session-log child tables, listed explicitly beside them (`:29-31`), because the registry default `COPY-SANITIZE` (`:42`) would drop its history (architecture 3.1 "Review reason dump policy"), in the S3 change that registers the set (StorageSchema) [S3].

#### Test churn

Each item: the existing test, what changes, and the TEST record that owns the update (architecture 8.4: churn is a step of the gate of the change that causes it, never an exclusion).

- `FederationStateAdapterRegistryTests.AddFederationStateAdaptersCoversRequiredDomains` (`tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/FederationStateAdapterRegistryTests.cs:21` at `a8d3934b`, CA1707 spelling) asserts the exact local-only domain list (`:36-38`) and the exact domain collection (`:44-57`); both gain `agent_context` as local-only and not apply-supported in the Phase 2 change that registers the domain, with the rewritten case recorded red in Phase 2 before that change (architecture 3.1 "Test placement"). Owner: TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 (FR-MCP-103-AC006).
- Tests that dispatch through the production composition with a record `WorkspacePath` naming no registered workspace receive 400 `validation_error` from WorkspaceResolutionBehavior where develop runs the handler; their count is not measured at `a8d3934b` (inference: a test that builds its own dispatcher, as `MemoryGlobalDefaultWorkspaceTests` does, never runs the behavior). SharedComponents' G2 and G3 runs find them, and each registers its workspace in its fixture without weakening an assertion. Owner: TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 (TR-MCP-MT-002-AC001).
- Kept green, as evidence that the shared components change no develop outcome: the existing UseCases, Products, Memory, and GraphRAG suites through the production composition (`ExistingHandlersProductionPipelineBehaviorUnchanged`); `MemoryGlobalDefaultWorkspaceTests.ControllerGlobalCreateEmptyWorkspaceReturnsCreated` (`:100`); `WorkspaceResolutionMiddlewareTests.XWorkspacePathHeaderInvalidPathReturns400` and `NoHeaderNoKeyPassesThroughWithoutResolution` (`:110,145`); `ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests.FromExceptionDbUpdateUniqueIsConflictWithInner` (`:24`, a raw unique violation stays non-retryable `conflict`); `McpErrorClassifierTests.ClassifySqliteBusyIsRetryablePersistenceError` (`:47`); `ReplMcpErrorClassifierTests.FromExceptionGraphMaterializationDeadlockIsBackendUnavailable` (`:77`) and `StorageCommandBudgetTests.ClassifyGraphMaterializationDeadlockIsBackendUnavailable` (`tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/StorageCommandBudgetTests.cs:66-68`), because a deadlock outside SerializableWrite keeps its 503 at the classifiers (the service-level 1205 case whose outcome becomes the 409 is the Turn persistence domain's, `SessionLogDenseGraphTests.cs:100`); `WikiDumpG7OverlayTests.DumpPolicyRegistryCoversEveryCurrentDbSetExactlyOnce` (`:57`), because the registry reflects every `DbSet` and gives an unlisted one the default policy (`WikiDumpTablePolicyRegistry.cs:10-16,42`), so new sets never turn it red; and the `QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests` `DispatcherTests`, which resolve `IDispatcher` from a root provider built without scope validation (`tests/QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests/DispatcherTests.cs:88-96`, `BuildServiceProvider()` with no options), so the scoped registration keeps their outcome (inference: no test of that project enables `ValidateScopes`). Owners: TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 and TEST-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001, whose statements above cite each of these baselines with the AC it guards.

### Domain: Build tooling (Nuke; accountable through TR only, no TEST records)

Bounded contexts (architecture 8.2 and 8.3): BuildTooling (a Phase 4 work package that owns `build/`, including the infrastructure suite `build/Build.Tests`; it calls no C# component and runs in parallel with PluginCorePowerShell from the start of Phase 4, and HostPluginAdoption follows both), and the prerequisites P0.1a and P0.1c (TR-MCP-NUKE-011 to -014, before Phase 1). Use cases: none (FR-MCP-BUILD-001 is outside the D3 use-case scope, recon req-ab.md U1).

Rules of this domain (operator, 2026-10-10: "NUKE is only accountable through TR."; "FR: Project will provide tooling for repeatable builds." / "Now, the TR are implementation detail of the FR."; operator, 2026-10-11: "Nuke does not run its own test."): Nuke behavior is specified only in TR-MCP-NUKE-* TRs under FR-MCP-BUILD-001, each AC names the Build.Tests cases that verify it, and no TEST record and no FR AC cites a Build.Tests case. Build.Tests is infrastructure, never a unit test, never a Nuke target, and never in G2: it runs directly as the Build.Tests infrastructure step, `dotnet test build/Build.Tests`, when `build/` changes, the one exception to the Nuke rule (architecture 8.4). The analyzer branch moves the suite from `tests/Build.Tests` to `build/Build.Tests` (commit `fe5879ba` on `chore/netanalyzers-all`, which merges first, architecture 8.1) and places it outside the analyzer scope, so its cases keep their spelling, underscores included; a new Build.Tests case named in this domain is PascalCase. A case marked "named here" is named by this plan because the architecture states the rule without a case. The `PluginSessionLogIntegration` target, G3's runner of `tests/QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests` with skips as failures (`build/Build.PluginSessionLogIntegration.cs:12-100` at `a8d3934b`), gets no TR-MCP-NUKE record in this plan, because the plan uses it as it is and changes none of its behavior; no store TR covers it today (TR-MCP-NUKE-001, -002, and -005 to -010 do not), and its Build.Tests cases evidence FR-MCP-PLUGININT-001 instead, the pre-existing breach recon req-ab.md T8 records, which the `-WhatIf` ruling check of TR-MCP-NUKE-005 (TODO PLAN-BUILDWHATIF-001, outside this plan) reports.

#### Technical requirements

- TR-MCP-NUKE-003 Plugin sync: version, receipt, parent resolution, lockfiles, PowerShell-only scope, and the open-turn cache guard (Nuke `SyncAgentPlugins`, process SyncAgentPluginsAtVersion, architecture 4.4; implementation detail of FR-MCP-BUILD-001, no TEST record; operator, 2026-10-10; context BuildTooling). The `--plugin-version` parameter is new (develop computes the next minor version, `build/Build.SyncAgentPlugins.cs:63,89-111` at `a8d3934b`, and keeps doing so when the parameter is absent); this plan syncs once at `--plugin-version 1.120.0`, after the named prerequisite BUG-BUILD-008 has landed at 1.119.0, the version its unpushed branches of eight plugin repos and three installed caches already carry (architecture 8.4 and 8.5). Each repo the sync writes passes the packaged-path check `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnlyPackage` (`:244-245,405-433`), which all nine pass, and the core integrity check (`:244,387-403`, `plugins/core/sync/check-core-integrity.ps1`), which fails on any file `CORE-MANIFEST.yaml` lists whose SHA-256 differs; so a repo whose synced builders, tool descriptors, or other core files differ fails at its own sync step, while the receipt of AC-002 names `lib/plugin-hook.ps1` as the architecture states (this reconciles whatif-c.md WC-M14 with architecture 4.4; inference: the sync writes the manifest from the source files it copies). The sync ends by refreshing the installed host caches of the synced version (`RefreshKnownPluginCaches`, `:71,652-662`, develop behavior, kept), so it runs only at rollout and as a same-version re-sync, never in a gate (architecture 8.4). AC-001 `SyncAgentPlugins` syncs lib-ps to all nine hosts, the three TypeScript hosts included, and packs nothing (operator, 2026-10-11, PowerShell-only ruling), so it no longer calls `RefreshNodePluginCoreVendorPackages` (`:61`), writing the explicit, idempotent `--plugin-version` to all nine (a re-run at the same version refreshes the synced content and changes no version), and, for a TypeScript host that P0.7 records as loading compiled output, running that host's existing `npm run build` to compile its adapter (a build step of the sync, never a test); AC-002 a run fails when its receipt does not list exactly the nine repos or does not show every host at the target version with the `lib/plugin-hook.ps1` SHA-256 of `plugins/core/lib-ps/plugin-hook.ps1`; AC-003 the default plugin parent is TR-MCP-NUKE-006's `ResolveOfficialPluginSiblingParent`; without `--agent-plugin-parent`, a root with no sibling plugin repos finds none and fails the run (today the target logs a warning and returns, `:49-54`), and with the flag all nine are found; AC-004 the sync changes a TypeScript host's `package-lock.json` only by running npm in that host, never by writing or re-serializing the file itself, so `RewriteVersionedJson` no longer rewrites lockfiles and the version plan holds no lockfile (`:262,289,320-334,352`): the one-time removal of the packed-core dependency runs `npm uninstall` of that package in the host, and a version bump goes through `npm version <version> --no-git-tag-version`; after a sync `npm ci` succeeds in every TypeScript host and `npm install --package-lock-only` leaves its lockfile unchanged (operator, 2026-10-10: "Never edit an npm lockfile.  It belongs to npm.  If you want it changed, use npm."; BUG-BASELINE-012 found the 2026-10-01 sync leaving a stale integrity that `npm ci` rejected, fixed with `npm update @sharpninja/mcpserver-plugin-core`); AC-005 `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnly` (`:75-87`) applies an extension rule to every tracked path (`git ls-files`) of each of the nine repos, `tests/` included: on a TypeScript host it allows only the adapter entry file `src/index.ts` (the source of the `main: dist/index.js` that every TypeScript host's `package.json` names), `tsconfig.json`, `package.json`, and the npm-owned lockfile; the git-ignored `dist/` may hold only the compiler output of that entry file (`index.js`, `index.d.ts`, and their `.map` files) from the host's existing `npm run build` (`tsc`), and the git-ignored `node_modules/` holds installed dependencies, which are not plugin code and are not scanned; any other bats, bash, Node, TypeScript, or Python file fails the run; its content rule (the forbidden-reference scan, `:477-500`) covers only the packaged paths `lib/`, `hooks/`, `skills/`, `plugin.json`, and `CORE-MANIFEST.yaml` (`:418-422`), because over every tracked path it would fail 41 tracked documents and tests of the nine repos (each `ENFORCEMENT.md`, most `README.md` files, and `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1`, architecture 8.4); the tracked-tree run is not a step of the sync: it runs at HostPluginAdoption's exit, after that change removes the TypeScript hosts' TypeScript logic, Jest suites, and vendored Node core, while the sync runs the packaged-path check on each repo it writes (architecture 4.4 and 8.5) (operator, 2026-10-11: "You should be designing all scripts and script tools around PowerShell and only PowerShell."); AC-006 the cache guard `HasOpenPluginTurn` (`:666,692-726`), which today scans only `current-turn.yaml` files for `status: in_progress`, also treats a cache as holding an open turn when any `sessions/<hostSessionKey>/turn-state.yaml` under it lists a request in `openDelivered` (the per-host-session store of architecture 3.4 and 4.1), so `ReplacePluginCache` retains that cache in both cases (architecture 8.4, BuildTooling churn). Evidence, in the Build.Tests infrastructure step of BuildTooling (A9's sync scope): `SyncAgentPluginsExplicitVersionTests`, `SyncAgentPluginsTypeScriptHostsReceiveLibPsTests` (which also asserts that the sync makes no pack or vendor call), `SyncAgentPluginsHostNameTests`, `SyncAgentPluginsReceiptMixedVersionsOrHashFailsGate`, `SyncAgentPluginsParentResolutionTests`, `SyncAgentPluginsLockfileViaNpmTests` (with the rewritten `PlanPluginVersionUpdates_BumpsMinorAndSkipsNodeModules`, Test churn), `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnlyAllPathsIncludingTestsAdapterEntryOnlyTests`, `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnlyCompiledAdapterOutputAllowed`, `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnlyExtraNodeHelperSourceFails`, `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnlyExtraDistFileFails`, `ValidatePluginPowerShellOnlyContentRulePackagedPathsOnly` (named here: a tracked document or test naming bash passes, a packaged file naming bash fails), `SyncAgentPluginsRunsPackagedPathCheckNotTrackedTreeCheck` (named here), `ReplacePluginCache_OpenTurn_RetainsExistingCache` (existing, re-seeded per host session), and `ReplacePluginCacheLegacyCurrentTurnInProgressRetainsExistingCache` (named here: the legacy-file seed the existing case carries today). [A9; BuildTooling]
- TR-MCP-NUKE-004 Review-threshold config validation (Nuke `ConfigValidator`, `build/ConfigValidator.cs`; implementation detail of FR-MCP-BUILD-001, no TEST record; operator, 2026-10-10; context BuildTooling). Named prerequisite BUG-BUILD-005, fixed before the first G5 run under TR-MCP-NUKE-008 (which stays as it is, architecture 5.1): today `ConfigValidator` recognizes only a root `Mcp:` line (`build/ConfigValidator.cs:9-10` at `a8d3934b`) while the live `appsettings.yaml` root is `QBrainAi:` (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/appsettings.yaml:46`), so `./build.ps1 ValidateConfig` fails ("Missing 'Mcp' section", `build/Build.ValidateConfig.cs:21-22`). The review thresholds are declared under the canonical root as `QBrainAi: SessionLog: ReviewThresholds:` and read as `Mcp:SessionLog:ReviewThresholds:*` after `CanonicalSectionProjectionProvider` (architecture 5.1). The `AgentContext` ranges of architecture 6.2 are not a G5 check: `AgentContextOptions.Validate()` with `ValidateOnStart()` refuses a value outside them at server start and its G2 cases prove it, so `ValidateConfig` does not check them (architecture 6.2 and 8.4; this answers whatif-c.md Q9). AC-001 `ConfigValidator` (`./build.ps1 ValidateConfig`) rejects a review threshold outside [0,1] at `SessionLog:ReviewThresholds:{Agent|Hostile}:{Correctness|Completeness}` under the canonical `QBrainAi:` root, under the legacy `Mcp:` root, and in each per-instance override (`Instances:<name>:SessionLog:ReviewThresholds:*`, `McpInstanceResolver`) under either root, and where both roots set a key it checks the canonical value, as TR-MCP-NUKE-008 AC-003 resolves it (architecture 5.1 and 8.4). - TR-MCP-NUKE-011 (under FR-MCP-BUILD-001, no TEST record) Targeted test runs through Nuke: `Test` accepts `--test-projects` and `--test-filter`, the G1 route and G3's route to the integration and validation projects (architecture 8.4: no Nuke target runs `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests`, `QBrainAi.SessionLog.Validation`, `QBrainAi.Repl.IntegrationTests`, or `QBrainAi.Acid.IntegrationTests` today, because `Test` leaves out every `*.IntegrationTests` and `*.Validation` project and filters out `Category=Integration`, `build/Build.Test.cs:16-24,50`, `build/Build.TestDependencies.cs:60-61`, and `MigrationIntegrationTests` runs only `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests` and Build.Tests, `:70-74`); AC-001 `--test-projects` runs only the named projects, and a name may select a test or validation project that `Test` leaves out by default (`NukeTestTestProjectsRunsOnlyNamed`, `NukeTestTestProjectsSelectsDefaultExcludedIntegrationAndValidationProjects`, named here); AC-002 a name that matches no test or validation project of the solution fails before any test runs, and so does a name that selects `build/Build.Tests`, which no Nuke target runs (TR-MCP-NUKE-014; architecture 3.1 "Test placement") (`NukeTestUnknownProjectFailsBeforeRun`, `NukeTestTestProjectsBuildTestsRefusedBeforeRun`, named here); AC-003 `--test-filter` is appended as `(<category filter>)&(<filter>)`, and for a project that `Test` leaves out by default the category filter drops its `Category!=Integration` term, the trait the integration projects' classes carry (`NukeTestTestFilterCombinedWithCategory`, `NukeTestDefaultExcludedProjectCategoryFilterDropsIntegrationTerm`, named here); AC-004 without the parameters the target is unchanged (`NukeTestNoParametersUnchanged`, with the default-selection pins of `TestTarget_ExcludesPluginIntegrationFromUnitInventory`, `tests/Build.Tests/PluginSessionLogIntegrationTargetTests.cs:38` at `a8d3934b`, kept green). Every item runs in the Build.Tests infrastructure step of the change that causes it (architecture 8.4) and is accountable only through the TR-MCP-NUKE TR named, never through a TEST record. Line numbers are at `a8d3934b`, in `tests/Build.Tests/`, which the analyzer merge moves to `build/Build.Tests/` with the same lines.

- `BuildTargetTests.ReplacePluginCache_OpenTurn_RetainsExistingCache` (`BuildTargetTests.cs:569`) seeds `current-turn.yaml` at the cache root; it keeps its outcome with a per-host-session `turn-state.yaml` seed, and `ReplacePluginCacheLegacyCurrentTurnInProgressRetainsExistingCache` keeps the legacy-file seed (TR-MCP-NUKE-003 AC-006, BuildTooling). The case's summary label `TEST-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-002` (`:566`) and the `HasOpenPluginTurn` doc comment that names the same TEST id (`build/Build.SyncAgentPlugins.cs:692-695`) are re-pointed to TR-MCP-NUKE-003 AC-006 in that change; TEST-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-002's own statement ("ReplacePluginCache retains the current cache ...") stays true and unchanged.
- `BuildTargetTests.SyncAgentPlugins_RefreshesNodeCoreVendorPackageBeforeCaches` (`:139`, summary label `TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-PSONLY-001` at `:137`) asserts that the target calls `RefreshNodePluginCoreVendorPackages` before `RefreshKnownPluginCaches(pluginRoots, nextVersion)`; TR-MCP-NUKE-003 AC-001 removes that call, so BuildTooling removes the case with it, and `SyncAgentPluginsTypeScriptHostsReceiveLibPsTests` asserts the replacement (no pack or vendor call).
- `BuildTargetTests.PlanPluginVersionUpdates_BumpsMinorAndSkipsNodeModules` (`:471`) seeds a `package-lock.json` (`:482`) and asserts that the version plan updates it (`:493`); under TR-MCP-NUKE-003 AC-004 that assertion reverses (the plan holds no lockfile), and the rest of the case is unchanged (BuildTooling).
- Kept green until MAINT-LIBNODERETIRE-001 deletes the Node-core build code (`build/Build.SyncAgentPlugins.cs:502-650`, from the TR-MCP-SYNC-001 vendor-name constant to the `package.json` reference rewrite), and removed in that change, accountable through TR-MCP-NUKE-003 AC-001: the three source checks of `SyncAgentPluginsVendorNameTests` (`SyncAgentPluginsVendorNameTests.cs:25,37,50`, TEST-MCP-194's evidence), `BuildTargetTests.SyncAgentPlugins_EnsuresNodeCoreNpmCiBeforeNpmRunBuild` (`:103`), and `BuildTargetTests.NodePluginCoreHasTsc_RequiresLocalTscFile` (`:119`), because they read the vendor helper's source, which stays (unreached) until then (inference from their source-text assertions).

MAINT-LIBSHRETIRE-001 and MAINT-LIBNODERETIRE-001 delete those files, so each change drops the assertions over the deleted files and keeps the rest, among them the PowerShell core's legacy REPL command (`:235`) and the `tools/typescript/mcp-repl-ts` callers (`:256,266-267`); accountable through TR-MCP-NUKE-003 AC-001 (the PowerShell-only sync), as the plugin domain asks (inference: the 1.x alias rules of TR-MCP-QBRAIN-003 and TR-MCP-QBRAIN-005, which these cases cite, hold in the files that remain).

## TDD AC allocation by phase

This allocation follows the delivery structure of the architecture document section 8 (the four execution phases of 8.1 and 8.2, the bounded contexts and merge order of 8.3, the gates and case placement of 8.4); each scope entry below names its problem domain (the `### Domain:` sections of the requirements catalog) and the bounded context that delivers it, and a case that needs two contexts runs at the gate of the one that merges later in the rollout order (architecture 8.4).

Two kinds of AC. Baseline-green ACs document behavior that exists and passes today; this plan has exactly five (FR-SUPPORT-016 `ac-1`..`ac-4` and TR-SUPPORT-CORE-016 `ac-1`); they need a green receipt from the existing named tests run in P0 (`./build.ps1 Test --test-projects QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests --test-filter "FullyQualifiedName~SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests"`, the Nuke parameters of P0.1a, zero failures, zero skips), no RED, and must stay green at every later gate: those tests replace a turn with `completed` (`tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.cs:26,61` at `a8d3934b`), so from the CompletionGate enforcement change set (S10) on they stay green only through the per-project `WithCompletionEvidence(turn, requestClass)` fixture helper, which adds a class and terminal reviews and weakens no assertion (architecture 8.4, test churn). Every other AC is new or changed behavior and is assigned to the slice that writes its RED test first; extension slices add the same AC on their surface. Every listed new or changed AC is RED and then mocks-green in Phase 3, before any production code, and green at the gate of the Phase 4 work package that implements it (the slice label names the scope entry whose RED test first covers the AC); the phase HV verifies every new or changed AC of the phase has its RED and green receipts and every baseline-green AC its green receipts.

- Phase HV claims include: every new or changed AC above has a RED receipt (TRX or Pester result with the failing assertion) recorded on the Phase 3 TODO of "Execution organization" before code, and a green receipt at the gate of the work package that implements it; every baseline-green AC has its P0 green receipt and stays green at each phase gate (from the CompletionGate enforcement change set on, through `WithCompletionEvidence`, as stated above); any AC without its required receipts is a FAIL.

Phase C allocation: P0 allocates every AC of the agent-context catalog to the slice whose RED first covers it, by unit of work (architecture document section 6.5, each unit's owning context per section 8.3): AgentContextContracts the contract ACs (options, enums, DTOs, error codes, golden contract file `docs/context/agent-context-contract.json`); AgentContextStorage the storage ACs; AgentContextPackages BuildAgentContextPackageCommand, GetAgentContextPackageQuery, QueryAgentContextPackagesQuery, ReplayAgentContextPackageQuery and the relational path of RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery; AgentContextResponsesAndTrust SubmitAgentResponseCommand, GetAgentResponseQuery, QueryAgentResponsesQuery, RunDeterministicGradeCommand, RecordModelGradeCommand, DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand, RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, EvaluateStalenessCommand, CreateAgentContextHvRequestCommand, GetAgentContextHvRequestQuery, QueryAgentContextHvRequestsQuery; AgentContextRelationships CreateAgentContextRelationshipCommand, DeleteAgentContextRelationshipCommand, QueryAgentContextRelationshipsQuery; AgentContextGraphRag the GraphRAG path of RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery and ProjectAgentResponsesCommand; AgentContextServerSurfaces the surface ACs of FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-008, including the routes of GetAgentContextStatusQuery and GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery, whose handlers and handler cases are AgentContextPackages' and AgentContextResponsesAndTrust's (ContextPackages and ResponsesAndTrust deliver them, architecture 8.3); AgentContextPluginCore the plugin ACs and process RunStatelessInvocation; AgentContextExit documentation and the phase exit. The section 3.5 components the agent-context units call (SerializableWrite, IdempotentCreate, ReadableKeyAllocator, PagedQuery, ModeGateBehavior, SecretRedactor, ContentHasher) are SharedPlatform's, and a unit's tests mock their Phase 2 seams and never test their branches again (architecture 3.5). TR-MCP-NUKE-011, TR-MCP-NUKE-012, and TR-MCP-NUKE-013 are allocated to P0.1a, and TR-MCP-NUKE-014 to P0.1c.

## Wire contracts (documented in P0 before tests)

Phase A and Phase B: the wire contracts (per-turn outcomes, plugin receipts, hook statuses, the Stop gate text and remedy order, the ingress mint and failsafe mint record, the arrival bridge files, the plugin per-turn store, the Phase B turn shape, score lexemes, review error codes, sections, search, write isolation, and new surfaces) are defined in the architecture document section 3.4. The error rule is section 3 (Errors): every REST error of a unit this plan adds or changes is the `ClassifiedError` problem+json body raised as `ClassifiedErrorException` with its code, status, and retryable flag from `ErrorCodes` (namespace `QBrainAi.Client.Errors`, section 3.5.2) and mapped by `McpErrorClassifier`'s one branch; the completion gate is 400 `validation_error` whose message starts with the token `session_turn_completion_gate`; a SerializableWrite exhaustion is 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` and a reused idempotency key 409 `conflict` with `retryable: false`; a busy store outside SerializableWrite keeps 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Services/McpErrorClassifier.cs:129-139` at `a8d3934b`); a workspace that does not resolve is 400 `validation_error` with `details.field` `workspacePath` on every surface, while REST's `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware` keeps its own bodies; a body-binding failure keeps today's validation problem keyed by JSON path; and every body a unit keeps is stated per unit (sections 4.2 and 5.2). The typed failure codes the plugin maps, including the REPL policy's `transaction_degraded` and `mutation_not_transactional`, are C9 (section 3.3), and the turn statuses agent surfaces may write are `TurnStatus` (section 3.5.3: `in_progress`, `completed`, `failed`, `canceled`, and the legacy `cancelled`; `closed` and every other value refused with 400 `validation_error`).

Phase C
- Every model (options, entities, DTOs), unit-of-work request and response, endpoint (REST route with alias, MCP tool, client method, REPL `client.AgentContext.*` method, PowerShell builder, tool descriptor), and error code is defined in `docs/architecture/SessionTurn-Review-AgentContext-Architecture.md` sections 6.2-6.7 (identical in `docs/Stateless-Agent-Context-Management.md`), on the shared components of section 3.5 (the request pipeline of 3.5.1, the storage and error components of 3.5.2, and the agent-context components of 3.5.5), with one activity diagram per unit (GetAgentContextStatusQuery, BuildAgentContextPackageCommand, GetAgentContextPackageQuery, QueryAgentContextPackagesQuery, ReplayAgentContextPackageQuery, SubmitAgentResponseCommand, GetAgentResponseQuery, QueryAgentResponsesQuery, RunDeterministicGradeCommand, RecordModelGradeCommand, DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand, RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, EvaluateStalenessCommand, CreateAgentContextRelationshipCommand, DeleteAgentContextRelationshipCommand, QueryAgentContextRelationshipsQuery, RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, ProjectAgentResponsesCommand, process RunStatelessInvocation, GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery, CreateAgentContextHvRequestCommand, GetAgentContextHvRequestQuery, QueryAgentContextHvRequestsQuery) and one sequence diagram per command or query, each named by it. Phase 2 freezes them in `docs/context/agent-context-contract.json` before any Phase 3 test; any later change is a Phase 2 contract amendment recorded as a decision.
- Conventions taken from the live code (read-only survey 2026-10-11) and fixed by the architecture: problem+json errors through `ClassifiedErrorException` and `ErrorCodes` mapped by `McpErrorClassifier`'s one branch (section 3.5.2), string keys of 128 characters (the live convention is `{prefix}-{Guid:N}`; this plan deliberately uses the meaningful, bounded readable keys of the architecture document section 6.3 instead, allocated by ReadableKeyAllocator per workspace, section 3.5.2, operator 2026-10-11: "Please use human-readable, meaningful identifiers."), `DateTimeOffset *Utc` timestamps, 4NF child tables for lists that carry the root ids of every parent (section 6.3), idempotency through IdempotentCreate with PayloadFingerprint over unique indexes that include `WorkspaceId` and read past the soft-delete filter (the store has no concurrency tokens; section 3.5.2), per-response serialized writes through SerializableWrite, one Serializable transaction whose delegate reads and writes only the database (the pattern of `SessionLogService.cs:153-157` at `a8d3934b`; sections 3.5.2 and 6.3), paging through PagedQuery with `PageBounds` `Refuse` and the wire type `PageResult<TItem>` (section 3.5.2), hashes through ContentHasher over the stored normal form (section 3.5.2), MCP tools named `agent_context_<verb>` with `workspacePath` first and JSON-string bodies, REPL access through the `GenericClientPassthrough` allow-list (`workflow.*` namespaces are deprecated by FR-MCP-REPL-006) with the `agentContext` client namespace service-gated in `ClientMutationPolicy` and its eleven query methods on the read allowlist (section 6.5, "Transaction-gated REPL clients"), and YAML validators that always pass an explicit `payload.params.<key>` path.

## Scope catalog: shared platform and build tooling (delivered through the four execution phases)

SharedPlatform, scope entry (Shared components, architecture document sections 3.5.1 and 3.5.2). Domain: Shared platform: dispatch, workspace resolution, storage, and errors. Context: SharedComponents, second and alone in Phase 4, after StorageSchema (whose registered model its three-provider cases use) and before every other server context and ReplAndAgents (architecture 8.3); its Phase 2 seams and stubs are Phase 2 work (the two CQRS behaviors registered as pass-through stubs, and `IDispatcher` kept at today's registration until this context changes it, architecture 8.2). Contract: the scoped `IDispatcher` facade in `src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/` (published as `QBrainAI.Cqrs`; today a singleton that resolves handlers and behaviors from the root provider, `src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:24-25` and `src/QBrainAi.Cqrs/Dispatcher.cs:17,93,96` at `a8d3934b`); `WorkspaceResolutionBehavior` and `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Workspaces/`), registered with `AddCqrsBehavior` in both composition roots (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Program.cs:489-490`, `src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/McpStdio/McpStdioHost.cs:319-320`); `ModeGateBehavior` and `IModeGatedRequest` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/`); `DbErrorClassifier`, `SerializableWrite`, `IdempotentCreate` with `PayloadFingerprint`, `ReadableKeyAllocator`, `PagedQuery` and `PageBounds` (`src/QBrainAi.Storage/Errors/`, `Transactions/`, `Idempotency/`, `Keys/`, `Paging/`); `PageResult<TItem>`, `ClassifiedErrorException`, and `ErrorCodes` (`src/QBrainAi.Client/Paging/`, `src/QBrainAi.Client/Errors/`) with the one `ClassifiedErrorException` branch of `McpErrorClassifier` (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Services/McpErrorClassifier.cs:14`) and the REPL classifier's pass-through of a server `ClassifiedError` at every status (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplMcpErrorClassifier.cs:60-95` at `a8d3934b`, where the `conflict` branch forces `retryable` false at `:60-68` and only a 503 passes the server's code through); `SecretRedactor` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Redaction/`) and `ContentHasher` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/Hashing/`); each component with its one test class (architecture 3.5); after resolving, WorkspaceResolutionBehavior calls `OverrideWorkspaceId` on the dispatch scope's `McpDbContext`, as the REST middleware does (architecture 3.1, "Workspace and the database context"). Records (the Shared platform domain section): TR-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001 AC-001..AC-007, owned by TEST-MCP-SHAREDPLATFORM-001, and the updated TR-MCP-CQRS-001, TR-MCP-CQRS-005, TR-MCP-MT-001..003, TR-MCP-DB-003, FR-MCP-MEMORY-001, TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001, FR-SUPPORT-012, and TEST-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001 (new AC001..AC003). RED (each component's test class in G2, its three-provider cases in G3): `DbErrorClassifierProviderCodesTimeoutNeverTransient` (one theory row per provider code of section 3.5.2; a timeout or transport failure is never a transient conflict), `SerializableWriteInMemoryRunsOnceCallerTransactionRefused`, `IdempotentCreateMismatchPoliciesConcurrentDuplicateFindsWinner`, `ReadableKeyAllocatorRandomHexRequestIdFormatCollisionNextCandidate`, `PagedQueryClampAndRefuseBounds`, `SecretRedactorEverySanitizerAndApiKeyRuleRedactedAndDetected`, `PipelineBehaviorsBothCompositionRootsWorkspaceResolutionBeforeModeGate`, `WorkspaceResolutionRecordPathFiltersDbContextCreatedBeforeBehavior`, `McpErrorClassifierClassifiedErrorExceptionOneBranchRestAndMcpBodies`, `ErrorCodesRetryableOnlyOutageBusyStoreAndExhaustedConflict`, `ClassifiedErrorDomainRefusalNamesJsonPathInDetailsFieldNoErrorsMember`, `ReplClassifierServerClassifiedErrorKeepsCodeRetryableDetailsEveryStatus` (the REPL classifier carries a server `ClassifiedError`'s code, message, retryable flag, and details through at every status, architecture 3.5.2), `ExistingHandlersProductionPipelineBehaviorUnchanged` (the existing UseCases, Products, Memory, and GraphRAG suites run once through the production composition), `DispatchNestedSharesDbContextAndTransaction`, `DispatchParallelRequestsTwoWorkspacesNoSharedDbContext`, `WorkspaceResolutionUnregisteredPath400EverySurface`, `WorkspaceResolutionParallelDispatchesTwoWorkspacesNoCrossWorkspaceRead`, `WorkspaceResolutionGlobalMemoryCreateNoWorkspaceCreated`, `SerializableWriteForcedInterleavingTestEntityCommitsWhollyBeforeOrAfter` and `SerializableWriteRetriesExhausted409ConflictRetryableNothingSaved` (each on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server), `IdempotentCreateSoftDeletedKeyRevivedCreatedTrue`, `ReadableKeyAllocatorSoftDeletedSuffixNeverReused`, `ReadableKeyAllocatorMaxLengthRootIdsFitAndStayDistinct` (three providers), `ReadableKeyAllocatorShortRootIdsKeepFullReadableForm`, `ReadableKeyAllocatorSameParentIdTwoWorkspacesSameKeyNoCollisionNoExhaustion`, `ContentHasherTextWithEmAndEnDashesStoredAndReadBackHashEqual`, and `ReplClassifierConflictRetryableTrueKept`, with the baselines the Shared platform domain names (`WorkspaceResolutionMiddlewareTests`, `MemoryGlobalDefaultWorkspaceTests`, `DispatcherTests`, and the classifier baselines of TEST-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001). Cases of these components that need a later context run at that context's gate (architecture 8.4): `TurnWriteConcurrentDifferentTurnsSameSessionBothCommitNoConflictAnswer` at SessionTurnLogging's (A1), `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingChoreFlipVsRunningWriteRunningKept` at TurnReviews' (S4), `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingReviewResetVsCompleteNeverClosedWithoutReview` and `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingClassChangeVsCompleteGateSeesCommittedClass` at the CompletionGate enforcement change set's (S10), `BuildOnSqliteHookWriteDuringRetrievalNotBlocked` at ContextPackages' (AgentContextPackages), and `ReadableKeyAllocatorSuffixPast99WidensLatestGradeOrderKept` at ResponsesAndTrust's (AgentContextResponsesAndTrust; inference from the 8.4 rule, because the case orders grades). Test churn: the Shared platform domain's `#### Test churn`, carried by this entry's gate (architecture 8.4: churn is a step of the gate of the change that causes it, never an exclusion). Gates G1, G2, G3 (`./build.ps1 MigrationIntegrationTests --skip InstallTestDependencies` for the three-provider cases), G5; its exit runs green before any context that calls these components starts (architecture 8.4).

BuildTooling, scope entry (Nuke targets and the infrastructure suite `build/Build.Tests`). Domain: Build tooling (accountable only through the TR-MCP-NUKE TRs under FR-MCP-BUILD-001, no TEST record). A5 MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 Truthful plugin receipts. Domain: Turn persistence, decision lists, and closing. Context: PluginCorePowerShell. Contract (processes PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt and DrainFailsafe, architecture 4.4; the failsafe drain rules of architecture 3.1, "Failsafe drain": the quarantine is the one place for a record the drain cannot replay, a retryable answer stops the walk without consuming attempts, a final answer increments `drainAttempts` up to the budget of 5, the first-success drain trigger is removed, and nothing replays from the quarantine subdirectory; every retryable answer, a transport outage, 503 `backend_unavailable`, a busy store's 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true`, or 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, is a `queued` receipt with its failsafe record kept and exit 0, and a final code, the REPL policy's `transaction_degraded` and `mutation_not_transactional` included, is reported with that code and exit 1, architecture 3.4): `Get-ReplTurnWriteOutcome` (the plugin persists every turn verb through `client.SessionLog.SubmitAsync` with the one bound turn, `repl-invoke.ps1:1602-1736`, so the outcome is the submit `turns[]` entry for the bound requestId, while `appendDialog` and `setTurnTitle` map their own route results: 200 persisted, 404 `request_unknown`, outage queued); receipt `-Code -StoredStatus`; `Invoke-ReplPersistTurn` fails loudly on `skipped_terminal`; cached terminal status refuses verbs locally (`turn_immutable`); the close verbs remove a request from the open delivered list and the stop ledger only when the mapped outcome confirms the server close (TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 AC-007); the A3 alias rebind is extended to real queued writes through `Invoke-ReplPersistTurn`. RED: SessionTurnIntegrity Describe R4 (mocked `Invoke-ReplRaw` outcomes: `OutcomesSkippedTerminalPersistedFalseExitNonZero`, `OutcomesPerTurnMappingFromServerTurns`, `OutcomesDialogAndTitleRoutesReceiptMapping`, `OutcomesServerWithoutTurnsOutcomeVerifiedFalseNeverConfirmedClose`; a queued, refused, or failed close leaves the request open and ledgered while a confirmed close removes it (`CloseVerbOnlyServerConfirmedCloseLeavesOpenListAndLedger`); the drain rebinds real queued writes in original order through the persist path, and each A3 crash boundary loses no write (`AliasRebindQueuedWritesOriginalOrderThroughPersistPath`, `AliasJournalCrashBoundariesOneMintNoLostWrite` with real queued writes)); the retryable and final answers (`OutcomesRetryableAnswerQueuedFailsafeKeptExitZeroTurnStaysLedgered`, `OutcomesFinalServerCodeLiveVerbClearsRecordExitOne`, which rewrites the `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1:3386` case: a live final unknown code now clears the write-ahead record); a verb bound to a pending id drains first and never sends the pending id (`PendingTargetServerReachableVerbDrainsMintFirstNeverSendsPendingIdAllRoutes`); a dialog bound to a pending id is captured behind its mint record and never sent, and a dialog answered 404 is `request_unknown`, final (`DegradedDialogCapturedBehindMintRecordNeverSentNotFoundRequestUnknownFinal`; from S8/S9 also the missing `requestClass`, and S8 updates these cases' expected remedy accordingly as a RequirementRefined decision), the model's evidence and close calls are captured as `unsupported_session` failsafe records and nothing reaches the server, the next Stop warns and does not block, a restart keeps the records and the original (`sessionId`, `requestId`), and the next SessionStart drains them through the trusted recovery flow so the server shows the turn closed with that evidence; `UnsupportedSessionMintOrWriteForOtherTurnRefusedNothingCaptured`; the same from an earlier open turn for an unknown bridge record and a truncated transcript (`StopGateUnknownBridgeRecordEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedThenWarnsOnly`, `StopGateTruncatedTranscriptEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedThenWarnsOnly`), and for a synthetic `unsupportedSince` with any other reason (`StopGateSyntheticUnsupportedSinceAnyReasonEarlierOpenTurnCloseCapturedThenWarnsOnly`; the real duplicate-hook transition is an A8 and A9 case); before S10 the server has no completion gate, so A7 asserts only capture, the Stop warning, and a drain the server accepts (the review refusal is tested in S8 and S9, the gate-rejected drain in S10); each block/warn row incl. two consecutive Stop invocations with a still-open turn, the second carrying `stop_hook_active=true`, both blocking and a pass after the model closes the turn, a host-forced stop recording `stop_gate_overridden_by_host`, a request minted before a passing Stop and delivered after it blocking the next Stop until closed, across a restart (`StopGateEnqueueR2PassForR1DequeueR2NextStopBlocksUntilClosed`), and a crash injected before and after the atomic replace that delivers a request never leaves the ledger and the cursor disagreeing (`StopGateDeliveryCrashBoundariesLedgerAndCursorAgree`), and a passing Stop clears only confirmed-closed ledger entries while preserving the queue, cursor, `unsupportedSince`, and a concurrent delivery, across a crash (`StopGatePassClearsOnlyEligibleLedgerEntriesPreservesStateAcrossCrash`), `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` for a withdrawn in-progress request and none once the model has failed it, `duplicate_delivery_suspected` for two mints within 2 s with equal prompt hash and none for 2.1 s or differing hash, the gate blocks while a request turn opened since the last Stop is in progress and never calls completeTurn or failTurn (`StopGateBlocksDeliveredInProgressWithdrawnSeparatelyQueuedWarnsOnly`, `StopGateNeverCompletesOrFailsTurn`), the findings function does no IO (`StopGateFindingsIsPureNoIo`), hook-authored actions not evidence, the failed-build regression (the existing Pester test at `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1:2512`); a model close held in the ordinary failsafe by a transport outage turns the block into the degraded persistence warning while its ledger entry stays until the server confirms the close (`StopGateOutageCloseQueuedWarnsDegradedLedgerKeptUntilConfirmed`); with the arrival bridge dead, the Stop hook applies pending transcript records up to the transcript length at its start (payload `transcript_path`) and blocks on a request delivered but unclosed (`StopGateBridgeDeadAppliesPendingRecordsBlocksDeliveredUnclosed`); the close-verb evidence rule and remedies (`CloseVerbNoEvidenceRefusedTurnStaysInProgress`, `CloseVerbCommitOnlyEvidenceAccepted`, `StopGateClosedWithoutEvidenceElsewhereWarnsOnly`, `StopGateEveryBlockRemedyAcceptedOnBlockedTurnStatus`, `StopGateWithdrawnFailedPerInstructionNextStopPasses`, `StopGateEachRemedyFollowedLiterallyNextStopPasses`); the ledger is exactly the requests delivered since the last passing Stop plus every entry whose close the server has not confirmed, and the gate evaluates it together with the separate scan of `withdrawn` entries still `in_progress` (a request minted from `enqueue` and withdrawn by `popAll` before any delivery leaves the ledger empty, is blocked as `turn_withdrawn_unclosed`, and after it is failed by its original pair the next Stop passes; the block survives a restart and never appears in another host session: `StopGateWithdrawnBeforeDeliveryEmptyLedgerBlocksWithdrawnUnclosedFailByPairNextStopPasses`), and a turn the server reports terminal from another surface leaves the open list (`StopGateLedgerListsDeliveredSinceLastPassPlusUnconfirmedCloses`, `StopGateTurnClosedElsewhereDroppedFromOpenList`, `StopGateQueuedInProgressServerTurnNoBlockBeforeDeliveryWarns`; a withdrawn-before-delivery turn later caught by `unsupportedSince` is captured by its original pair, warned, and closed at the drain, with restart and other-host-session negatives: `StopGateWithdrawnBeforeDeliveryThenUnsupportedCloseCapturedByPairNextStopWarnsDrainCloses`); warnings for `nonce_source_missing` and a legacy `current-turn.yaml` turn (`StopGateNonceSourceMissingWarns`, `StopGateLegacyCurrentTurnInProgressWarned`; the earlier `StopGateUnboundLegacyFailsafeWarnedWithPath` is withdrawn, because a legacy record that names no request is quarantined by the drain and belongs to no open turn, architecture 3.1 "Quarantined legacy records"); ## Scope catalog: Phase B, request class, review records, thresholds (delivered through the four execution phases)

S1 MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-001 Shared contract seams (`src/QBrainAi.Client/SessionLog/`, namespace `QBrainAi.Client.SessionLog`, for the seams the server, the REPL, and McpAgent share; architecture 3.5). Domains: Turn reviews, scores, and thresholds; Request class and the completion gate. Contexts: the Phase 2 shared files (the review DTOs in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/SessionLog/` and the golden contract file, architecture 8.3) and TurnReviews (the seams' implementations and the section 3.5.4 components). Test churn: none (new seams and contracts only). Contracts: `RequestClasses`; `IRequestClassNormalizer` (policy RequestClassification, architecture 5.3); `ReviewStatuses`; `ICompletionPolicy.GetMissing(targetStatus, requestClass, agentStatus, hostileStatus)` (policy CompletionPolicy, the S1 completion-policy seam of architecture 5.3, `CompletionPolicyContractTests`); `SessionLogTurnReviewInput`, `SessionLogTurnReviewKind`, and `SessionLogTurnReviewDto` (defined once in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/SessionLog/`, namespace `QBrainAi.Client.Models.SessionLog`, and used by the server, architecture 5.1); `ITurnReviewValidator.Validate(kind, requestClass, priorStatus, input)` (policy ReviewValidation, the review validator seam of S1: per-status rules, transitions, otherNotes, receipts, ranges, exact error codes, in the check order of architecture 5.3); `ReviewThresholds` record; `ScoreVerdict` (namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Scoring`, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Scoring/`: derives `Pass` or `Fail` inclusively and returns the thresholds to stamp, architecture 3.5.4); `ReviewDefaults` (namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Reviews`, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Reviews/`, architecture 3.5.4); `ScoreLexeme` and `ScoreLexemeJsonConverter` (namespace `QBrainAi.Client.Scoring`, `src/QBrainAi.Client/Scoring/`, architecture 3.5.4); `UtcTimestampJsonConverter`; `TurnSections`; the gate message builder (a server type of TurnReviews, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Reviews/`, namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Reviews`, architecture 5.3); golden contract file `docs/context/session-turn-review-contract.json`, created and frozen in this slice's contract step before RED (request classes, review statuses, per-status required/forbidden fields, otherNotes rules, threshold derivation cases, completion-policy table, HV receipt mapping cases, error codes and gate message texts), read by C# `SessionTurnReviewGoldenContractTests` here and consumed unchanged by S8 (Pester); any later change to it is an S1 contract change recorded as a decision; the completion-gate refusal, a `ClassifiedErrorException` (namespace `QBrainAi.Client.Errors`, a SharedPlatform component) with 400 `validation_error` and the gate message builder's text, which starts with the token `session_turn_completion_gate` and lists the missing items in the order requestClass, agentReview, hostileReview (architecture 3.4 Errors and 5.3, policy CompletionGate; S5's surface cases carry it through `McpErrorClassifier`'s one `ClassifiedErrorException` branch, and S10's callers raise it). RED: one case per review error code (`TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.InvalidStatusReviewInvalidStatus`, `TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.FieldForbiddenForStatusReviewFieldForbiddenForStatus`, `TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.HostilePassFailWithoutPathsReviewReceiptPathsRequired`, `TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.FieldRequiredForStatusIncludingStatusOmittedFewerThanTwoScores`, `TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.FieldTooLongReviewFieldTooLong`, `TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.TimestampWithoutOffsetReviewTimestampInvalid`, `TurnReviewValidatorContractTests.BlankReasonReviewReasonBlank`, `RequestClassNormalizerContractTests.InvalidValueRequestClassInvalid`; new case names are PascalCase without underscores, CA1707), the golden field-to-code map in C# (`GoldenContractFieldToCodeMapAllSurfaces`) and its multi-violation cases, the first code in the check order of architecture 5.2 and 5.3 (`GoldenContractMultipleViolationsSameFirstCodeAllSurfaces`, run again on each surface by S5, S6, and S8), the completion policy's missing-class case (`CompletionPolicyMissingClassRequiresHostileReviewAsNonChore`, `CompletionPolicyContractTests`), the score lexeme cases in `ScoreLexemeTests` (19 and 28 digits, `review_score_format`, `review_score_precision`, `review_score_out_of_range`, an invalid score refused at deserialization before any lookup), CT suites per seam incl. boundary Theories (the derivation boundaries in `ScoreVerdictTests`, ScoreVerdict's one test class, architecture 3.5: 0.98 Pass, 0.9799999999999999999999999999 Fail, one score below Fail, per-kind thresholds, Other never derived, Chore flip and reverse, no auto-change past default, otherNotes rule (text rejected unless Other; Other with null, empty, or whitespace-only otherNotes rejected with `review_other_notes_required`), each per-status required/forbidden field, hostile receipt paths, transitions). S8 MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-008 Plugin core. Domains: Plugin core runners and host adoption; Turn reviews, scores, and thresholds (FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-005); Request class and the completion gate (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004); Turn persistence, decision lists, and closing (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003 and the clear-session coordinator, TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001). Contexts: PluginCorePowerShell (the core, processes ConvertHvReceiptToReview, ClearSession, and AdvisoryCompletionPrecheck, architecture 5.4) and HostPluginAdoption (each repo's `skills/session/SKILL.md` and `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1`). Test churn: the plugin-core review items of the Plugin core runners and host adoption domain's `#### Test churn`, and A7's R5 expected remedy, refined below, carried by this entry's G4 and G6. `McpPluginShim.psm1`: `ConvertTo-McpSessionDecimalString`, `Get-McpSessionTurnCompletionMissing`, `ConvertFrom-McpHvReceipt` (accuracy/100, completeness/100 as `[decimal]`, the verdict passed as `hvVerdict` for the server to map, reasons, reviewedAt, reviewer, receipt paths), `New-McpPluginTurnReview`, `-RequestClass`. `repl-invoke.ps1`: `Protect-ReplLosslessScalars` in `Convert-ReplParamsYamlToObject` (414-434) for `correctness`, `completeness`, `reviewedAt` (block, flow, JSON); `Invoke-WorkflowRecordReview` (targets the D2 invocation binding or the `requestId` override (required under D9 while two or more delivered requests are open), or for a turn of an earlier MCP session its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair (FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002-AC002, C5), which is how a turn that is no longer its session's write target is reviewed; works on terminal turns; no failsafe so an older queued review never overwrites a newer one, including in a host session with `unsupportedSince`, where a review is refused and recorded after the drain through the original pair (`UnsupportedSessionReviewRefusedNotCaptured`), the gate-rejected drain being an S10 case); requestClass on update/complete/fail; advisory local completion pre-check (reports the missing items with the exact gate message as a warning before the close is sent and never refuses a close; for an already open turn of a host session with `unsupportedSince`, the close goes to the failsafe per FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC001, FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC002); server gate rejection is reported with the server's message and missing items, keeps turn in_progress, and clears failsafe, including for a drained `unsupported_session` close; stop gate adds `session_turn_completion_gate` for a turn whose most recent close the server refused with that code. RED: new `SessionTurnReviewGate.Tests.ps1` (unsupported-session cases against a server double: a Code turn missing reviews is open, `unsupportedSince` is recorded, the advisory pre-check warns and the close goes into the failsafe with no server write, and Stop warns (`PrecheckUnsupportedSessionCodeTurnMissingReviewsCloseCapturedNoServerWrite`); a review in that session is refused and not captured (`UnsupportedSessionReviewRefusedNotCaptured`); the pre-check warns with the missing items and the exact gate message and the close is still sent (`PrecheckAdvisoryWarnsMissingItemsNeverRefuses`); when the server double refuses a close with 400 `session_turn_completion_gate`, the surface reports the server's message and missing items, the turn stays in progress, and the next Stop blocks with `session_turn_completion_gate` naming the server's missing items first (`StopGateServerGateRefusalBlocksWithServerMissingItems`); the real gate-rejected drain runs in S10; golden-contract cases read from `docs/context/session-turn-review-contract.json`, gate messages, HV mapping: AGREE 99/99 -> server-derived Pass at 0.99/0.99; AGREE 97/99 -> server-derived Fail; DISAGREE with scores deriving Fail -> Fail; DISAGREE with scores deriving Pass -> Other with otherNotes naming the failed claims; Codex HV receipt (reviewer JSON and response jsonl) accepted as input; lossless parsing in every YAML/JSON form, otherNotes rule, Unnecessary not writable, recordReview on completed turn, backfill pair cases `RecordReviewEarlierSessionPairAccepted`, `RecordReviewRequestIdAloneForEarlierSessionRefused`, `RecordReviewPairFromOtherConversationRefused`, `RecordReviewConcurrentSessionsTargetCapturedOnce`, pre-check case `PrecheckExternalWriterEvidenceCompletionAccepted` (a class or review written by another writer is not reported missing); an invalid HV receipt is refused before any write with the exact wire-contract code per field (`HvReceiptInvalidRefusedBeforeWriteExactCodes`, `HvReceiptDisagreeUnknownOnlyEmptyReasonsOtherNotesNamesUnknownClaims`, `HvReceiptDisagreeNoListedClaimsEmptyReasonsOtherNotesVerdictLevelReason`); the golden field-to-code map and its multi-violation cases in Pester (`GoldenContractFieldToCodeMapAllSurfaces`, `GoldenContractMultipleViolationsSameFirstCodeAllSurfaces`); against the server double, an open Code turn of a host session with `unsupportedSince` gets, before its close is captured, the reduced remedy naming only the missing model evidence, the missing `requestClass`, and the close call, never the terminal reviews (`StopGateUnsupportedSessionReducedRemedyEvidenceClassAndCloseNoReviews`), and the expected remedy of A7's R5 unsupported-session cases is updated the same way, and a Code turn of a supported session that lacks both model evidence and terminal reviews gets the accumulated remedy whose literal execution makes the next Stop pass (`StopGateCodeTurnMissingEvidenceAndReviewsAccumulatedRemedyNextStopPasses`, re-run against the real gate in S10); on a locally terminal turn `recordReview` and requestClass-only updates are accepted while other verbs stay refused with `turn_immutable` (`TerminalTurnRecordReviewAndClassOnlyAcceptedOtherVerbsTurnImmutable`)). Its plugin core reaches all nine plugins through the one Phase 4 sync at 1.120.0 (A9's explicit-version rule), so G6 runs for all nine; `./build.ps1 ValidatePluginPowerShellOnly --agent-plugin-parent /home/sharpninja/github`, plugin `skills/session/SKILL.md` and `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1`. Gates G4, G2, G3 PluginSessionLogIntegration, G5, G6. A close that carries evidence writes it first as a verified additive non-transition write and then the transition, so a gate refusal loses nothing (`CloseWithEvidenceEvidenceWriteVerifiedBeforeTransitionGateRefusalLosesNothing`, against a server double). Clear-session workflow (TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001; S8 ships the PowerShell clear-session coordinator runtime in the plugin core with its Pester RED cases in `SessionTurnReviewGate.Tests.ps1`, run in G4, and edits no skill text: S9 edits the skill in all nine repos once the TypeScript hosts reach this coordinator through their adapters): the coordinator enumerates every open request turn of the host session by its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair, reads its class, model evidence, reviews, and `blockers` section, and selects in this precedence: a withdrawn turn gets `failTurn` by original pair (reason "withdrawn before delivery"); else a turn marked blocked (its `blockers` section, the existing decision-list field of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC001, holds at least one nonblank model-authored entry, read for that turn by its original pair) gets `failTurn` with the first entry as its reason, even when it also meets the completion policy (`ClearSessionReadyAndBlockedBlockedFailsFirstWithBlockerReason`); else a turn that meets the S1 golden completion policy and the model-evidence check gets `completeTurn`; else it stays in progress, reported by requestId with its exact missing items (`ClearSessionGateUnmetTurnStaysInProgressReported`); `failed` requires `requestClass` only, and no review is ever fabricated (operator option A; `ClearSessionWithdrawnAndBlockedFailedByOriginalPairWithReasonsClassRequired`); a withdrawn or blocked turn that lacks model evidence or a class (missing or `Unclassified`) is not sent to `failTurn`: the coordinator reports it with its `queryText`, its fail reason, and the missing prerequisites in the Stop gate remedy order (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC009), and after the model writes them by the original pair within the same clear-session run, the coordinator's rerun reads them back and fails it with that reason (a withdrawn fixture with no model evidence and no class, and a blocked fixture whose class is `Unclassified`: `ClearSessionWithdrawnAndBlockedMissingEvidenceOrClassReportedWithQueryTextWrittenByPairRerunFails`); ordinary close verbs keep their advisory pre-check. A mixed case proves the selection: two ready turns close by their original pairs while one unready turn stays in progress with its exact missing items, and no review is fabricated (`ClearSessionMixedTurnsReadyClosedByOriginalPairUnreadyStayWithMissingItemsNoReviewFabricated`). An AGREE receipt that lists any FAIL or UNKNOWN claim is sent as `hvVerdict` DISAGREE (`HvReceiptAgreeWithFailOrUnknownClaimSentAsDisagree`).

S9 MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-009 Review and class surfaces on the TypeScript hosts through the PowerShell core (operator, 2026-10-11, PowerShell-only ruling: the pre-ruling S9, a TypeScript implementation in `plugins/core/lib-node`, is withdrawn; S8 already implements and tests the review and class verbs, lossless scores, the advisory pre-check, HV receipt mapping, the evidence-first close, and the clear-session coordinator once, for every host). Domain: Plugin core runners and host adoption (with TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC002 of the Turn persistence domain). Contexts: PluginCorePowerShell (`lib/tool-descriptors.json`, and the nine-repo clear-session parity case in `tests/QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests`, the project PluginCorePowerShell owns) and HostPluginAdoption (the TypeScript hosts' adoption and the clear-session skill text in all nine repos), architecture 8.3; BuildTooling has no part, because the clear-session evidence never runs in Build.Tests (architecture 3.1 "Test placement"). Test churn: the TypeScript-host items of the Plugin core runners and host adoption domain's `#### Test churn` (each host's Jest suites replaced by its Pester adapter suite in HostPluginAdoption's one change), carried by this entry's G6. Contract: `lib/tool-descriptors.json` gains the review and class entries (`requestClass` on the session update, complete, and fail tools; `session_record_review` targeting the D2 binding, the `requestId` override (required under D9 while two or more delivered requests are open), or for a turn of an earlier MCP session its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair, FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002-AC002, C5), with `correctness` and `completeness` declared as strings so no host hands its adapter a binary double (C10), each mapped to its S8 command. RED (Pester; `TypeScriptHostBindings.Tests.ps1` in the core and each TypeScript host's `tests/TypeScriptHostAdapter.Tests.ps1`): a 19- and a 28-digit score sent through each TypeScript host's adapter reaches the server double unchanged (`TsHostScores19And28DigitsArriveUnchanged`), the review and class descriptors map to the S8 commands (`ToolDescriptorsReviewAndClassMapToS8Commands`), and the cline-v2 and opencode stop-equivalents block on a server gate refusal, naming the server's missing items (`TsHostStopEquivalentServerGateRefusalBlocksWithServerMissingItems`).
