Its descriptors reach all nine plugins through the one Phase 4 sync at 1.120.0 (A9's explicit-version rule). Gates: G4; G6 for all nine plugins, because the sync touches all nine (Pester in all nine repos from HostPluginAdoption on, architecture 8.4); G2; G3 `./build.ps1 PluginSessionLogIntegration` (the nine-repo clear-session parity case below). S9 edits the clear-session skill text in all nine plugin repos at once (the six PowerShell hosts, for example `mcpserver-claude-code-plugin` `skills/clear-session/SKILL.md`, which today tells the model to complete the open turn, and cline, cline-v2, opencode, which reach the S8 coordinator through their adapters) to the selection steps, including the prerequisites-before-fail step (for each withdrawn or blocked turn the coordinator reports with missing prerequisites, write them by its original pair, a designDecision and then the class judged from its `queryText`, and run the coordinator again before the session-end step), which `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionAndGateSteps` asserts with the other selection steps, byte-identical across the nine (FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC002, TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC002, and the changed inline AC1 with its 8-to-9 byte-parity clause). The evidence is application evidence, so it runs in application suites and never in Build.Tests, which evidences only the TR-MCP-NUKE TRs (architecture 3.1 "Test placement"; the Turn persistence domain's TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001): RED `ClearSessionSkillListsSelectionAndGateSteps` in each plugin repo's `tests/Skills.Tests.ps1` (extending today's clear-session case, `mcpserver-claude-code-plugin/tests/Skills.Tests.ps1:29-33` at that repo's head), run in G6 through `./build.ps1 TestAgentPlugins`, the TypeScript hosts gaining the case with their Pester suites in HostPluginAdoption; and `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionAndGateSteps` in `tests/QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests`, which reads `skills/clear-session/SKILL.md` from the nine repos of the scenario catalog under the sibling plugin parent (`PluginSessionLogCatalog.SiblingPluginParent`, `tests/QBrainAi.PluginIntegration.Tests/PluginSessionLogCatalog.cs:36-57` at `a8d3934b`, which resolves `/home/sharpninja/github` from a worktree), run in G3 through `./build.ps1 PluginSessionLogIntegration`, failing, never skipping, when fewer than nine repos are found. S11 only re-runs them.

S10 MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-010 Server completion gate and origins. Domain: Request class and the completion gate. Contexts: TurnReviews (the CompletionGate enforcement change set, kept separate so that it merges and deploys after HostPluginAdoption, architecture 8.5), SessionTurnLogging (the S10 write-origin scope with the gate call in `SessionLogService.cs`, architecture 8.3), and ReplAndAgents (REPL `importRecovery`'s report of the turns the gate refuses, `src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/SessionLogWorkflow.cs:326`, and the optional `requestClass`, `agentReview`, and `hostileReview` arguments of QBAgent's `mcp_session_turn_complete`, `src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Services/QuadBrainInternalToolExecutor.cs:314-328` at `a8d3934b`; architecture 3.1, "Completion gate writers"). Test churn: the Request class domain's `#### Test churn`, carried in this change set's gate as a step and never as an exclusion (architecture 8.4: about 196 existing tests in 43 files write a `completed` or `failed` turn without a class or reviews, at least 120 of them on the real server path, and migrate through the per-project `WithCompletionEvidence(turn, requestClass)` helper; eight assert an outcome the gate reverses and are rewritten to the gated outcome, `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/SessionLogServiceTests.cs:538,1230,1261` and `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.IntegrationTests/Controllers/SessionLogControllerTests.cs:386,420,701,733,777` at `a8d3934b`; `SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests.cs:26,61` stay green through the helper; the PluginIntegration scenarios that complete turns record a class and reviews first; and `tests/QBrainAi.Repl.Core.Tests/SessionLogWorkflowProductionTests.cs:190`'s `completed-local` turn reaches the gate as `completed`). This plan adds no marker field, and the marker format and its signing scheme stay as they are; the signed `agentPlugins.contractDigest` content changes with A9's nine `agent_plugins` contracts, and TEST-MCP-189's lib-sh and lib-node marker resolvers retire under `MAINT-LIBSHRETIRE-001` and `MAINT-LIBNODERETIRE-001`, both recorded by the Plugin core runners and host adoption domain against FR-MCP-140, TR-MCP-SEC-005, and TEST-MCP-189 (recon `req-ab.md` C12). The server gate is the only completion authority; the 1.120.0 plugins' local pre-check and the REPL and McpAgent pre-checks stay advisory (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC002, TR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-004 AC-004, TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 AC-003), and the Stop completion-gate block fires only on a close the server refused (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003), so a rollback to a pre-S10 build needs no client change. `WriteOrigin` (Agent, Import, ServerInternal, Replication), `IWriteOriginAccessor` (internal AsyncLocal scope), `SessionTurnGateContext`, and the enforced policy CompletionGate (architecture 5.3), which returns the missing items of policy CompletionPolicy and rejects nothing itself: a single-turn mode rejects at once and SubmitSessionLogCommand once after every turn is evaluated, naming each failing requestId, with S1's completion-gate refusal (`ClassifiedErrorException`, 400 `validation_error`, message starting with `session_turn_completion_gate`); applied by `TurnWritePipeline` (architecture 3.5.3) on the upsert, PATCH, PUT, lifecycle, and submit paths, after policies ReviewValidation and RequestClassification, while the QBAgent terminal compliance check (`ValidateTerminalTurnCompliance`, `SessionLogService.cs:1329-1353` at `a8d3934b`) stays in the single-turn modes only, as today (the plugin, REPL-workflow, and McpAgent verbs close turns through the submit path, so the submit gate is the one they meet: `CompletionGateServiceTests.SubmitTransitionWithoutClassOrReviewsRejectedAtomicallyWholeSubmission`, `CompletionGateServiceTests.SubmitTwoTurnsFailGateErrorNamesEachRequestIdNothingPersisted`); trigger: a transition into `completed` or `failed`, or a Replace that carries either over any stored status, an already terminal turn included (`ReplaceCompletedTurnOmittingReviewsRefusedCompletionGateReviewsKept`); otherwise never gated (an additive write to a terminal turn, a class-only change on a terminal turn, `canceled`, and `cancelled` are never gated; a write naming `closed` never reaches the gate, `TurnStatus` refuses it); atomic rejection: SerializableWrite rolls the write back, so nothing of it is saved and the data context stays reusable (TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002 AC-004, architecture 5.3); the two forced interleavings that need the enforced gate run at this change set's gate on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server (`TurnWriteForcedInterleavingReviewResetVsCompleteNeverClosedWithoutReview`, `TurnWriteForcedInterleavingClassChangeVsCompleteGateSeesCommittedClass`, architecture 8.4). Exempt writers: `AuditedAgentCliClient`, `WorkspacePolicyService`, `QuadBrainOpenAiChatService` (ServerInternal, Chore), federation apply (Replication), `SessionLogIngestor` and `TranscriptSessionLogPersister` (Import). Gated: `QuadBrainInternalToolExecutor` (its `mcp_session_turn_complete` gains the optional `requestClass`, `agentReview`, and `hostileReview` arguments, TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004), REPL importRecovery (a recovered turn the gate refuses is reported in the import result with the gate's missing items and never written with a fabricated review; the REPL's status normalization stays, so `superseded`, the one normalized value outside the agent write set, is refused with 400 `validation_error`, `SessionLogWorkflow.cs:725-750` at `a8d3934b`; architecture 3.1). RED: the unsupported-session drain against the real gate (a drained Code close missing reviews is rejected with `session_turn_completion_gate`, stays listed with its original pair, a review through C5 succeeds, and a later close succeeds: `UnsupportedSessionCodeTurnDrainedCloseRejectedByGateStaysListedReviewViaPairThenCloses`); the accumulated Stop remedy against the real gate (`StopGateCodeTurnMissingEvidenceAndReviewsAccumulatedRemedyNextStopPasses`); the clear-session prerequisites-before-fail path against the real gate, run against the same `PluginSessionLogIntegration` branch server as the real-gate cases below (a direct `failTurn` of a turn that has model evidence and no class reaches the server and is refused with `session_turn_completion_gate` (agent surfaces cannot write `Unclassified`, and the gate treats a missing class and `Unclassified` alike), while clear-session reports a withdrawn turn with no model evidence and no class, never sends it to `failTurn`, and fails it with its reason, accepted, once the model has written its designDecision and class by the original pair: `RealGateClearSessionMissingEvidenceOrClassDirectFailRefusedPrerequisitesThenFailAccepted` in the PowerShell core); gate CT, service table Theory across every surface (incl. Fail and Other complete, failed needs class only, Chore with Unnecessary hostile completes, Code with Unstarted hostile blocked, legacy terminal enrichment not re-gated, `CompletionGateServiceTests.StandardAgentCompleteWithoutClassRejected`, `CompletionGateServiceTests.LifecycleCompleteAndFailEnforceGate`, `CompletionGateServiceTests.LifecycleCompleteNoClassReturns400`, the QBAgent-only evidence gate `CompletionGateServiceTests.EvidenceGateQbAgentOnlyStandardAgentNoEvidenceItemsCompletesWithClassAndReviews`, and the rewritten develop test `SessionLogServiceTests.UpsertTurnAsyncCompletedEmptyTurnAcceptedForClaudeCodeRejectedForQBAgent`, which TEST-MCP-SESSIONLOG-003 already cites, in place of a planned duplicate), the class-only change and exhaustion cases (`ClassOnlyChangeOnCompletedTurnLeavesPolicyUnmetAcceptedNotRegated`, `GatedCloseWriteBoundExhausted409ConflictRetryableNothingSaved`; the refusal body is S5's), the QBAgent tool gate (`QbAgentTurnCompleteToolWithoutClassOrReviewsRefusedWithThemCompletes`), the two `importRecovery` cases (`ImportRecoveryGateRefusedTurnsReportedWithMissingItemsNothingSavedNoReviewFabricated`, `ImportRecoverySupersededKeptByNormalizationServerRefusalSurfaced`, the REPL envelope carried by `ReplClassifierServerClassifiedErrorKeepsCodeRetryableDetailsEveryStatus`, architecture 3.5.2), `CompletionGateContractTests`, `WriteOriginTests`, `WriteOriginExemptionTests`, `WriteOriginArchitectureTests`, `QuadBrainSessionTurnToolGateTests`, and `SessionLogWorkflowImportRecoveryGateTests` (the Request class domain's TEST rows), origin tests, exemption tests, classifier tests, the QBAgent tool's optional arguments carried to CompleteSessionTurnCommand, `importRecovery`'s refused-turn report with the gate's missing items and the `superseded` refusal, architecture test forbidding origin scope in controllers/MCP/REPL/client/plugins. Fixture migration via a per-project `WithCompletionEvidence(turn, requestClass)` helper (adds class and terminal reviews; assertions never weakened), the Request class domain's test churn above. Gates G1, G2, G3 all integration suites, G4 (PowerShell core), G5, and G6 for all nine synced plugins, because S10 is the first slice whose server enforces the real completion gate against those plugins. Real-gate cases (a close missing class or reviews is refused by the server, the surface reports the server's message and missing items, the turn stays in progress, and the Stop gate or its equivalent blocks with `session_turn_completion_gate` naming the server's missing items first), run against the S10 branch server started by the `PluginSessionLogIntegration` host fixture, one per host and each only when that host's P0.2 row passed (otherwise its `<Host>P02NotPassedTurnLoggingUnsupportedZeroMintsZeroWrites` case): `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksClaudeCode`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksCowork`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksCodex`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksCopilot`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksGrok`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksGrokBot`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksCline`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksClineV2`, `RealGateCloseRefusedReportsServerMissingItemsStopBlocksOpenCode` (one PowerShell core; the TypeScript hosts through their adapters and stop-equivalents), plus `RealGateReplSurfacesServerRefusal` and `RealGateMcpAgentSurfacesServerRefusal`, and the accumulated Stop remedy under the real gate (`StopGateCodeTurnMissingEvidenceAndReviewsAccumulatedRemedyNextStopPasses`); zero failures and zero skips before the TurnReviews HV; the phase-wide checks re-run every plugin gate. Against the real gate, a rejected close's carried evidence (live or drained) is on the server before the refusal and survives a restart between the rejection and the corrected close, with every carried field read back (`RealGateRejectedCloseEvidenceSurvivesRestartReadBackEveryField`).

S11 MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-011 Documentation and exit. Domains: every Phase A and Phase B domain (their documentation). Context: Documentation (`docs/` and `templates/`, last in the merge order, architecture 8.3). Test churn: the documentation contract tests extended below. `docs/context/session-log-schema.md` (RequestTurn fields, TurnReview section, status lifecycle, thresholds, completion gate, Phase A outcomes, per-turn isolation, stop gate), `docs/context/session-log-workflow-api.md` (sections, aliases, item-key rule, recordReview, outcomes), `templates/prompt-templates.yaml` session protocol (classify, record reviews, close every turn), `docs/MCP-SERVER.md` config keys, and `docs/AGENT-PLUGIN-FEATURE-MATRIX.md` review rows (`requestClass`, `recordReview`, exact scores, and the completion pre-check per plugin, FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-008-AC005). RED: `SessionLogReviewDocsContractTests` (new, beside `PluginFeatureMatrixDocsContractTests` in `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Documentation/`, run by G2, never in `build/Build.Tests`) and `PluginFeatureMatrixDocsContractTests` extended to the review features; S11 re-runs, without owning, S9's `ClearSessionSkillListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (G6) and `ClearSessionSkillNineReposByteIdenticalListsSelectionAndGateSteps` (G3). Gates full G1-G6 (all nine plugins) with zero failures and zero skips, the Build.Tests infrastructure step only when this entry changes `build/` (it carries no clear-session evidence), and the G5 phase-exit rule (strict TR/TEST run within the P0 baseline; scoped use-case gate per operator D3). The phase-wide Phase 4 HV covers the Phase B changes with the rest of the plan; the phase and domain TODOs that deliver S1-S11 move to Complete on their own HV pass, and a phase-wide DISAGREE reopens the TODOs it names under the TODO transition rules of the BDPv4 contract.

## Scope catalog: Phase C, stateless agent context (delivered through the four execution phases)

These scope entries are delivered through the four execution phases of "Execution organization": their contract parts in Phase 2, their RED and mocks-green parts in Phase 3, and their real implementation, integration gates, and live checks in Phase 4 under the bounded contexts StorageSchema, SharedComponents (the section 3.5 components they call, scope entry SharedPlatform), ContextPackages, ResponsesAndTrust, ContextGraph, ReplAndAgents, PluginCorePowerShell, HostPluginAdoption, BuildTooling, and Documentation; the HV points are those of "Execution organization". Entry to domain and context: AgentContextContracts is Phase 2 (shared contracts, every agent-context domain), with its RED in Phase 3; AgentContextStorage is the Shared platform domain in StorageSchema; AgentContextPackages is the Context packages and token budget domain in ContextPackages; AgentContextResponsesAndTrust is the Responses, grading, trust, and response lifecycle domain in ResponsesAndTrust; AgentContextRelationships and AgentContextGraphRag are the Relationships, retrieval, and GraphRAG projection domain in ContextGraph; AgentContextServerSurfaces is the Agent-context surfaces, mode, and stateless invocation domain in Phase 2 (dispatch-only surfaces, `ClientMutationPolicy`, and the surface error mapping), with each unit's handler in the context that owns it (architecture 8.3); AgentContextPluginCore is that same domain in PluginCorePowerShell for the core and HostPluginAdoption for the hosts; AgentContextExit is Documentation. Contracts, diagrams, and units are in the architecture document section 6, on the shared components of section 3.5; each RED list below names the architecture's cases for its units (section 6, each code or branch with its case), and P0 writes each unit's ACs from the same sections (operator decision "Outline now, catalog in P0"; the domain sections list these cases as the input to the P0 catalog). A case that needs two contexts runs at the gate of the one that merges later in the rollout order (ContextPackages, ResponsesAndTrust, ContextGraph; architecture 8.4 and 8.5). Test churn: each entry carries its domain's `#### Test churn` items, where the domain section lists any, in its own gate (architecture 8.4).

AgentContextContracts, scope entry (Contracts). Domains: the four agent-context domains. Context: Phase 2 shared files (configuration, DTOs, JSON context registrations, golden contracts, architecture 8.3), RED in Phase 3. `AgentContextOptions` (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/AgentContextOptions.cs`, namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.AgentContext`, section `Mcp:AgentContext` declared under `QBrainAi: AgentContext:` in `appsettings.yaml` and projected by `CanonicalSectionProjectionProvider`, `Validate()`, `ValidateOnStart()`; its ranges are startup checks proven in G2, never a G5 `ValidateConfig` check, architecture 6.2 and 8.4), the enums, every DTO defined once in `src/QBrainAi.Client/Models/AgentContext/` (namespace `QBrainAi.Client.Models.AgentContext`) and used by the server (architecture 3.5 and 6.4, so no server and client copies), with `McpClientJsonContext` registration (type, `List<>`, `IReadOnlyList<>`, `PageResult<TItem>`), the error codes in `ErrorCodes` with their HTTP status and retryable flag (architecture 6.4, including `agent_context_transition_invalid`, `agent_context_relationship_protected`, 409 `conflict` with each `retryable` value, and the REPL policy's `transaction_degraded` and `mutation_not_transactional`), and the golden contract file `docs/context/agent-context-contract.json` (kinds, types, statuses, error codes, trust-rule cases, canonical rendering cases, token-estimate cases), frozen before RED. RED: options validation boundaries (`AgentContextOptionsTrustBelow098FailsStartup`, `AgentContextOptionsTrustAt098And1Accepted`, and one case per startup validation failure of architecture 6.2), source-generated round trips of every DTO (28-digit scores as lexemes), golden contract tests (kinds, types, statuses, codes, canonical rendering and token-estimate cases), trust-rule CT with an oracle and a mutant (trust on OR instead of AND; trust with one UNKNOWN). The `agent_context` federation domain registration (FR-MCP-103-AC006: `FederationStateAdapterRegistry.RequiredDomains` as a local-only domain, `InferDomain` mapping `/qbrainai/agent-context`) is a Phase 2 change that completes behavior rather than stubbing it, so its cases are recorded red in Phase 2 before the registration, and Phase 3 keeps them green (architecture 3.1 "Test placement"): `FederationAgentContextRoutesLocalOnlyNeverQueuedNeverApplied` and the rewritten `FederationStateAdapterRegistryTests.AddFederationStateAdaptersCoversRequiredDomains`. Gates G1, G2, G5.

AgentContextStorage, scope entry (Storage). Domain: Shared platform: dispatch, workspace resolution, storage, and errors. Context: StorageSchema (the registered model change, the migration of all three providers, and the dump policies, in the same change as the sets, architecture 8.3); Phase 2 writes the entity and configuration classes as compiling, unregistered classes (architecture 8.2). The entities of section 6.3 in `src/QBrainAi.Storage/Entities/AgentContext/` (namespace `QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Entities.AgentContext`) registered in `McpDbContext` (composite keys (`WorkspaceId`, key) and composite foreign keys, workspace query filters, indexes that include `WorkspaceId`, unique idempotency indexes, restrict deletes, `StampWorkspaceId`, the `ResolveWorkspaceIdForAddedEntity` cases that stamp a child from its parent, and the root-id columns of every child row), an explicit dump policy for each new `DbSet` in `WikiDumpTablePolicyRegistry.PolicyFor` (`COPY-SANITIZE+HISTORY+INERT`, except `AgentContextProjections`, `OMIT-LOCAL`; FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/WikiDumpTablePolicyRegistry.cs:19-43` at `a8d3934b`, architecture 6.1), migration `<utc>_AddAgentContext` per provider, and a 28-digit decimal round trip. RED: `RootIdsEveryAgentContextChildRowEqualsItsParents` (over every table of section 6.3), `MigrationsUpDownUpThreeProvidersSchemaAndDataEqual` (with the other two migrations, architecture 8.5), `WikiDumpAgentContextTablesExplicitPoliciesProjectionsOmitLocal` (FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003; the registry coverage test `tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Services/WikiDumpG7OverlayTests.cs:56-71` at `a8d3934b` stays green as a baseline and is not churn, because it cannot go red: the registry reflects every `DbSet` and gives an unlisted one `COPY-SANITIZE`, `src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/WikiDumpTablePolicyRegistry.cs:10-16,42`), and the 28-digit round trip on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server; the readable-key cases are SharedPlatform's (`ReadableKeyAllocatorTests`). AgentContextPackages, scope entry (Package construction (BuildAgentContextPackageCommand, GetAgentContextPackageQuery, QueryAgentContextPackagesQuery, ReplayAgentContextPackageQuery, relational RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, and the handler of GetAgentContextStatusQuery)). Domain: Context packages and token budget (the status query's FR-MCP-AGENTCONTEXT-009 cases with the Agent-context surfaces domain). Context: ContextPackages (`src/QBrainAi.Services/AgentContext/Sources/`, `Packages/`, `Packages/Commands/`, `Packages/Queries/`, architecture 8.3). Components (architecture 3.5.5): `SourceResolver` and `SourceKind` (`SourceResolver.ReadCommit` is the commit reader; a reference that names no existing source is 400 `validation_error` naming it), `PackageAssembler` (the secret filter through `SecretRedactor`, the omission filter, findings, the mandatory-context check, the nested loop FillBudgetWithCandidates, and rendering) with the candidate sources, renderer, and token estimator, `CandidateRanker`, and `BuiltPackageLookup`; the units read their sources before their SerializableWrite and the delegate stays database-only (architecture 3.1, "Database-only delegates"), and Build reads staleness through `IResponseStalenessRule` read-only and persists nothing but its package and links. RED: deterministic replay (same inputs, same commit, same `contentSha256`) from the immutable input snapshot only (`ReplayAfterConfigChangeIdentical`, `ReplayAfterOmittedSourceChangeIdentical`, `ReplayAfterRequirementTodoTurnProfileChangeIdentical`, `ReplaySourceWithEmAndEnDashesIdenticalTrue`, `ReplayNoInputSnapshot409ReplaySnapshotUnavailable`, `ReplayOtherBuilderVersion409ReplayBuilderChangedRecordedAndCurrent`, and the replay half of `SubmitOrReplayOverflowPackage409PackageOverflow`); the canonical rendering with findings after the items (`RenderFindingsAfterItemsCoveredByContentSha256`); mandatory profile completeness and its refusal (`BuildNoProfileOrRuleFile409ProfileUnavailable`), rule files outside version control kept as provenance only (`BuildUntrackedRuleSourceNoDerivedFromLinkHashKeptOnItem`), the request's own turn never a candidate (`BuildOwnTurnNotACandidateNoDerivedFromOwnTurn`), a turn of another agent (`BuildTurnOfOtherAgent404SessionTurn`), an unreadable commit (`BuildCommitUnreadable409CommitUnavailable`); ranking order and tie-breaks (score rounding half-even to 6 places with `sourceRef` ordinal tie-breaks giving the same order on Linux and Windows), contradiction and blocker findings, secret exclusion and redaction, workspace isolation, the per-kind source hash definitions; the budget and candidate limits (`BuildMandatoryEqualsBudgetBuiltOneMoreOverflows`, `FillBudgetCandidateEqualsRemainingIncluded`, `BuildCandidatesBeyondPerKindLimitOmittedBelowCandidateLimit`, `BuildMaxPriorResponsesZeroNoPriorResponseItem`, `BuildFocusRefNamesRejectedResponseOmittedRejected`); token overflow with stored `Overflow` and the same 422 on a retry or a concurrent build (`BuildRetryAfterLostOverflowResponseSame422StoredDiagnostics`, `BuildConcurrentOverflowBoth422`); idempotency and the database-only delegate (`BuildRetryAfterSourceChangedSameKeySameInputs200Stored`, `BuildDuplicateDeliverySameKey200StoredPackage`, two concurrent builds with one key give one package and the loser returns it, `BuildTurnStatusChangedDuringReadsReadsAgainThenSaves`, `BuildLinkWriteFailsNothingCommittedRetryCreatesLinks`, and `BuildOnSqliteHookWriteDuringRetrievalNotBlocked`, which needs a session-log write and runs at this context's gate, architecture 8.4); relational retrieval ranked by the turn and focus refs the request names, with every prior-response candidate checked for staleness read-only before it is returned (`RetrieveSameQueryTextTwoTurnNeighborhoodsDifferentRanking`, `RetrieveUnknownTurn404SessionTurn`, `RetrieveAfterEvidenceFileChangeResponseExcludedNothingWritten`; the `retrieval_disabled` and `graphrag_kinds_unsupported` fallbacks are AgentContextGraphRag's cases); package queries by the whole turn key (`QueryPackagesFiltersAgentSessionIdAndRequestId`); the status query (`StatusAfterDependencyChangeCountsStaleNotTrustedWritesNothing`, `StatusThousandTrustedResponsesAnswersWithinSessionStartHookBound`). The build cases whose ranking inputs come from GraphRAG (`BuildGraphRagScoreChangedSameSourcesDifferentInputsSha256`) run at ContextGraph's gate (AgentContextGraphRag), which merges later. Gates G1, G2, G3, G5.

AgentContextRelationships, scope entry (Relationships (CreateAgentContextRelationshipCommand, DeleteAgentContextRelationshipCommand, QueryAgentContextRelationshipsQuery)). AgentContextGraphRag, scope entry (GraphRAG (GraphRAG path of RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, ProjectAgentResponsesCommand)). Domain: Relationships, retrieval, and GraphRAG projection. Context: ContextGraph (the GraphRAG retrieval path behind the retrieval seam ContextPackages calls, architecture 8.3). RED against an `IGraphRagService` substitute: every fallback reason (`graphrag_disabled`, `graphrag_not_initialized`, `graphrag_not_indexed`, `index_in_progress`, `retrieval_disabled`, `graphrag_error`, and `graphrag_kinds_unsupported` for a request whose `kinds` is absent or names a kind other than `PriorResponse`) answered by the relational path with the same candidates the relational path gives directly (`RetrievePreferGraphRagUseForRetrievalFalseRelationalWithRetrievalDisabled`, `RetrieveGraphRagOtherKindsRequestedRelationalWithReason`, and the theory `RetrieveGraphRagUnavailableRelationalWithFallbackReason` over the status and error reasons); the GraphRAG path in `GraphRagStorageScope.Workspace` only, one hit per response, `Trusted` and fresh hits, ordered by score then `sourceRef` and cut to the limit (`RetrieveGraphRagOrderedByScoreThenSourceRefCutToLimit`, `RetrieveGraphRagDuplicateHitsForOneResponseReturnedOnce`, `ProjectionAndGraphRagRetrievalWorkspaceScopedNoCrossWorkspaceHit`); AgentContextServerSurfaces, scope entry (Server surfaces (GetAgentContextStatusQuery, GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery, and every route of the externally callable units GetAgentContextStatusQuery, BuildAgentContextPackageCommand, GetAgentContextPackageQuery, QueryAgentContextPackagesQuery, ReplayAgentContextPackageQuery, SubmitAgentResponseCommand, GetAgentResponseQuery, QueryAgentResponsesQuery, RunDeterministicGradeCommand, RecordModelGradeCommand, RejectAgentResponseCommand, SupersedeAgentResponseCommand, EvaluateStalenessCommand, CreateAgentContextRelationshipCommand, DeleteAgentContextRelationshipCommand, QueryAgentContextRelationshipsQuery, RetrieveAgentContextCandidatesQuery, ProjectAgentResponsesCommand, GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery, CreateAgentContextHvRequestCommand, GetAgentContextHvRequestQuery, QueryAgentContextHvRequestsQuery)). RED: one controller, MCP, client (`MockHttpHandler`), and REPL dispatch case per unit and per error code of section 6.4 (`WorkspaceResolutionUnregisteredPath400EverySurface`, `RouteIdUnknownInWorkspace404NotFoundNamesResource`, `PagedQueriesLimitOrOffsetOutOfBounds400NamesField`, `ModeOffEveryGatedCommand409AgentContextDisabledQueriesAnswer` over every gated command and every query), plus `/mcpserver` alias cases, the REPL policy cases (`ReplAgentContextReadsAllowedWhileDegradedWritesTransactionDegraded`, `ReplAgentContextWritesAllowedWhileTransactionsRequiredServiceGated`), the Mode gate's runtime reload and completeness (`ModeGateReloadedConfigurationOffRefusesWithoutRestart`: ModeGateBehavior and GetAgentContextStatusQuery read `Mode` through `IOptionsMonitor<AgentContextOptions>`, architecture 3.1 "Mode at runtime"; `ModeGateEveryAgentContextCommandExceptTrustDecisionImplementsMarker`, architecture 3.1 "Mode gate completeness"), and the hosted agent's `mcp_client_invoke` reaching `client.AgentContext.*` under the same units and policies (architecture 3.1 "Hosted agents"); the builder binding case is AgentContextPluginCore's. architecture 3.1 "Builder binding case"), the bridge-host trigger (`StatelessInvocationBridgeHostHookSyncsAndTakesBridgeMintedRequestId`, `StatelessInvocationBridgeHostEnqueueNotYetWrittenPromptRefused`, `StatelessInvocationAbsorbedMidTurnRunsAsPilotNeverRefused`), the Mode cache (`StatelessInvocationHostWithoutSessionStartModeReadAtFirstPrompt`, `StatelessInvocationModeUnreadableAtSessionStartAssumedOff`, `StatelessInvocationModeSwitchedOnMidSessionTakesEffectAtNextSessionStart`, `StatelessInvocationModeSwitchedOffMidSessionBuild409DisabledContinuesStatefulNoCancel`, `StatelessInvocationModeSwitchedOffBeforeCloseSubmit409DisabledTurnClosesNormally`, `StatelessInvocationHostCannotRefuseTreatedAsOffNoBuildFeatureMatrixRow`), the turn outcomes (`StatelessInvocationDuplicateDeliverySecondBuildReturnsStoredPackageNoSpuriousRefusal`, `StatelessInvocationMintOutagePendingTurnPromptRefusedCancelQueuedBehindMint`, `StatelessInvocationFinalMintRefusalPromptRefusedStopWarnsNamingPrompt`), the build errors (`StatelessInvocationBuildTransactionDegradedTreatedAsBuildError`, `StatelessInvocationOverflowRefusalAndFailureNoteCarryTokenCounts`, `StatelessPackageFailureCancelCloseQueuedByOutageStopWarnsDegradedDrainConfirmsAndClears`, `IsolationHooksNeverPersistCanceledExceptOwnTurnPackageFailure`), the submission and the whole turn key (`StatelessInvocationPackageIdLostFoundAgainByRequestId`, `StatelessInvocationPackageRecoveryFiltersWholeTurnKeyTwoLegacySessionsSameRequestId`, `StatelessInvocationIdempotencyKeyFromWholeTurnKeyTwoLegacySessionsTwoPackages`), the tool loop in `Stateless`, where a later model call of the same request keeps that request's own prompt, tool calls, and results while earlier requests stay excluded (`StatelessToolLoopOwnToolCallsKeptPriorRequestsExcluded`; architecture 3.1 "Tool loops in `Stateless`"), and: mode Off, Pilot, and Stateless paths, a package build failure (overflow or error, or a success response whose package is not `Built`, `StatelessInvocationPackageNotBuiltRefused`) refusing the prompt with the error shown and closing that request's own turn `canceled` with that reason, sent as the bound turn at status `canceled` with the error as its `failureNote` through SubmitSessionLogCommand on the persist path of PersistTurnWriteWithReceipt (operator: "Fail the invocation"), the only `canceled` a hook writes, removed from `openDelivered` and the Stop ledger after the server confirms (`StatelessPackageFailureClosesOwnTurnCanceledRemovedFromLedgerNoOtherTurnTouched`), a host that cannot refuse a prompt reporting the limitation, in Stateless, on a host whose P0.2 row records a history-exclusion operation, a model request that carries exactly the rendered package, the current request, and the host's `REQUIRED MEMORIES` block and no prior turn (`StatelessHostSentinelPriorTurnAbsentFromModelRequest`, run per supported host and asserted on the message list the host passes to its model call, captured by the host test harness and not by the adapter fixture, which records only what the adapter forwards, architecture 6.6; the Phase 4 live check repeats it on each host whose P0.2 row records history exclusion), and on a host without one `Stateless` treated as `Pilot` with the feature-matrix limitation and a `Stateful` submission (`StatelessHostWithoutHistoryExclusionTreatedAsPilotFeatureMatrixRow`), `invocationMode` on submission; the required-memory cases of the TypeScript hosts' adapter suites (`ClineV2BeforeModelForwardedRequiredMemoriesAddedOnce`, `OpenCodeChatMessageOutputCarriesRequiredMemories`, FR-MCP-MEMORY-007) run in G6 from HostPluginAdoption on (architecture 8.4). It reaches all nine plugins through the one Phase 4 sync at 1.120.0 (A9's explicit-version rule). AgentContextExit, scope entry (Documentation, pilot readiness, and exit). Domains: the four agent-context domains (their documentation). Context: Documentation (last in the merge order, architecture 8.3). `docs/Stateless-Agent-Context-Management.md` and `docs/architecture/SessionTurn-Review-AgentContext-Architecture.md` updated to the delivered code, `docs/MCP-SERVER.md` config keys, `docs/AGENT-PLUGIN-FEATURE-MATRIX.md` agent-context rows, `docs/context/agent-context-workflow-api.md`. Gates full G1-G6 on Linux (LAB-OMARCHY) and Windows (PAYTON-LEGION2 through the WSMan route) with zero failures and zero skips, the G5 phase-exit rule over the agent-context catalog, then the phase-wide Phase 4 HV.

## Breaking changes, rollout, backfill

No phase is purely additive (architecture document section 3, "Changes to existing behavior"). Every change this plan makes to existing behavior, listed once, each with the record or code it changes:

- Turn identity and arrival: hook-opened turns carry server-minted requestIds (format `req-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>-<8 hex>` instead of `req-<utc>-prompt-<4 hex>`, ReadableKeyAllocator `RandomHex`); the prompt-text dedupe is removed; a prompt that carries a hostile-review brief opens its own turn (replaces FR-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001's text-detected background-brief reuse, architecture 5.4); TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011 refined (slash commands open turns); `workflow.sessionlog.beginTurn` is retired from the plugin and refused with `mint_requires_host_ingress`, and the plugin's `openSession` opens the host session's recorded `mcpSessionId` and writes no `session-state.yaml` (architecture 4.1).
- Turn writes: new prompts no longer cancel turns, and the Stop gate no longer auto-closes; turn-level isolation: a single-turn write (upsert, patch, replace, complete, fail, the mint, the review record and reset) isolates its turn and that turn's children, a submission keeps its session-level Serializable transaction, and the session summary refresh after a single-turn write is its own short write whose failure never fails the turn write (architecture 3.1); agent surfaces (REST, MCP, client, REPL, plugin) write only `in_progress`, `completed`, `failed`, `canceled`, and the legacy `cancelled`, and a write naming `closed` or any other value is refused with 400 `validation_error`, `details.field` `status`, while server-internal writers and existing rows keep `closed` (`TurnStatus`, architecture 3.5.3).
- Completion gate (the CompletionGate enforcement change set): completions without class or terminal reviews are rejected for all hosts, McpAgent, external MCP callers, QBAgent's `mcp_session_turn_complete` (which gains the optional `requestClass`, `agentReview`, and `hostileReview` arguments of CompleteSessionTurnCommand), and REPL `importRecovery` (which reports each refused recovered turn with the gate's missing items and never fabricates a review; its status normalization stays, so a recovered `superseded` status is refused with 400 `validation_error`, `src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/SessionLogWorkflow.cs:725-750` at `a8d3934b`); the refusal is 400 `validation_error` with the `session_turn_completion_gate` token (architecture 3.1, "Completion gate writers", and 5.3).
- Storage answers: a deadlock or busy store that outlasts SerializableWrite's four attempts answers 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true` where develop answers 503 (`backend_unavailable` from `StorageGraphMaterializationException`, or a raw provider error), and the plugin keeps its failsafe record for it as for an outage, so the failsafe outcome of TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-005, TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001, and FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003 ac6 holds while their status changes from 503 to 409; a busy store outside SerializableWrite keeps 503 `persistence_error` with `retryable: true` (architecture 3.1, "Deadlock and busy outcomes"); the REPL classifier carries a server `ClassifiedError` answer through at every status, its `code`, `message`, `retryable`, and `details` reaching the REPL envelope unchanged, where today the `conflict` branch forces `retryable` false (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplMcpErrorClassifier.cs:60-68` at `a8d3934b`), only a 503 passes the server's code through (`:70-95`), and any other answer becomes a classifier code or `method_invocation_error` (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ReplCommandDispatcher.cs:3346-3370`); an exception without a server body keeps today's classification (TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001-AC003, architecture 3.5.2).
- Errors: every REST error of a unit this plan adds or changes is the `ClassifiedError` problem+json body from `ClassifiedErrorException` and `ErrorCodes`; a JSON document that does not parse answers 400 `validation_error`, where develop's classifier default answers 500 for a `JsonException` (`src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp/Services/McpErrorClassifier.cs:142-147` at `a8d3934b`); the domain review refusals name their field in `details.field` and carry no `errors` member (replaces, for those refusals only, the `errors` placement of FR-SUPPORT-012's third AC and FR-SUPPORT-010B); `WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware`'s bodies and every body a unit keeps stay as develop, so FR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001's envelope AC is recorded as deliberately left open for them (architecture section 3, Errors).
- Request pipeline: `IDispatcher` in the published `QBrainAI.Cqrs` becomes a scoped facade, and WorkspaceResolutionBehavior runs on every command and query, existing ones included (the UseCases, Products, Memory, and GraphRAG handlers), in both composition roots; a record path that names an unregistered workspace answers 400 `validation_error` with `details.field` `workspacePath` on every surface, a record that may run with no workspace declares `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace` (FR-MCP-MEMORY-001), and the behavior never refuses a dispatch develop serves today (architecture 3.5.1; TR-MCP-CQRS-001, TR-MCP-CQRS-005, TR-MCP-MT-001 to -003); the existing session-log routes dispatch through CQRS units whose Phase 2 handlers delegate to today's `ISessionLogService` members; `ISessionLogService` and `ISessionLogWorkflow` gain members.
- Published packages (architecture 8.1): `QBrainAI.Client` gains public API in Phase 2 (the new DTOs, `ClassifiedErrorException`, `ErrorCodes`, `PageResult<TItem>`, `ScoreLexeme`, and `ScoreLexemeJsonConverter`, beside the existing `McpClientException` family, which stays the typed error the client raises), a minor version effect; the scoped `IDispatcher` keeps every public signature, but a consumer that resolves it from the root provider or injects it into a singleton fails under DI scope validation (inference), a major version effect. Release version rule: `PackNuGet` packs `QBrainAI.Client`, `QBrainAI.Cqrs`, `QBrainAI.Cqrs.Mvvm`, `QBrainAI.Repl.Core`, and `QBrainAI.McpAgent` at one version (`build/Build.PackNuGet.cs:8-9,15-43,46-74` at `a8d3934b`), the first release after Phase 4 publishes both changes, and because FR-MCP-QBRAIN-004 ends the 1.x configuration aliases at 2.0 the operator sets that release's version at the release (Octopus), with release notes that name both changes.
- Plugin state and drain: the plugin cache layout changes to per-turn files and per-host-session `turn-state.yaml`; `current-turn.yaml` becomes a read-only legacy status file never used for targeting (replaces TR-MCP-REPL-014's and TEST-MCP-REPL-029's title targeting, TR-MCP-REPL-020's cache fallback, and FR-MCP-171's and TR-MCP-PERSIST-002's retention there), and code-verify writes `lastBuildStatus` into the write target's turn file (architecture 4.1); the workspace `session-state.yaml` keeps develop's trust and header fields and is never read for targeting (architecture 3.1 "Host session start state"); the prompt hook's no-session recovery (`Invoke-PluginNoSessionRecovery`, `plugins/core/lib-ps/plugin-hook.ps1:541` at `a8d3934b`) is replaced by process StartHostSession run for the hook's own key (architecture 3.1 "Prompt without a recorded session"); the degraded missing-turn recovery submit (FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001 AC004, TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001 AC003) is replaced: a dialog bound to a `pending-<nonce>` id is captured behind its mint record, and no plugin write creates a turn except the mint (architecture 3.1 "Degraded missing-turn dialog"); a live verb answered with a final unknown code now clears its write-ahead failsafe record and exits 1 (the turn persistence domain, rewriting `PluginPowerShellRuntime.Tests.ps1:3386`); compaction and resume keep the recorded MCP session, with no session-rotation rebind (replaces TR-MCP-PLUGIN-012's rebind, FR-MCP-XAGENT-001's same-agent rotation clause, and FR-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001's "completeTurn survives sessionId rebind"), and a resumed host session key revives its `session_end` marker at SessionStart (architecture 3.1); the failsafe drain keeps the quarantine as the one place for a record it cannot replay, stops at a retryable answer without consuming attempts, counts final answers against a budget of 5, no longer drains on the first successful verb (TEST-MCP-REPL-037), replays nothing from the quarantine subdirectory, and the Stop gate warns while the quarantine holds a record of an open turn (architecture 3.1, "Failsafe drain"; FR-MCP-REPL-011, TR-MCP-REPL-016, TR-MCP-REPL-017, FR-MCP-FAILSAFE-001, FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004).
- Stored data: soft delete is revived, not refused: IdempotentCreate and ReadableKeyAllocator read past the soft-delete filter, a soft-deleted key is revived and written anew, and a deleted suffix is never allocated again (TR-MCP-DB-003, architecture 3.1); every hash and byte-for-byte check this plan adds is computed over the stored normal form, because `McpDbContext` replaces em and en dashes in every saved string (`LineSanitizer.Sanitize`, `McpDbContext.cs:1351-1360,1704-1718` at `a8d3934b`; ContentHasher, architecture 3.5.2), so a client that hashes its raw text with those dashes gets another value (inference); search runs on the stored text, so a raw value redacted in a review field matches its turn, which reads back redacted (TR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002 AC-005 loses its "matches no turn" clause, architecture 3.4 Search).
- REPL policy (FR-MCP-120, TR-MCP-TXN-001): `ClientMutationPolicy.Evaluate` drops the `!ReadMethods.ContainsKey(clientName)` clause (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/ClientMutationPolicy.cs:213` at `a8d3934b`), and the `agentContext` namespace is service-gated with its reads on the read allowlist; no existing decision changes, because no develop namespace is in both lists (architecture 6.5).
- Tooling and hosts: validator/installer rewrite `~/.claude/settings.json` with backup; the TypeScript hosts' tool audit moves into the PowerShell core's `tool-audit` hook, keeping its `tool_call` and `tool_result` dialog items but writing no `design_decision` action per tool event (architecture 3.2, Tool-audit hook); the TypeScript hosts drop the packed Node core (removed with `npm uninstall`) and their jest suites for Pester adapter suites; `SyncAgentPlugins` replaces the Node-core pack with the lib-ps sync and syncs at an explicit version; `plugins/core/lib-node` and `plugins/core/lib-sh` retire under `MAINT-LIBNODERETIRE-001` and `MAINT-LIBSHRETIRE-001`; the marker's signed `agentPlugins.contractDigest` content changes with the nine `agent_plugins` contracts (format and signing unchanged).
- Rollout order (the Phase 4 order of "Execution organization" and architecture 8.5; the pre-merge validation deploy that the Phase 4 live checks use is repeated from the merged heads after the VERIFY PR merges): deploy the server with StorageSchema, SharedComponents, SessionTurnLogging, TurnReviews without the CompletionGate enforcement change set, ContextPackages, ResponsesAndTrust, ContextGraph, and ReplAndAgents merged (agent context Mode `Off`: the plugins stay stateful, every gated agent-context command answers 409 `agent_context_disabled`, and the read-only agent-context queries answer), together with a REPL release carrying A1's `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` and S6's `workflow.sessionlog.recordReview` (the plugins use whatever `qbrain-ai-repl` is installed and resolved: `lib-ps/ensure-repl.ps1`, used by all nine hosts, returns at once when an executable is already installed; so before each live check, the codex assertion, and every `PluginSessionLogIntegration` gate run (its plugin processes use the installed REPL unless `MCP_REPL_EXECUTABLE` names one, `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1:674-680`), the branch build is installed and selected explicitly with `./build.ps1 InstallReplTool --update-tool` from the branch head, and the receipt records the executable path, its version or commit, and that `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` and `workflow.sessionlog.recordReview` answer; QB-AGENT-001's pin follows the C12 amendment) -> merge PluginCorePowerShell and BuildTooling -> SyncAgentPlugins once at 1.120.0 for all nine plugins (after the named prerequisite BUG-BUILD-008 has landed at 1.119.0; the sync carries the Phase A, B, and C plugin work and validates each repo it writes with the packaged-path check) + the installed-equals-synced step, which the sync's own refresh of the installed caches of that version begins + validator repair + Claude Code restart + operator trust of the codex plugin hooks in Codex (codex reports `turn_logging_unsupported` until the codex live assertion passes; the plugins' local pre-check only warns and never refuses a close, and their Stop completion-gate block fires only on a close the server refused, so they need no activation signal); a same-version re-sync, which refreshes content and never bumps the version, runs only when a later Phase 4 fix changes `plugins/core/lib-ps` -> merge HostPluginAdoption, whose exit runs `./build.ps1 ValidatePluginPowerShellOnly --agent-plugin-parent /home/sharpninja/github` over the tracked trees of all nine repos -> `MAINT-LIBSHRETIRE-001`, and, once Codex ACKs the 2026-10-12 PowerShell-only CONTRACT entry or the operator resolves it, `MAINT-LIBNODERETIRE-001` -> merge and deploy the CompletionGate enforcement change set (the server gate becomes the only completion authority; a rollback to the deploy before it needs no client change and no marker change; its deploy receipt lists the workspace's turns in progress, read with QuerySessionLogsQuery at status `in_progress`, and nothing is backfilled, architecture 3.1 "Turns open at the CompletionGate deploy") -> Documentation -> the integration checks (architecture 8.5). The deploy receipt records the commit SHA of the deploy before the CompletionGate change set (a fixed SHA, valid whatever merge method the PRs use), and a gate rollback goes first to that SHA. Server deploy only via the Nuke `UpdateService` target, with operator approval, run elevated as the build source requires (`build/Build.UpdateService.cs:40` documents `sudo --chdir . pwsh -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ./build.ps1 UpdateService`, and `UpdateLinuxService` refuses on Linux unless `id -u` is 0, `:228-232`, as `LinuxServiceHelper.Update` does again, `build/LinuxServiceHelper.cs:52-53`). Deploy procedure on this host (used by the rollout and by both live checks): as uid 1000, `./build.ps1 Publish --configuration Release` (the Nuke target writes `<absolute worktree>/artifacts/qbrain-ai`, which is the `<publishDir>` below; operator 2026-10-11: builds run through Nuke, never `dotnet publish` directly); then, from the worktree directory (no `--chdir`), `sudo env "PATH=/home/sharpninja/.dotnet:$($env:PATH)" "DOTNET_ROOT=/home/sharpninja/.dotnet" /home/sharpninja/.local/bin/pwsh.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File <absolute worktree>/build.ps1 UpdateService --skip-build --publish-source <publishDir> --skip-version-bump`, with `pwsh.exe` resolved to its absolute path on whichever host deploys. The command is issued from PowerShell, so the current PATH is passed as `$($env:PATH)` (a Bash-style `$PATH` is an empty PowerShell variable and would drop `/usr/bin`); before the deploy a read-only check, `env "PATH=/home/sharpninja/.dotnet:$($env:PATH)" /home/sharpninja/.local/bin/pwsh.exe -NoProfile -NonInteractive -Command '(Get-Command -CommandType Application id, dotnet).Source; dotnet --list-sdks'`, records that `id` and `dotnet` resolve and that 10.0.201 is listed (PowerShell only: no shell script runs in the deploy). The operator answers any password prompt and the agent never enters one; the elevated run records `id -u` = 0 and `dotnet --list-sdks`, and the deploy receipt records the Nuke target outcome; afterwards `git status --porcelain` and a check for root-owned files in the worktree must both be clean (if not, the operator restores ownership before work continues). The deploy runs before the matching plugin sync (plugins never ship ahead of the server they call). Rollback: `UpdateService` to the prior build leaves 1.120.0 plugins on the unsupported-mint path of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008 until the server returns; migrations roll back only through their tested Down (Up, Down, and Up on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server with schema and data equal after the second Up, `MigrationsUpDownUpThreeProvidersSchemaAndDataEqual`); a binary-only rollback while the schema stays at the new migrations is safe for turns, because the review status columns carry the database default `Unstarted` and `RequestClass` the default `Unclassified`, so a turn the prior binary inserts reads as its ReviewDefaults after the roll-forward, which runs no second backfill (`RollbackPriorBinaryInsertsTurnRollForwardReadsDefaults`), and the prior binary never writes the Phase C tables (architecture 8.5).
- Backfill: `RequestClass='Unclassified'`, both review statuses `Unstarted` on existing rows, soft-deleted rows included, with the same values as database defaults (architecture 5.1). Existing twin/canceled turns are not rewritten (this session's were remapped manually).
- Phase C is not purely additive (architecture section 3, "Changes to existing behavior"): its own surface is new tables, routes, tools, client members, and plugin builders, with `Mcp:AgentContext:Mode` defaulting to `Off`, so agents behave statefully as today until the operator sets `Pilot`; but the shared components it brings change existing behavior in every Mode, listed once above (the scoped `IDispatcher` and WorkspaceResolutionBehavior, the retryable 409, the `QBrainAI.Client` API growth, the `ClientMutationPolicy` change, soft-delete revive, and the stored normal form in hashes), and it adds `agent_context` to the federation `RequiredDomains` as a local-only domain (FR-MCP-103), a dump policy per new table (FR-MCP-WIKIEXPORT-003), and FR-MCP-130 AC 3's wording narrowed to brain-slot output (architecture 6.1). Rollout (architecture 8.5): deploy the server with the agent-context server contexts (Mode `Off`), then the one Phase 4 plugin sync (1.120.0) after PluginCorePowerShell, then `Pilot` on the shared servers only on the operator's instruction, taking effect at each host session's next start and only on hosts whose prompt hook can refuse a prompt (P0.2 probe (j); any other host runs `Pilot` and `Stateless` as `Off` with its feature-matrix limitation), then the pilot report (GetAgentContextPilotReportQuery) to the operator; `Stateless` as a default is a later operator decision taken from that report. Rollback: Mode `Off` stops agent-context behavior without a deploy, and a running host session whose plugin cached `Pilot` or `Stateless` gets 409 `agent_context_disabled` at its next build or submission, caches `Off`, and continues statefully (no refusal, no `canceled` close); the stored records and the pilot report stay readable in `Off` for audit; the migration rolls back only through its tested Down. Server deploys use the Nuke `UpdateService` target with operator approval; releases use Octopus only (container on LAB-OMARCHY).

## Risks and incidental bugs (file via triage, not in scope)

- Risks: SQLite cannot translate decimal comparisons (no score filters); PostgreSQL `reviewedAt` microsecond precision; the arrival bridge depends on Claude Code's transcript `queue-operation` record format and lifecycle (observed 2026-10-09 in this workspace's transcripts: 117 `enqueue`, 79 `dequeue`, 35 `remove` of which 27 carry `absorbed_mid_turn`, 2 `popAll`; not a documented contract), handled by the lifecycle in FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC010 and the `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` block, so A3 pins the observed line shapes (including `remove` with and without a reason and `popAll`) in fixture tests and the bridge reports `arrival_bridge_format_unrecognized` (stop-gate warning) instead of guessing when the format changes; Linux file locks are advisory (FileShare.None works between .NET processes).
- Triage (already filed this session): `triage-report-d3bcb648a72642f0a52c68c801316a0f` and `-1a283182658245f3a28a7bf36a4d0fe2` (twin turns), `-629497bab74c4676823aebbccfb58cc0` (missing `lib/session-start.ps1`), `-a03b85ac70594a298fa9d56eca566492` (transient storage outage).
- Triage (filed 2026-10-09 after source verification; none is in scope here): TransactionGated `SessionQuery` omits `Commits.Files` (`triage-report-41d7d27c53f2464f8339fe963079a55e`); gated restore and clone do not carry PlanFile/TodoId (group `triage-group-804b21086f3019ab`; verified 2026-10-09T18:09Z by a read-only `workflow.triage.getGroup` with `workspacePath` set to the worktree `/home/sharpninja/github/McpServer/.claude/worktrees/session-20261009`, where it was filed by mistake: title "Gated restore and clone do not carry PlanFile and TodoId", 1 report (status `grouped`), group status `failed`; the main-workspace route returned not found earlier; the operator directed moving it to the main workspace, which is still pending, and its `failed` group status is not processed triage; P0.1 repeats the read-only check); revive resurrects replaced children (`triage-report-953f44d7574942419e6d5d3123acb971`); restamp skips commit files (`triage-report-d8c40d80a4ba454e8b5b96b9b5762105`); federation snapshot drops most turn fields (`triage-report-ab06ac3befed4ff99725ef6047715158`); `TodoExecutionService.cs:1474` queries `ListType == "filesModified"` but writers store `FileModified` (`triage-report-04659db176a8473bbf2aa9b6caf1f98a`). Phase B's graph-path work (S4) touches the same transaction-gated paths and must not regress them; the phase-wide Phase 4 HV brief lists these ids so the reviewer can tell pre-existing defects from new ones.
- Phase C risks: with no grading pipeline in this effort, a response is trusted only after an HV agent grades the HV request covering its turn, so trusted prior context accrues only as fast as HV requests are reviewed (the future HV pipeline removes that limit); replay determinism depends on the commit being readable (a dirty working tree is recorded as `WorkingTreeDirty` and code items hash the committed blob, never the working copy; staleness reads files at the workspace HEAD commit, so an uncommitted edit never makes a response stale); the token count is an estimate (`ceil(utf8Bytes / BytesPerToken)`), not a model tokenizer, so the budget is conservative by configuration; GraphRAG results can differ from the relational path, which is why GraphRAG only proposes candidates and trusted status always comes from the relational store; profile and rule sources can hold secrets (the marker holds the API key), so the secret filter, `SecretRedactor`, redacts mandatory items and omits optional ones, and the marker, a rule file outside version control regenerated at every server start, is provenance only and never a staleness dependency (architecture 6.2); the HostileReview grade model in the codebase uses `double` scores, so Phase C reuses Phase B's lossless decimal rules instead.
- Round-5 risk projection (recon `risk.md`), as the round-6 architecture leaves it. Resolved in the architecture, each by a named case this plan schedules: R-R1 and R-R2 (the scoped `IDispatcher` facade, the behaviors registered in both composition roots, `IAllowsGlobalWorkspace`, and the 400 answer of TR-MCP-MT-002 on every surface: `ExistingHandlersProductionPipelineBehaviorUnchanged`, `DispatchNestedSharesDbContextAndTransaction`, `DispatchParallelRequestsTwoWorkspacesNoSharedDbContext`, `WorkspaceResolutionGlobalMemoryCreateNoWorkspaceCreated`; SharedPlatform); R-R3 (turn-level isolation, `TurnWriteConcurrentDifferentTurnsSameSessionBothCommitNoConflictAnswer`; A1); R-R4 (database-only delegates and a bounded status read, `BuildOnSqliteHookWriteDuringRetrievalNotBlocked`, `StatusThousandTrustedResponsesAnswersWithinSessionStartHookBound`; AgentContextPackages); R-R5 (`DbErrorClassifier` by provider code, TR-MCP-TRIAGEERR-001 updated; SharedPlatform); R-B3 (`closed` refused, `cancelled` accepted, `TurnStatus`); R-B5 (the REPL classifier carries a server `ClassifiedError` through at every status, `retryable` included: `ReplClassifierConflictRetryableTrueKept`, `ReplClassifierServerClassifiedErrorKeepsCodeRetryableDetailsEveryStatus`); R-D1 (`MigrationsUpDownUpThreeProvidersSchemaAndDataEqual`); R-D2 (database defaults, `RollbackPriorBinaryInsertsTurnRollForwardReadsDefaults`); R-D4 (keys per workspace, `ReadableKeyAllocatorSameParentIdTwoWorkspacesSameKeyNoCollisionNoExhaustion`); R-D5 (root ids on every child row, `RootIdsEveryAgentContextChildRowEqualsItsParents`, `ReviewReasonRootIdsEqualItsTurnKey`); R-P1 and R-P7 (the analyzer branch merges first under the operator's code-freeze exception; RootNamespace plus folder, one type per file, PascalCase test names); R-P4 (cases that need a later context run at its gate, architecture 8.4); R-P5 (the surface error mapping is a Phase 2 shared file with one golden case per route); R-R6 (dispatch history unchanged: the dispatcher's last 200 request and result texts stay in memory, read only by `tests/QBrainAi.Cqrs.Tests/DispatcherTests.cs:171-210` and exposed by no route or tool, and the turn text they hold is the text the store holds, so routing the session-log units through `IDispatcher` adds no disclosure surface, while the federated read stays because the queries delegate to `ISessionLogService`; architecture 3.1 "Dispatch history"); R-R7 (`ModeGateEveryAgentContextCommandExceptTrustDecisionImplementsMarker` fails when a command other than DecideAgentResponseTrustCommand lacks `IModeGatedRequest`; architecture 3.1 "Mode gate completeness"; AgentContextServerSurfaces); R-R8 (requirement writes dispatch EvaluateStalenessCommand after their commit, outside the requirement transaction and only while Mode is not `Off`, and a failed dispatch is logged and never fails the write; architecture 3.1 "Requirement writes and staleness"; `RequirementWriteStalenessDispatchFailureLoggedWriteSucceeds`); R-D3 (the gate refuses a turn still in progress at the CompletionGate deploy until it carries the items the gate names, the S10 rollout step lists the workspace's turns in progress in its deploy receipt, the Stop remedy names each one's missing items, and nothing is backfilled; architecture 3.1 "Turns open at the CompletionGate deploy"). Still open, carried as risks: R-B1 (two error vocabularies in the published client: `ClassifiedErrorException` beside `McpClientException`, which stays the typed error the client raises), R-B2 and R-B6 (version skew between the server deploy and the one sync: a plugin older than 1.120.0 classifies a retryable 409 by output text as a hard rejection, `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1:1736-1745` at `a8d3934b`, inference from recon `risk.md`; and an older installed REPL keeps turns `pending-<nonce>`, which the explicit `InstallReplTool --update-tool` receipt before each live check and gate run mitigates), R-H1 (the diagrams must be rendered to the page standard before each HV), and R-P8 (a P0 batch rerun after a partial failure).
- Triage filed 2026-10-11 while preparing this merge: REPL `generateDocument` validator (triage-report-c03d190f194b4a76a4923ca626adbb41, fixed in P0.0a); Windows ServiceUpdateTests (triage-report-cdf116adf956449fb0a8e576bd572f26) and HostileReview startup on Windows (triage-report-f4d47e1f85b746de8ce3f5926103711f), both P0.1 baseline items; missing HostileReview migrations (triage-report-3081edb1d0414e8fb737738438cd3ccd, outside this plan).
- TypeScript-host adapter start cost: every forwarded event and tool call starts `pwsh`, as every hook on the PowerShell hosts already does; the adapters forward only the hooks the host binding table names and, on opencode's `event` hook, only an event of type `session.idle`, so streaming events start no `pwsh`, and A9's live smoke on cline-v2 and opencode records the observed per-call latency for the operator (no gate depends on it).
- Coordination risk: concurrent changes to the server session-log files listed in "Coordination with QB-AGENT-001"; mitigated by the order-independent contracts C1-C12, the `MEMORY-PROCESS-007` channel, rebasing each affected Phase 4 context branch onto current `develop` before its work starts, and re-running that context's gates after any rebase. The 2026-10-11 PowerShell-only ruling retires `plugins/core/lib-node`, on which QB-AGENT-001's governance work is built; the 2026-10-12 CONTRACT entry must be ACKed by Codex or resolved by the operator before `MAINT-LIBNODERETIRE-001` starts, while the governed-host protections are ported to PowerShell in A8 (R2-F10). The round-5 changes to C9 (the 409 `conflict` with `retryable: true`, the one `ErrorCodes` table, the REPL policy codes) reach QB-AGENT-001 through the consolidated C1-C12 text that P0.7 posts as a CONTRACT entry (architecture 3.3), whose ACK is tracked like the 2026-10-12 entry (recon `risk.md` R-P6).
- Plugin defects found by the 2026-10-09 inventory are in scope and fixed in A9 (see the matrix and host binding table), not filed separately.

## End-to-end verification

- Phase A live check (part of the Phase 4 live checks, run before the phase-wide Phase 4 HV reviewer is launched and before the VERIFY PR, on this host: the branch build deployed with the deploy procedure of the rollout step under operator approval, with its uid-0, `dotnet --list-sdks`, Nuke-outcome, and clean-worktree receipt, the branch REPL installed and selected with `./build.ps1 InstallReplTool --update-tool` from the branch head and recorded (executable path, version or commit, `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` answering), the locally synced 1.120.0 plugins installed into each host by the installed-equals-synced step with its receipt, the validator repair, and a restart; the post-merge rollout repeats deploy, REPL install, sync, and the installed-equals-synced step from the merged heads). Under the P0.2 gating rule, the Claude Code part below runs only when claude-code's P0.2 row passed (otherwise it shows `turn_logging_unsupported` with zero mints and writes, with the P0.2 receipt), and the codex assertion only when codex's row passed (a failed codex row already stopped P0): after the operator trusts the codex hooks, a fresh Codex prompt launched through the exact HV launch form (`codex exec -m gpt-6-astra -c model_reasoning_effort="max" ... --json -`, prompt on stdin) produces a hook-minted server turn (`client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` receipt); then the queue part of the codex live assertion: in an interactive Codex session on the shared app-server daemon (restarted by the refresh step), a message sent with `codex queue --thread <id> --message <text>` (its default route, which is assumed to reach the shared daemon; if it cannot reach the session, the assertion fails) while a turn runs produces exactly one hook-minted server turn of its own, with its own `turn_id` nonce, distinct from the running turn (`client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` receipt); until both parts pass, codex stays `turn_logging_unsupported`. The claude-code part runs only when its P0.2 row passed (otherwise it records `ClaudeCodeP02NotPassedTurnLoggingUnsupportedZeroMintsZeroWrites` with the P0.2 receipt and shows the operator the limitation): in a fresh Claude Code session send three prompts (two of them identical text, one queued mid-turn) and run one background agent; `client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` shows exactly three user turns with server-minted requestIds, none canceled, no `<task-notification>` turn, the queued message's turn minted at its enqueue time (turn creation within 2 seconds of the transcript `enqueue` timestamp), and every write of the work done after the queued message on the turn the D2 rule assigns; with R1 and R2 both delivered and open, an unqualified write is refused with `request_ambiguous` and the server shows zero writes from it, writes with explicit ids in the order R1, R2, R1 land on R1, R2, R1, and after R1 is closed an unqualified write lands on R2; run `/clear` and send one prompt, and confirm its writes land on the new session's turn; edit two turns concurrently and confirm exact per-turn content; create a canceled turn through the typed client (a client-supplied canceled persist), then write to it through the plugin and confirm `persisted:false code: skipped_terminal` with exit 1 and zero server actions; separately, for a turn the plugin already knows is terminal, confirm the plugin refuses the write with `turn_immutable` without a server call; confirm the stop gate blocks until every open turn is closed by the model.
- Phase B live check (part of the Phase 4 live checks, before the phase-wide Phase 4 HV and the VERIFY PR, on this host against a pre-merge deploy of the branch build through the deploy procedure of the rollout step under operator approval, with its uid-0, `dotnet --list-sdks`, Nuke-outcome, and clean-worktree receipt, the branch REPL installed and selected with `./build.ps1 InstallReplTool --update-tool` from the branch head and recorded (executable path, version or commit, `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` and `workflow.sessionlog.recordReview` answering), after the one Phase 4 sync at 1.120.0 (installed into each host by the installed-equals-synced step) and the CompletionGate enforcement deploy; the post-merge rollout repeats it from the merged heads): new turn shows `agentReview.status=Unstarted`, `hostileReview.status=Unstarted`; set `requestClass: Chore` -> hostile becomes `Unnecessary`; record agent review 0.98/0.98 -> derived `Pass` with stamped thresholds; 0.9799999999999999999999999999 -> `Fail`; supplied `Pass` with 0.97 -> 422 `review_status_conflict`; `otherNotes` with `Fail` -> 400 `review_other_notes_not_allowed`; `Other` without `otherNotes` -> 400 `review_other_notes_required`; complete a `Code` turn with hostile `Unstarted` -> `session_turn_completion_gate`; record hostile review from an HV receipt (with jsonl paths) -> completes; read back 28-digit scores byte-equal through REST, MCP, REPL, and plugin.
- Per-plugin: every plugin's own suite green (Pester in all nine repos through `./build.ps1 TestAgentPlugins`). Live smoke on each host that is installed on this machine (Claude Code here; Codex, Grok, Cline v2 via the fork at `/home/sharpninja/github/cline`, OpenCode, Copilot, Cowork where available), split by the host's recorded feature-matrix disposition (D6, D7): a supported or adapted host shows that one prompt yields one turn, a background event yields none, `recordReview` round-trips a 28-digit score, and the completion gate message surfaces; a host recorded as unsupported for turn logging shows that every session-log verb reports `turn_logging_unsupported`, and `client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` shows zero mints and zero writes from it, with its feature-matrix row as the receipt. Cowork, when installed, also runs the multi-form and FIFO bridge assertion (one mint per message for each prompt form, `dequeue` delivering the head); on failed or unavailable probes it shows `turn_logging_unsupported` with zero mints and writes. Hosts not installed are covered by their test suites and `PluginSessionLogIntegration`, and the receipt says so explicitly.
- Agent-context live check (architecture 8.6; part of the phase-wide Phase 4 HV, before the VERIFY PR, on LAB-OMARCHY and on PAYTON-LEGION2 through the WSMan route, after the deploy and the plugin sync, each on a separate server instance started for the check with its own `--instance`, Mode `Pilot`, and explicit `ProfileSources` and `RuleSources`, never on a shared server, so no other live agent session is subject to prompt refusal and the profile never resolves in a service account's home): a delivered request builds one package that `client.AgentContext.QueryPackagesAsync` returns with the request's root ids (on claude-code and claude-cowork the prompt hook takes the bridge-minted `requestId` after applying pending transcript records); replay reports `identical: true`; an over-budget fixture prompt is refused with `agent_context_budget_exceeded` and its token counts shown to the operator, no answer is produced, the request's turn is closed `canceled` with that reason and leaves the Stop ledger once the server confirms the close, an `Overflow` package is stored, and a retried build with the same idempotency key returns the same 422 with the stored diagnostics; the model submits its response with the `agent_context_submit_response` tool before closing the turn, and the response passes the deterministic grade; an HV request covering its turn is created, the HV agent records a passing model grade with verified receipts in the grade-only continuation of its reviewer thread, and the response becomes `Trusted`; a model grade from another grader or with mismatched receipts is refused; a later package includes it as a prior response; committing a change to a file it depends on makes it `Stale` and the next package omits it with reason `stale`, while an uncommitted edit does not, because staleness reads files at the workspace HEAD commit; with GraphRAG disabled, retrieval reports `graphrag_disabled` and returns the relational candidates; with GraphRAG enabled, the trusted response is projected and a rejected one removed; the pilot report lists these requests by `invocationMode`, all `Stateful` under `Pilot` (a pilot run in `Pilot` yields no `Stateless` row), and counts the `Overflow` package and the refused prompt; on each host whose P0.2 row records history exclusion, the sentinel prior turn is absent from the message list the host passes to its model call in `Stateless` (`StatelessHostSentinelPriorTurnAbsentFromModelRequest`, repeated live, architecture 6.6); switching the instance to Mode `Off` while a host session runs makes its next build answer 409 `agent_context_disabled`, after which the host session continues statefully with no refusal and no `canceled` close, and the read-only queries, the pilot report among them, still answer.
- Traceability: G5 phase-exit rule (strict TR/TEST findings contain no catalog id and nothing outside the P0 baseline; `client.UseCases.GetCoverageAsync` lists no catalog FR without a Realizes UC, operator D3).
